
===== FILE: src/components/admin/contact-message-actions.tsx =====
'use client'

import { useState } from 'react'
import { useRouter } from 'next/navigation'
import { Check, Loader2, RotateCcw } from 'lucide-react'
import { Button } from '@/components/ui/button'

export function ContactMessageActions({ id, handled }: { id: string; handled: boolean }) {
  const router = useRouter()
  const [busy, setBusy] = useState(false)
  const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null)

  async function toggleHandled() {
    if (busy) return

    setBusy(true)
    setError(null)

    try {
      const response = await fetch(`/api/admin/messages/${encodeURIComponent(id)}`, {
        method: 'PATCH',
        headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
        body: JSON.stringify({ handled: !handled }),
      })
      const payload = await response.json().catch(() => null)

      if (!response.ok) {
        throw new Error(payload?.error?.message ?? 'Could not update this message.')
      }

      router.refresh()
    } catch (err) {
      setError(err instanceof Error ? err.message : 'Could not update this message.')
    } finally {
      setBusy(false)
    }
  }

  return (
    <div className="flex flex-col items-end gap-1">
      <Button
        type="button"
        size="sm"
        variant={handled ? 'outline' : 'default'}
        disabled={busy}
        onClick={toggleHandled}
        className="gap-2"
      >
        {busy ? (
          <Loader2 className="h-3.5 w-3.5 animate-spin" aria-hidden="true" />
        ) : handled ? (
          <RotateCcw className="h-3.5 w-3.5" aria-hidden="true" />
        ) : (
          <Check className="h-3.5 w-3.5" aria-hidden="true" />
        )}
        {handled ? 'Mark unhandled' : 'Mark handled'}
      </Button>
      {error && (
        <span className="max-w-48 text-right text-xs text-destructive" role="alert">
          {error}
        </span>
      )}
    </div>
  )
}


===== FILE: src/components/admin/newsletter-admin-form.tsx =====
'use client'

import { useState } from 'react'
import { useRouter } from 'next/navigation'
import { CheckCircle2, Loader2, Mail, Send, TriangleAlert } from 'lucide-react'
import { Button } from '@/components/ui/button'
import { Input } from '@/components/ui/input'
import { Label } from '@/components/ui/label'
import { Textarea } from '@/components/ui/textarea'

export function NewsletterAdminForm({
  adminEmail,
  confirmedCount,
}: {
  adminEmail: string
  confirmedCount: number
}) {
  const router = useRouter()
  const [subject, setSubject] = useState('')
  const [body, setBody] = useState('')
  const [confirmText, setConfirmText] = useState('')
  const [sending, setSending] = useState<'test' | 'broadcast' | null>(null)
  const [result, setResult] = useState<{
    tone: 'success' | 'warning' | 'error'
    message: string
  } | null>(null)

  async function submit(test: boolean) {
    if (sending) return
    setResult(null)

    if (!test && confirmText !== 'SEND') {
      setResult({ tone: 'error', message: 'Type SEND in the confirmation box first.' })
      return
    }

    setSending(test ? 'test' : 'broadcast')
    try {
      const response = await fetch('/api/admin/newsletter', {
        method: 'POST',
        headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
        body: JSON.stringify({
          subject,
          body,
          test,
          ...(test ? {} : { confirm: confirmText }),
        }),
      })
      const payload = await response.json().catch(() => null)
      if (!response.ok) {
        throw new Error(payload?.error?.message ?? 'The send failed. Check the server logs.')
      }

      const data = payload?.data
      if (test) {
        setResult({ tone: 'success', message: `Test email sent to ${adminEmail}.` })
        router.refresh()
      } else {
        const sent = data?.sent ?? 0
        const failed = data?.failed ?? 0
        setResult({
          tone: failed > 0 ? 'warning' : 'success',
          message:
            failed > 0
              ? `Sent to ${sent} subscribers; ${failed} failed. Check the email server logs before retrying.`
              : `Sent to ${sent} confirmed subscribers.`,
        })
        setConfirmText('')
        router.refresh()
      }
    } catch (error) {
      setResult({
        tone: 'error',
        message: error instanceof Error ? error.message : 'The send failed. Try again.',
      })
    } finally {
      setSending(null)
    }
  }

  return (
    <div className="space-y-6">
      <div className="grid grid-cols-1 gap-4 sm:grid-cols-[1fr_auto] sm:items-end">
        <div className="space-y-1.5">
          <Label htmlFor="newsletter-subject">Subject</Label>
          <Input
            id="newsletter-subject"
            value={subject}
            onChange={(event) => setSubject(event.target.value)}
            placeholder="New module: authentication from scratch"
            maxLength={140}
          />
          <p className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">{subject.length} / 140</p>
        </div>
        <div className="rounded-lg border border-border/60 bg-muted/20 px-3 py-2 text-xs text-muted-foreground">
          {confirmedCount} confirmed{' '}
          {confirmedCount === 1 ? 'subscriber' : 'subscribers'}
        </div>
      </div>

      <div className="space-y-1.5">
        <Label htmlFor="newsletter-body">Message</Label>
        <Textarea
          id="newsletter-body"
          value={body}
          onChange={(event) => setBody(event.target.value)}
          placeholder={'A short note about what changed and why it matters.\n\nPlain text only. Line breaks are preserved.'}
          rows={12}
          maxLength={20_000}
        />
        <p className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">{body.length} / 20,000 characters</p>
      </div>

      {(subject.trim() || body.trim()) && (
        <div className="rounded-xl border border-border/60 bg-background p-4">
          <p className="mb-2 text-xs font-medium uppercase tracking-wide text-muted-foreground">
            Preview
          </p>
          <div className="rounded-lg border border-border/60 bg-muted/20 p-4">
            <p className="font-semibold">{subject.trim() || 'Your subject'}</p>
            <p className="mt-3 whitespace-pre-wrap text-sm leading-relaxed text-muted-foreground">
              {body.trim() || 'Your message'}
            </p>
            <p className="mt-4 border-t border-border/60 pt-3 text-xs text-muted-foreground">
              Every email includes an unsubscribe link. Unsubscribing deletes the address.
            </p>
          </div>
        </div>
      )}

      {result && (
        <p
          className={
            result.tone === 'success'
              ? 'flex items-start gap-2 rounded-lg border border-emerald-500/30 bg-emerald-500/10 px-3 py-2 text-sm text-emerald-700 dark:text-emerald-300'
              : result.tone === 'warning'
                ? 'flex items-start gap-2 rounded-lg border border-amber-500/30 bg-amber-500/10 px-3 py-2 text-sm text-amber-700 dark:text-amber-300'
                : 'flex items-start gap-2 rounded-lg border border-destructive/30 bg-destructive/5 px-3 py-2 text-sm text-destructive'
          }
          role={result.tone === 'error' ? 'alert' : 'status'}
        >
          {result.tone === 'success' ? (
            <CheckCircle2 className="mt-0.5 h-4 w-4 flex-shrink-0" aria-hidden="true" />
          ) : (
            <TriangleAlert className="mt-0.5 h-4 w-4 flex-shrink-0" aria-hidden="true" />
          )}
          <span>{result.message}</span>
        </p>
      )}

      <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-3 border-t border-border/60 pt-5">
        <Button
          type="button"
          variant="outline"
          onClick={() => submit(true)}
          disabled={Boolean(sending) || !subject.trim() || !body.trim()}
          className="gap-2"
        >
          {sending === 'test' ? (
            <Loader2 className="h-4 w-4 animate-spin" aria-hidden="true" />
          ) : (
            <Mail className="h-4 w-4" aria-hidden="true" />
          )}
          Send test to me
        </Button>
        <p className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">
          Test sends are prefixed with [TEST] and use a placeholder unsubscribe link.
        </p>
      </div>

      <div className="rounded-xl border border-destructive/30 bg-destructive/5 p-4">
        <h2 className="text-sm font-semibold">Send to the real list</h2>
        <p className="mt-1 text-xs leading-relaxed text-muted-foreground">
          This sends immediately to {confirmedCount} confirmed{' '}
          {confirmedCount === 1 ? 'address' : 'addresses'}. There is no undo and no scheduling.
          Type <strong className="text-foreground">SEND</strong> to unlock the button.
        </p>
        <div className="mt-3 flex flex-col gap-3 sm:flex-row sm:items-center">
          <Input
            aria-label="Type SEND to confirm"
            value={confirmText}
            onChange={(event) => setConfirmText(event.target.value)}
            placeholder="Type SEND"
            className="sm:max-w-44"
            autoComplete="off"
          />
          <Button
            type="button"
            variant="destructive"
            onClick={() => submit(false)}
            disabled={
              Boolean(sending) ||
              confirmText !== 'SEND' ||
              confirmedCount === 0 ||
              !subject.trim() ||
              !body.trim()
            }
            className="gap-2"
          >
            {sending === 'broadcast' ? (
              <Loader2 className="h-4 w-4 animate-spin" aria-hidden="true" />
            ) : (
              <Send className="h-4 w-4" aria-hidden="true" />
            )}
            Send to {confirmedCount} confirmed
          </Button>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  )
}


===== FILE: src/components/admin/reports-client.tsx =====
'use client'

import { useCallback, useEffect, useState } from 'react'
import Link from 'next/link'
import {
  Check,
  ExternalLink,
  Flag,
  Loader2,
  RefreshCw,
  ShieldCheck,
  Trash2,
  X,
} from 'lucide-react'
import { Badge } from '@/components/ui/badge'
import { Button } from '@/components/ui/button'
import { Card, CardContent } from '@/components/ui/card'
import { ConfirmDialog } from '@/components/ui/confirm-dialog'
import { timeAgo } from '@/lib/time'

type ReportStatus = 'open' | 'resolved' | 'dismissed' | 'all'
type ReportAction = 'resolve' | 'dismiss' | 'delete'

interface ReportItem {
  id: string
  targetType: 'project' | 'comment' | 'profile'
  targetId: string
  reason: string
  details: string | null
  status: string
  resolutionNote: string | null
  createdAt: string
  reviewedAt: string | null
  reviewedBy: { id: string; name: string | null } | null
  reporter: { id: string; name: string | null; image: string | null }
  target: {
    title: string
    preview: string
    href: string | null
    authorName: string | null
    available: boolean
  }
}

const reasonLabels: Record<string, string> = {
  spam: 'Spam or advertising',
  harassment: 'Harassment or hate',
  inappropriate: 'Inappropriate content',
  copyright: 'Copyright violation',
  other: 'Something else',
}

const filters: { value: ReportStatus; label: string }[] = [
  { value: 'open', label: 'Open' },
  { value: 'resolved', label: 'Resolved' },
  { value: 'dismissed', label: 'Dismissed' },
  { value: 'all', label: 'All' },
]

export function AdminReportsClient() {
  const [status, setStatus] = useState<ReportStatus>('open')
  const [reports, setReports] = useState<ReportItem[]>([])
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true)
  const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null)
  const [busyId, setBusyId] = useState<string | null>(null)
  const [deleteTarget, setDeleteTarget] = useState<ReportItem | null>(null)

  const load = useCallback(async () => {
    setLoading(true)
    setError(null)
    try {
      const response = await fetch(`/api/admin/reports?status=${status}`, {
        cache: 'no-store',
      })
      const payload = await response.json().catch(() => null)
      if (!response.ok) {
        throw new Error(payload?.error?.message ?? 'Could not load reports.')
      }
      setReports(payload.data as ReportItem[])
    } catch (err) {
      setError(err instanceof Error ? err.message : 'Could not load reports.')
    } finally {
      setLoading(false)
    }
  }, [status])

  useEffect(() => {
    const timer = window.setTimeout(() => void load(), 0)
    return () => window.clearTimeout(timer)
  }, [load])

  async function act(report: ReportItem, action: ReportAction) {
    if (busyId) return
    setBusyId(report.id)
    setError(null)

    try {
      const response = await fetch(`/api/admin/reports/${report.id}`, {
        method: 'PATCH',
        headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
        body: JSON.stringify({
          action,
          note:
            action === 'delete'
              ? `Removed by moderator (reported as ${reasonLabels[report.reason] ?? report.reason}).`
              : undefined,
        }),
      })
      const payload = await response.json().catch(() => null)
      if (!response.ok) {
        throw new Error(payload?.error?.message ?? 'Could not update this report.')
      }

      setDeleteTarget(null)
      await load()
    } catch (err) {
      setError(err instanceof Error ? err.message : 'Could not update this report.')
    } finally {
      setBusyId(null)
    }
  }

  const openCount = reports.filter((report) => report.status === 'open').length

  return (
    <div className="space-y-8">
      <div className="space-y-3">
        <Badge variant="outline" className="gap-1.5">
          <ShieldCheck className="h-3.5 w-3.5" aria-hidden="true" />
          Maintainer only
        </Badge>
        <h1 className="text-3xl font-bold tracking-tight sm:text-4xl">Moderation queue</h1>
        <p className="max-w-2xl text-muted-foreground">
          Review reports on projects, comments, and profiles. Deleting content also closes
          every open report attached to it and writes an audit-log entry.
        </p>
      </div>

      <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-3">
        <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-2">
          {filters.map((filter) => (
            <Button
              key={filter.value}
              type="button"
              size="sm"
              variant={status === filter.value ? 'default' : 'outline'}
              onClick={() => setStatus(filter.value)}
            >
              {filter.label}
              {filter.value === 'open' && openCount > 0 ? ` (${openCount})` : ''}
            </Button>
          ))}
        </div>
        <Button
          type="button"
          variant="ghost"
          size="sm"
          className="gap-2"
          onClick={() => void load()}
          disabled={loading}
        >
          <RefreshCw className={`h-4 w-4 ${loading ? 'animate-spin' : ''}`} aria-hidden="true" />
          Refresh
        </Button>
      </div>

      {error && (
        <p role="alert" className="rounded-xl border border-destructive/30 bg-destructive/10 px-4 py-3 text-sm text-destructive">
          {error}
        </p>
      )}

      {loading && reports.length === 0 ? (
        <Card>
          <CardContent className="flex items-center justify-center gap-2 py-14 text-sm text-muted-foreground">
            <Loader2 className="h-4 w-4 animate-spin" aria-hidden="true" />
            Loading reports...
          </CardContent>
        </Card>
      ) : reports.length === 0 ? (
        <Card>
          <CardContent className="flex flex-col items-center gap-3 py-14 text-center">
            <Flag className="h-10 w-10 text-muted-foreground/50" aria-hidden="true" />
            <div>
              <p className="font-medium">Nothing in this queue</p>
              <p className="mt-1 text-sm text-muted-foreground">
                New reports will appear here automatically.
              </p>
            </div>
          </CardContent>
        </Card>
      ) : (
        <div className="space-y-3">
          {reports.map((report) => (
            <Card key={report.id}>
              <CardContent className="space-y-4 p-4 sm:p-5">
                <div className="flex flex-wrap items-start justify-between gap-3">
                  <div className="min-w-0">
                    <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2">
                      <Badge variant="secondary" className="capitalize">
                        {report.targetType}
                      </Badge>
                      <Badge
                        variant={report.status === 'open' ? 'destructive' : 'outline'}
                        className="capitalize"
                      >
                        {report.status}
                      </Badge>
                      <span className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">
                        {timeAgo(report.createdAt)}
                      </span>
                    </div>
                    <p className="mt-2 text-sm font-medium">
                      {reasonLabels[report.reason] ?? report.reason}
                    </p>
                    <p className="mt-1 text-xs text-muted-foreground">
                      Reported by {report.reporter.name?.trim() || 'a learner'}
                      {report.target.authorName
                        ? ` · Content by ${report.target.authorName}`
                        : ''}
                    </p>
                  </div>
                  {report.target.href && report.target.available && (
                    <Button asChild variant="outline" size="sm" className="gap-1.5">
                      <Link href={report.target.href} target="_blank" rel="noreferrer">
                        Open content
                        <ExternalLink className="h-3.5 w-3.5" aria-hidden="true" />
                      </Link>
                    </Button>
                  )}
                </div>

                <div className="rounded-lg border border-border/50 bg-muted/20 p-3">
                  <p className="text-sm font-medium">
                    {report.target.available ? report.target.title : 'Deleted content'}
                  </p>
                  {report.target.preview && (
                    <p className="mt-1 line-clamp-3 text-sm text-muted-foreground">
                      {report.target.preview}
                    </p>
                  )}
                </div>

                {report.details && (
                  <div>
                    <p className="text-xs font-medium uppercase tracking-wide text-muted-foreground">
                      Reporter details
                    </p>
                    <p className="mt-1 whitespace-pre-wrap text-sm">{report.details}</p>
                  </div>
                )}

                {report.resolutionNote && (
                  <p className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">
                    Resolution: {report.resolutionNote}
                  </p>
                )}

                {report.status === 'open' && (
                  <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-2">
                    <Button
                      type="button"
                      size="sm"
                      className="gap-1.5"
                      disabled={busyId === report.id}
                      onClick={() => void act(report, 'resolve')}
                    >
                      {busyId === report.id ? (
                        <Loader2 className="h-3.5 w-3.5 animate-spin" aria-hidden="true" />
                      ) : (
                        <Check className="h-3.5 w-3.5" aria-hidden="true" />
                      )}
                      Resolve
                    </Button>
                    <Button
                      type="button"
                      size="sm"
                      variant="outline"
                      className="gap-1.5"
                      disabled={busyId === report.id}
                      onClick={() => void act(report, 'dismiss')}
                    >
                      <X className="h-3.5 w-3.5" aria-hidden="true" />
                      Dismiss
                    </Button>
                    {report.targetType !== 'profile' && report.target.available && (
                      <Button
                        type="button"
                        size="sm"
                        variant="destructive"
                        className="gap-1.5"
                        disabled={busyId === report.id}
                        onClick={() => setDeleteTarget(report)}
                      >
                        <Trash2 className="h-3.5 w-3.5" aria-hidden="true" />
                        Delete content
                      </Button>
                    )}
                  </div>
                )}
              </CardContent>
            </Card>
          ))}
        </div>
      )}

      <ConfirmDialog
        open={deleteTarget !== null}
        onOpenChange={(open) => {
          if (!open) setDeleteTarget(null)
        }}
        title="Delete reported content?"
        description={
          deleteTarget
            ? `This permanently deletes the ${deleteTarget.targetType} and closes all open reports for it. This cannot be undone.`
            : ''
        }
        confirmLabel="Delete permanently"
        destructive
        pending={busyId !== null}
        onConfirm={() => {
          if (deleteTarget) return act(deleteTarget, 'delete')
        }}
      />
    </div>
  )
}


===== FILE: src/components/badge-earned-toast.tsx =====
'use client'

import { useCallback, useEffect, useRef, useState } from 'react'
import { useSession } from 'next-auth/react'
import { Trophy, X } from 'lucide-react'
import { useProgress } from '@/hooks/use-progress'
import { useCommunityStats } from '@/hooks/use-community-stats'
import { badgeDefinitions, getEarnedBadges, type BadgeDefinition } from '@/lib/badges'
import {
  tierColors,
  tierIcons,
  tierLabels,
} from '@/lib/badge-meta'
import { Card, CardContent } from '@/components/ui/card'
import { cn } from '@/lib/utils'

const TOAST_DURATION_MS = 3000
const STORAGE_PREFIX = 'nyxora-seen-badges'

interface SeenBadges {
  scope: string
  keys: Set<string>
}

export function BadgeEarnedToast() {
  const { state, isLoaded } = useProgress()
  const { data: session } = useSession()
  const { stats: community, isLoaded: communityLoaded } = useCommunityStats()
  const [queue, setQueue] = useState<BadgeDefinition[]>([])
  const seenRef = useRef<SeenBadges | null>(null)

  const scope = session?.user?.id ?? 'guest'
  const storageKey = `${STORAGE_PREFIX}:${scope}`

  const markNotified = useCallback(
    (keys: string[]) => {
      if (!session?.user?.id || keys.length === 0) return
      void fetch('/api/badges/pending', {
        method: 'POST',
        headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
        body: JSON.stringify({ keys: Array.from(new Set(keys)) }),
      }).catch(() => {
        // A missed mark only means the toast may appear again on another device.
      })
    },
    [session?.user?.id]
  )

  // Cross-device unlocks: persisted badges with no notifiedAt are shown once,
  // then marked so a second browser does not repeat the celebration.
  useEffect(() => {
    if (!session?.user?.id) return

    let cancelled = false
    fetch('/api/badges/pending', { cache: 'no-store' })
      .then((response) => (response.ok ? response.json() : null))
      .then((payload) => {
        if (cancelled) return
        const keys = (payload?.data?.keys ?? []) as string[]
        const badges = badgeDefinitions.filter((badge) => keys.includes(badge.key))
        if (badges.length === 0) return

        setQueue((existing) => {
          const queued = new Set(existing.map((badge) => badge.key))
          return [...existing, ...badges.filter((badge) => !queued.has(badge.key))]
        })
        markNotified(badges.map((badge) => badge.key))
      })
      .catch(() => {
        // Offline or signed out: the local toast path still works.
      })

    return () => {
      cancelled = true
    }
  }, [session?.user?.id, markNotified])

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!isLoaded || !communityLoaded) return

    const earned = getEarnedBadges({ state, ...community })
    const earnedKeys = new Set(earned.map((badge) => badge.key))

    let seen = seenRef.current
    if (!seen || seen.scope !== scope) {
      let raw: string | null = null
      try {
        raw = window.localStorage.getItem(storageKey)
      } catch {
        // Storage can be unavailable in privacy modes.
      }

      let keys: Set<string>
      if (raw === null) {
        // First visit with this scope: establish a silent baseline so existing
        // badges do not all pop up at once.
        keys = new Set(earnedKeys)
        try {
          window.localStorage.setItem(storageKey, JSON.stringify([...keys]))
        } catch {
          // Ignore storage failures.
        }
      } else {
        try {
          keys = new Set(JSON.parse(raw) as string[])
        } catch {
          keys = new Set(earnedKeys)
        }
      }

      seen = { scope, keys }
      seenRef.current = seen
      if (raw === null) return
    }

    const newBadges = earned.filter((badge) => !seen.keys.has(badge.key))
    let changed = false

    // A reset can remove badges; drop them so earning them again pops again.
    for (const key of Array.from(seen.keys)) {
      if (!earnedKeys.has(key)) {
        seen.keys.delete(key)
        changed = true
      }
    }

    if (newBadges.length > 0) {
      for (const badge of newBadges) seen.keys.add(badge.key)
      changed = true

      setQueue((existing) => {
        const queued = new Set(existing.map((badge) => badge.key))
        return [...existing, ...newBadges.filter((badge) => !queued.has(badge.key))]
      })
      markNotified(newBadges.map((badge) => badge.key))
    }

    if (changed) {
      try {
        window.localStorage.setItem(storageKey, JSON.stringify([...seen.keys]))
      } catch {
        // Ignore storage failures.
      }
    }
  }, [isLoaded, communityLoaded, state, community, scope, storageKey, markNotified])

  const current = queue[0]

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!current) return

    const timer = window.setTimeout(() => {
      setQueue((existing) => existing.slice(1))
    }, TOAST_DURATION_MS)

    return () => window.clearTimeout(timer)
  }, [current])

  if (!current) return null

  const [borderColor, bgColor, textColor] = tierColors[current.tier].split(' ')

  return (
    <div
      key={current.key}
      role="status"
      aria-live="polite"
      className="badge-toast-enter fixed bottom-4 right-4 z-[95] w-[calc(100vw-2rem)] max-w-sm"
    >
      <Card className={cn('relative overflow-hidden border-2 shadow-2xl', borderColor, bgColor)}>
        <CardContent className="flex items-start gap-3 p-4">
          <div className="text-3xl leading-none" aria-hidden="true">{current.icon}</div>
          <div className="min-w-0 flex-1">
            <p className="flex items-center gap-1.5 text-[10px] font-semibold uppercase tracking-wider text-emerald-600">
              <Trophy className="h-3 w-3" aria-hidden="true" />
              Badge unlocked
            </p>
            <p className="mt-0.5 font-semibold leading-tight">{current.name}</p>
            <p className="mt-0.5 text-xs text-muted-foreground">{current.description}</p>
            <p className={cn('mt-1 text-[10px] font-medium', textColor)}>
              {tierIcons[current.tier]} {tierLabels[current.tier]}
            </p>
          </div>
          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={() => setQueue((existing) => existing.slice(1))}
            className="rounded-md p-1 text-muted-foreground transition-colors hover:bg-muted hover:text-foreground"
            aria-label="Dismiss badge notification"
          >
            <X className="h-4 w-4" aria-hidden="true" />
          </button>
        </CardContent>
        <div className="badge-toast-progress absolute bottom-0 left-0 h-0.5 w-full bg-emerald-500" aria-hidden="true" />
      </Card>
    </div>
  )
}


===== FILE: src/components/badge-grid.tsx =====
'use client'

import { CheckCircle2, Lock } from 'lucide-react'
import { Card, CardContent } from '@/components/ui/card'
import {
  categoryIcons,
  tierColors,
  tierIcons,
  tierLabels,
  type BadgeDefinition,
} from '@/lib/badge-meta'
import { cn } from '@/lib/utils'

interface BadgeGridProps {
  badges: BadgeDefinition[]
  earnedKeys: Set<string>
  className?: string
}

export function BadgeGrid({ badges, earnedKeys, className }: BadgeGridProps) {
  return (
    <div
      className={cn(
        'grid gap-3 grid-cols-2 sm:grid-cols-3 lg:grid-cols-4 xl:grid-cols-6 2xl:grid-cols-7',
        className
      )}
    >
      {badges.map((badge) => {
        const earned = earnedKeys.has(badge.key)
        const [borderColor, bgColor, textColor] = tierColors[badge.tier].split(' ')

        return (
          <Card
            key={badge.key}
            className={cn(
              'relative text-center transition-all duration-300',
              earned
                ? `border-2 ${borderColor} ${bgColor} hover:shadow-lg`
                : 'opacity-40 grayscale border-dashed border-border/30'
            )}
          >
            <div className="absolute top-2 right-2 flex items-center gap-1">
              <span className="text-xs" aria-hidden="true">{tierIcons[badge.tier]}</span>
              <span className={`text-[10px] font-medium ${textColor}`}>{tierLabels[badge.tier]}</span>
              <span className="text-xs" aria-hidden="true">{categoryIcons[badge.category]}</span>
            </div>
            <CardContent className="pt-5 pb-4">
              <div className="text-4xl mb-2" aria-hidden="true">{badge.icon}</div>
              <p className="font-semibold text-sm">{badge.name}</p>
              <p className="text-xs text-muted-foreground mt-1 line-clamp-2">{badge.description}</p>
              {earned ? (
                <div className="mt-3 flex items-center justify-center gap-1">
                  <span className="inline-flex items-center gap-1 rounded-full bg-emerald-500/15 text-emerald-600 border border-emerald-500/20 px-2 py-0.5 text-[10px] font-medium">
                    <CheckCircle2 className="h-3 w-3" aria-hidden="true" />
                    <span>Earned</span>
                  </span>
                </div>
              ) : badge.tier !== 'bronze' ? (
                <div className="mt-2 text-center">
                  <span className="inline-flex items-center gap-1 px-2 py-1 rounded-full bg-muted/50 text-muted-foreground text-[10px] font-medium">
                    <Lock className="h-3 w-3" aria-hidden="true" />
                    <span>Locked</span>
                  </span>
                </div>
              ) : null}
            </CardContent>
          </Card>
        )
      })}
    </div>
  )
}


===== FILE: src/components/badges-client.tsx =====
'use client'

import Link from 'next/link'
import { ArrowLeft, CheckCircle2, Trophy } from 'lucide-react'
import { useProgress } from '@/hooks/use-progress'
import { useCommunityStats } from '@/hooks/use-community-stats'
import {
  badgeDefinitions,
  getEarnedBadges,
  sortBadgesByEarned,
} from '@/lib/badges'
import { BadgeGrid } from '@/components/badge-grid'
import { Button } from '@/components/ui/button'
import { Card, CardContent } from '@/components/ui/card'
import { Progress } from '@/components/ui/progress'

export function BadgesClient() {
  const { state, isLoaded, xp, streak } = useProgress()
  const { stats: community } = useCommunityStats()

  if (!isLoaded) {
    return (
      <div className="space-y-6">
        <div className="h-28 animate-pulse rounded-xl border border-border/50 bg-muted/40" />
        <div className="grid grid-cols-1 gap-3 grid-cols-2 sm:grid-cols-3 lg:grid-cols-4 xl:grid-cols-6 2xl:grid-cols-7">
          {Array.from({ length: 18 }).map((_, index) => (
            <div key={index} className="h-44 animate-pulse rounded-xl border border-border/50 bg-muted/40" />
          ))}
        </div>
      </div>
    )
  }

  const context = { state, ...community }
  const earnedBadges = getEarnedBadges(context)
  const earnedKeys = new Set(earnedBadges.map((badge) => badge.key))
  const sortedBadges = sortBadgesByEarned(badgeDefinitions, earnedKeys)
  const percentage = Math.round((earnedBadges.length / badgeDefinitions.length) * 100)

  return (
    <div className="space-y-8">
      <Card>
        <CardContent className="p-5 sm:p-6">
          <div className="flex flex-col gap-5 sm:flex-row sm:items-center sm:justify-between">
            <div>
              <p className="text-sm text-muted-foreground">Badge collection</p>
              <p className="mt-1 text-3xl font-bold">
                {earnedBadges.length}
                <span className="text-lg font-normal text-muted-foreground"> / {badgeDefinitions.length}</span>
              </p>
              <p className="mt-1 text-xs text-muted-foreground">{percentage}% unlocked</p>
            </div>
            <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-5 text-sm">
              <span className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 text-muted-foreground">
                <Trophy className="h-4 w-4 text-violet-500" aria-hidden="true" />
                <strong className="text-foreground">{earnedBadges.length}</strong> earned
              </span>
              <span className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 text-muted-foreground">
                <CheckCircle2 className="h-4 w-4 text-emerald-500" aria-hidden="true" />
                <strong className="text-foreground">{xp.toLocaleString()}</strong> XP
              </span>
              <span className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 text-muted-foreground">
                🔥 <strong className="text-foreground">{streak.longest}</strong> day streak
              </span>
            </div>
            <Button asChild variant="outline" size="sm" className="gap-2 sm:shrink-0">
              <Link href="/dashboard">
                <ArrowLeft className="h-4 w-4" aria-hidden="true" />
                Dashboard
              </Link>
            </Button>
          </div>
          <Progress value={percentage} className="mt-5 h-2" />
        </CardContent>
      </Card>

      <BadgeGrid badges={sortedBadges} earnedKeys={earnedKeys} />
    </div>
  )
}

===== FILE: src/components/certificates/print-button.tsx =====
'use client'

import { useEffect } from 'react'
import { Printer } from 'lucide-react'
import { Button } from '@/components/ui/button'

export function PrintButton({ auto = false }: { auto?: boolean }) {
  useEffect(() => {
    if (!auto) return
    const timer = window.setTimeout(() => window.print(), 350)
    return () => window.clearTimeout(timer)
  }, [auto])

  return (
    <Button type="button" variant="outline" className="gap-2" onClick={() => window.print()}>
      <Printer className="h-4 w-4" aria-hidden="true" />
      Print certificate
    </Button>
  )
}


===== FILE: src/components/community/comment-thread.tsx =====
'use client'


import { useEffect, useState } from 'react'
import { useRouter } from 'next/navigation'
import { useSession } from 'next-auth/react'
import { CornerDownRight, Loader2, MessageSquare, Send, Trash2 } from 'lucide-react'
import { Button } from '@/components/ui/button'
import { Badge } from '@/components/ui/badge'
import { ConfirmDialog } from '@/components/ui/confirm-dialog'
import { Textarea } from '@/components/ui/textarea'
import { UserChip } from '@/components/community/user-chip'
import { ReportButton } from '@/components/community/report-button'
import type { CommentItem } from '@/lib/community-types'
import { timeAgo } from '@/lib/time'

interface CommentThreadProps {
  targetType: 'lesson' | 'project'
  targetId: string
  title?: string
  description?: string
}

export function CommentThread({
  targetType,
  targetId,
  title = 'Discussion',
  description = 'Questions, corrections, and things that clicked — this is where the lesson keeps going.',
}: CommentThreadProps) {
  const { data: session, status } = useSession()
  const router = useRouter()

  const [comments, setComments] = useState<CommentItem[]>([])
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true)
  const [loadError, setLoadError] = useState<string | null>(null)
  const [reloadKey, setReloadKey] = useState(0)

  const [body, setBody] = useState('')
  const [submitting, setSubmitting] = useState(false)
  const [formError, setFormError] = useState<string | null>(null)

  const [replyTo, setReplyTo] = useState<string | null>(null)
  const [replyBody, setReplyBody] = useState('')
  const [replySubmitting, setReplySubmitting] = useState(false)

  const [deleteTarget, setDeleteTarget] = useState<{ id: string; kind: 'comment' | 'reply' } | null>(null)
  const [deletePending, setDeletePending] = useState(false)

  const total = comments.reduce((sum, comment) => sum + 1 + comment.replies.length, 0)

  useEffect(() => {
    const controller = new AbortController()

    async function load() {
      setLoading(true)
      setLoadError(null)
      try {
        const params = new URLSearchParams({ targetType, targetId })
        const response = await fetch(`/api/community/comments?${params.toString()}`, {
          signal: controller.signal,
        })
        const payload = await response.json().catch(() => null)
        if (!response.ok) {
          throw new Error(payload?.error?.message ?? 'Could not load the discussion.')
        }
        setComments(payload.data as CommentItem[])
      } catch (err) {
        if ((err as Error).name === 'AbortError') return
        setLoadError(err instanceof Error ? err.message : 'Could not load the discussion.')
      } finally {
        if (!controller.signal.aborted) setLoading(false)
      }
    }

    load()
    return () => controller.abort()
  }, [targetType, targetId, reloadKey])

  async function submitComment(text: string, parentId?: string) {
    const response = await fetch('/api/community/comments', {
      method: 'POST',
      headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
      body: JSON.stringify({ targetType, targetId, body: text, parentId }),
    })
    const payload = await response.json().catch(() => null)

    if (response.status === 401) {
      router.push(`/auth/signin?callbackUrl=${encodeURIComponent(window.location.pathname)}`)
      throw new Error('Please sign in again.')
    }
    if (!response.ok) {
      throw new Error(payload?.error?.message ?? 'Could not post your comment.')
    }
    return payload.data as CommentItem
  }

  async function handlePost(event: React.FormEvent) {
    event.preventDefault()
    if (!body.trim() || submitting) return
    setSubmitting(true)
    setFormError(null)
    try {
      const comment = await submitComment(body.trim())
      setComments((current) => [...current, comment])
      setBody('')
    } catch (err) {
      setFormError(err instanceof Error ? err.message : 'Could not post your comment.')
    } finally {
      setSubmitting(false)
    }
  }

  async function handleReply(event: React.FormEvent, parentId: string) {
    event.preventDefault()
    if (!replyBody.trim() || replySubmitting) return
    setReplySubmitting(true)
    setFormError(null)
    try {
      const reply = await submitComment(replyBody.trim(), parentId)
      setComments((current) =>
        current.map((comment) =>
          comment.id === parentId ? { ...comment, replies: [...comment.replies, reply] } : comment
        )
      )
      setReplyBody('')
      setReplyTo(null)
    } catch (err) {
      setFormError(err instanceof Error ? err.message : 'Could not post your reply.')
    } finally {
      setReplySubmitting(false)
    }
  }

  async function handleDelete(id: string) {
    setDeletePending(true)
    try {
      const response = await fetch(`/api/community/comments/${id}`, { method: 'DELETE' })
      if (response.ok) {
        setComments((current) =>
          current
            .filter((comment) => comment.id !== id)
            .map((comment) => ({
              ...comment,
              replies: comment.replies.filter((reply) => reply.id !== id),
            }))
        )
      } else {
        const payload = await response.json().catch(() => null)
        setFormError(payload?.error?.message ?? 'Could not delete the comment.')
      }
    } catch {
      setFormError('Could not delete the comment. Check your connection and try again.')
    } finally {
      setDeletePending(false)
      setDeleteTarget(null)
    }
  }

  return (
    <section aria-labelledby="discussion-heading" data-print-hide className="space-y-5">
      <div className="flex flex-wrap items-end justify-between gap-2">
        <div>
          <h2 id="discussion-heading" className="flex items-center gap-2 text-2xl font-bold tracking-tight">
            <MessageSquare className="h-5 w-5 text-blue-500" aria-hidden="true" />
            {title}
          </h2>
          <p className="mt-1 text-sm text-muted-foreground">{description}</p>
        </div>
        {!loading && total > 0 && (
          <Badge variant="secondary" className="text-xs">
            {total} {total === 1 ? 'comment' : 'comments'}
          </Badge>
        )}
      </div>

      {status === 'authenticated' ? (
        <form onSubmit={handlePost} className="rounded-xl border border-border/50 bg-card p-4">
          <Textarea
            value={body}
            onChange={(event) => setBody(event.target.value)}
            placeholder="Ask a question or share what helped..."
            rows={3}
            maxLength={2000}
            aria-label="Write a comment"
          />
          <div className="mt-3 flex items-center justify-between gap-3">
            <span className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">{body.length} / 2000</span>
            <Button type="submit" size="sm" disabled={submitting || !body.trim()} className="gap-2">
              {submitting ? <Loader2 className="h-4 w-4 animate-spin" aria-hidden="true" /> : <Send className="h-4 w-4" aria-hidden="true" />}
              Post comment
            </Button>
          </div>
          {formError && <p className="mt-2 text-sm text-destructive" role="alert">{formError}</p>}
        </form>
      ) : (
        <div className="rounded-xl border border-dashed border-border/60 bg-muted/20 p-5 text-sm">
          <p className="text-muted-foreground">
            Sign in to join the discussion — ask questions, post corrections, or help the next learner.
          </p>
          <Button
            size="sm"
            variant="outline"
            className="mt-3"
            onClick={() =>
              router.push(
                `/auth/signin?callbackUrl=${encodeURIComponent(window.location.pathname)}`
              )
            }
          >
            Sign in to comment
          </Button>
        </div>
      )}

      {loading ? (
        <div className="space-y-3" aria-busy="true" aria-label="Loading comments">
          {Array.from({ length: 3 }).map((_, index) => (
            <div key={index} className="h-24 animate-pulse rounded-xl border border-border/50 bg-muted/30" />
          ))}
        </div>
      ) : loadError ? (
        <div className="rounded-xl border border-destructive/30 bg-destructive/5 p-5 text-sm">
          <p className="text-muted-foreground">{loadError}</p>
          <Button variant="outline" size="sm" className="mt-3" onClick={() => setReloadKey((key) => key + 1)}>
            Try again
          </Button>
        </div>
      ) : comments.length === 0 ? (
        <p className="rounded-xl border border-dashed border-border/50 p-6 text-center text-sm text-muted-foreground">
          No comments yet. Be the first to ask or share something useful.
        </p>
      ) : (
        <ul className="space-y-4" role="list">
          {comments.map((comment) => {
            const own = session?.user?.id === comment.author.id
            return (
              <li key={comment.id} className="rounded-xl border border-border/50 bg-card p-4">
                <div className="flex items-start justify-between gap-3">
                  <UserChip user={comment.author} meta={timeAgo(comment.createdAt)} />
                  {own && (
                    <button
                      type="button"
                      onClick={() => setDeleteTarget({ id: comment.id, kind: 'comment' })}
                      className="rounded-md p-1.5 text-muted-foreground transition-colors hover:bg-destructive/10 hover:text-destructive"
                      aria-label="Delete your comment"
                    >
                      <Trash2 className="h-3.5 w-3.5" aria-hidden="true" />
                    </button>
                  )}
                </div>

                <p className="mt-3 whitespace-pre-wrap text-sm leading-relaxed">{comment.body}</p>

                <div className="mt-2 flex flex-wrap items-center gap-3">
                  {status === 'authenticated' && (
                    <button
                      type="button"
                      onClick={() => {
                        setReplyTo(replyTo === comment.id ? null : comment.id)
                        setReplyBody('')
                      }}
                      className="inline-flex items-center gap-1 text-xs font-medium text-muted-foreground transition-colors hover:text-foreground"
                    >
                      <CornerDownRight className="h-3.5 w-3.5" aria-hidden="true" />
                      Reply
                    </button>
                  )}
                  {!own && (
                    <ReportButton targetType="comment" targetId={comment.id} />
                  )}
                </div>

                {replyTo === comment.id && (
                  <form onSubmit={(event) => handleReply(event, comment.id)} className="mt-3 space-y-2">
                    <Textarea
                      value={replyBody}
                      onChange={(event) => setReplyBody(event.target.value)}
                      placeholder={`Reply to ${comment.author.name ?? 'this learner'}...`}
                      rows={2}
                      maxLength={2000}
                      aria-label="Write a reply"
                      autoFocus
                    />
                    <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                      <Button type="submit" size="sm" disabled={replySubmitting || !replyBody.trim()} className="gap-2">
                        {replySubmitting && <Loader2 className="h-3.5 w-3.5 animate-spin" aria-hidden="true" />}
                        Reply
                      </Button>
                      <Button type="button" size="sm" variant="ghost" onClick={() => setReplyTo(null)}>
                        Cancel
                      </Button>
                    </div>
                  </form>
                )}

                {comment.replies.length > 0 && (
                  <ul className="mt-4 space-y-3 border-l-2 border-border/60 pl-4" role="list">
                    {comment.replies.map((reply) => {
                      const replyOwn = session?.user?.id === reply.author.id
                      return (
                        <li key={reply.id}>
                          <div className="flex items-start justify-between gap-3">
                            <UserChip user={reply.author} meta={timeAgo(reply.createdAt)} />
                            {replyOwn && (
                              <button
                                type="button"
                                onClick={() => setDeleteTarget({ id: reply.id, kind: 'reply' })}
                                className="rounded-md p-1.5 text-muted-foreground transition-colors hover:bg-destructive/10 hover:text-destructive"
                                aria-label="Delete your reply"
                              >
                                <Trash2 className="h-3.5 w-3.5" aria-hidden="true" />
                              </button>
                            )}
                          </div>
                          <p className="mt-2 whitespace-pre-wrap text-sm leading-relaxed">{reply.body}</p>
                          {!replyOwn && (
                            <div className="mt-2">
                              <ReportButton targetType="comment" targetId={reply.id} />
                            </div>
                          )}
                        </li>
                      )
                    })}
                  </ul>
                )}
              </li>
            )
          })}
        </ul>
      )}

      <ConfirmDialog
        open={deleteTarget !== null}
        onOpenChange={(open) => {
          if (!open) setDeleteTarget(null)
        }}
        title={deleteTarget?.kind === 'reply' ? 'Delete this reply?' : 'Delete this comment?'}
        description={
          deleteTarget?.kind === 'reply'
            ? 'The reply will be removed immediately. This cannot be undone.'
            : 'The comment and any replies will be removed immediately. This cannot be undone.'
        }
        confirmLabel={deleteTarget?.kind === 'reply' ? 'Delete reply' : 'Delete comment'}
        destructive
        pending={deletePending}
        onConfirm={() => {
          if (deleteTarget) return handleDelete(deleteTarget.id)
        }}
      />
    </section>
  )
}


===== FILE: src/components/community/community-stats.tsx =====
'use client'


import { useEffect, useState } from 'react'
import type { CommunityStats as Stats } from '@/lib/community-types'

const labels: { key: keyof Stats; label: string }[] = [
  { key: 'members', label: 'Members' },
  { key: 'projects', label: 'Projects shared' },
  { key: 'comments', label: 'Comments posted' },
  { key: 'lessonsCompleted', label: 'Lessons completed' },
]

export function CommunityStats() {
  const [stats, setStats] = useState<Stats | null>(null)
  const [status, setStatus] = useState<'loading' | 'ready' | 'error'>('loading')

  useEffect(() => {
    const controller = new AbortController()

    async function load() {
      try {
        const response = await fetch('/api/community/stats', { signal: controller.signal })
        if (!response.ok) throw new Error('Could not load community stats.')
        const payload = await response.json()
        setStats(payload.data as Stats)
        setStatus('ready')
      } catch (error) {
        if ((error as Error).name === 'AbortError') return
        // The landing page stays useful without stats; show dashes.
        setStatus('error')
      }
    }

    load()
    return () => controller.abort()
  }, [])

  return (
    <div
      className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-6 sm:grid-cols-4"
      aria-busy={status === 'loading'}
      aria-live="polite"
    >
      {labels.map((entry) => (
        <div key={entry.key} className="text-center">
          {status === 'loading' ? (
            <div className="mx-auto h-9 w-16 animate-pulse rounded-md bg-muted" aria-hidden="true" />
          ) : (
            <p className="text-3xl font-bold tabular-nums">
              {stats ? stats[entry.key].toLocaleString() : '—'}
            </p>
          )}
          <p className="mt-1 text-sm text-muted-foreground">{entry.label}</p>
        </div>
      ))}
    </div>
  )
}


===== FILE: src/components/community/profile-client.tsx =====
'use client'


import { useEffect, useState } from 'react'
import Link from 'next/link'
import { useRouter } from 'next/navigation'
import { useSession } from 'next-auth/react'
import {
  ArrowLeft,
  Award,
  BookOpen,
  Check,
  Clock,
  ExternalLink,
  Eye,
  Flame,
  Loader2,
  MessageSquare,
  Pencil,
  Rocket,
  ThumbsUp,
  UserPlus,
  Users,
  Zap,
} from 'lucide-react'
import { Avatar, AvatarFallback, AvatarImage } from '@/components/ui/avatar'
import { Badge } from '@/components/ui/badge'
import { Button } from '@/components/ui/button'
import { Card, CardContent } from '@/components/ui/card'
import { Separator } from '@/components/ui/separator'
import type { ProjectListItem } from '@/lib/community-types'
import { tierIcons, tierOrder, type BadgeTier } from '@/lib/badge-meta'
import { shortDate, timeAgo } from '@/lib/time'
import { GithubIcon } from '@/components/icons/brand'
import { ReportButton } from '@/components/community/report-button'

interface ProfileResponse {
  id: string
  name: string | null
  image: string | null
  bio: string | null
  website: string | null
  githubUrl: string | null
  twitterUrl: string | null
  createdAt: string
  isSelf: boolean
  followers: number
  following: number
  relationship: {
    status:
      | 'self'
      | 'none'
      | 'following'
      | 'pending_outgoing'
      | 'pending_incoming'
    requestId: string | null
  }
  viewCounts: {
    allTime: number
    month: number
    week: number
    day: number
  } | null
  progress: {
    xp: number
    streakCurrent: number
    streakLongest: number
    lessonsCompleted: number
    featuredBadges: string[]
    badges: {
      key: string
      name: string
      description: string
      icon: string
      category: string
      tier: BadgeTier
      earnedAt: string | null
    }[]
  } | null
  projects: ProjectListItem[]
  contributions: {
    projects: number
    comments: number
    upvotes: number
  }
}

function displayName(user: { name: string | null }): string {
  return user.name?.trim() || 'Anonymous learner'
}

export function ProfileClient({ id }: { id: string }) {
  const router = useRouter()
  const { status: sessionStatus } = useSession()
  const [profile, setProfile] = useState<ProfileResponse | null>(null)
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true)
  const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null)
  const [relationship, setRelationship] = useState<ProfileResponse['relationship'] | null>(null)
  const [followers, setFollowers] = useState(0)
  const [following, setFollowing] = useState(0)
  const [followBusy, setFollowBusy] = useState(false)
  const [followError, setFollowError] = useState<string | null>(null)
  const [followNotice, setFollowNotice] = useState<string | null>(null)

  useEffect(() => {
    const controller = new AbortController()

    async function load() {
      setLoading(true)
      setError(null)
      try {
        const response = await fetch(`/api/community/people/${id}`, {
          signal: controller.signal,
        })
        const payload = await response.json().catch(() => null)
        if (!response.ok) {
          throw new Error(payload?.error?.message ?? 'Could not load this profile.')
        }
        const data = payload.data as ProfileResponse
        setProfile(data)
        setRelationship(data.relationship)
        setFollowers(data.followers)
        setFollowing(data.following)
      } catch (err) {
        if ((err as Error).name === 'AbortError') return
        setError(err instanceof Error ? err.message : 'Could not load this profile.')
      } finally {
        if (!controller.signal.aborted) setLoading(false)
      }
    }

    load()
    return () => controller.abort()
  }, [id])

  async function toggleFollow() {
    if (!profile || !relationship || followBusy) return
    if (sessionStatus !== 'authenticated') {
      const callbackUrl = encodeURIComponent(`/community/people/${profile.id}`)
      router.push(`/auth/signin?callbackUrl=${callbackUrl}`)
      return
    }

    setFollowBusy(true)
    setFollowError(null)
    setFollowNotice(null)

    try {
      if (relationship.status === 'pending_incoming' && relationship.requestId) {
        const response = await fetch(
          `/api/community/follow-requests/${relationship.requestId}`,
          {
            method: 'POST',
            headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
            body: JSON.stringify({ action: 'accept' }),
          }
        )
        const payload = await response.json().catch(() => null)
        if (!response.ok) {
          throw new Error(payload?.error?.message ?? 'Could not accept that request.')
        }
        // Accepting their request makes them follow you; it does not make you
        // follow them, so their follower count is unchanged.
        setFollowing((current) => current + 1)
        setRelationship({ status: 'none', requestId: null })
        setFollowNotice('Follow request accepted. You can follow them back if you like.')
        return
      }

      if (
        relationship.status === 'following' ||
        relationship.status === 'pending_outgoing'
      ) {
        const response = await fetch(`/api/community/people/${profile.id}/follow`, {
          method: 'DELETE',
        })
        const payload = await response.json().catch(() => null)
        if (!response.ok) {
          throw new Error(payload?.error?.message ?? 'Could not update your follow.')
        }
        if (relationship.status === 'following') {
          setFollowers((current) => Math.max(0, current - 1))
        }
        setRelationship({ status: 'none', requestId: null })
        return
      }

      const response = await fetch(`/api/community/people/${profile.id}/follow`, {
        method: 'POST',
      })
      const payload = await response.json().catch(() => null)
      if (!response.ok) {
        throw new Error(payload?.error?.message ?? 'Could not send that follow request.')
      }
      setRelationship({
        status: 'pending_outgoing',
        requestId: payload?.data?.requestId ?? null,
      })
      setFollowNotice('Follow request sent. They will be notified.')
    } catch (err) {
      setFollowError(
        err instanceof Error ? err.message : 'Could not update your follow.'
      )
    } finally {
      setFollowBusy(false)
    }
  }

  if (loading) {
    return (
      <div className="space-y-4" aria-busy="true" aria-label="Loading profile">
        <div className="h-40 animate-pulse rounded-xl bg-muted/50" />
        <div className="h-32 animate-pulse rounded-xl bg-muted/30" />
      </div>
    )
  }

  if (error || !profile) {
    return (
      <div className="rounded-xl border border-border/60 bg-muted/20 p-10 text-center">
        <h1 className="text-xl font-semibold">Profile not available</h1>
        <p className="mx-auto mt-2 max-w-md text-sm text-muted-foreground">
          {error ?? 'This learner may have removed their profile.'}
        </p>
        <Button asChild variant="outline" className="mt-5 gap-2">
          <Link href="/community/showcase">
            <ArrowLeft className="h-4 w-4" aria-hidden="true" />
            Back to the showcase
          </Link>
        </Button>
      </div>
    )
  }

  const name = displayName(profile)
  const links = [
    profile.website && { href: profile.website, label: 'Website', icon: ExternalLink },
    profile.githubUrl && { href: profile.githubUrl, label: 'GitHub', icon: GithubIcon },
    profile.twitterUrl && { href: profile.twitterUrl, label: 'Twitter', icon: ExternalLink },
  ].filter(Boolean) as { href: string; label: string; icon: React.ComponentType<{ className?: string }> }[]

  const relationshipStatus = relationship?.status ?? profile.relationship.status
  const followLabel =
    relationshipStatus === 'following'
      ? 'Following'
      : relationshipStatus === 'pending_outgoing'
        ? 'Requested'
        : relationshipStatus === 'pending_incoming'
          ? 'Accept request'
          : 'Follow'

  const badgeByKey = new Map(
    (profile.progress?.badges ?? []).map((badge) => [badge.key, badge])
  )
  const pinnedBadges = (profile.progress?.featuredBadges ?? [])
    .map((key) => badgeByKey.get(key))
    .filter((badge): badge is NonNullable<typeof badge> => Boolean(badge))
  const showcaseBadges =
    pinnedBadges.length > 0
      ? pinnedBadges
      : (profile.progress?.badges ?? [])
          .slice()
          .sort((a, b) => tierOrder[a.tier] - tierOrder[b.tier])
          .slice(0, 3)

  return (
    <div className="space-y-8">
      <Card className="overflow-hidden">
        <div className="h-24 bg-gradient-to-r from-emerald-500/20 via-blue-500/10 to-violet-500/20" aria-hidden="true" />
        <CardContent className="relative pt-0">
          <div className="-mt-10 flex flex-col gap-4 sm:flex-row sm:items-end sm:justify-between">
            <div className="flex items-end gap-4">
              <Avatar className="h-20 w-20 border-4 border-background">
                <AvatarImage src={profile.image || ''} alt="" />
                <AvatarFallback className="text-2xl">{name.charAt(0).toUpperCase()}</AvatarFallback>
              </Avatar>
              <div className="pb-1">
                <h1 className="text-2xl font-bold tracking-tight">{name}</h1>
                <p className="text-sm text-muted-foreground">Joined {shortDate(profile.createdAt)}</p>
                {relationshipStatus === 'pending_incoming' && (
                  <Badge variant="secondary" className="mt-1.5">
                    Requested to follow you
                  </Badge>
                )}
              </div>
            </div>
            {profile.isSelf ? (
              <Button asChild variant="outline" size="sm" className="gap-2">
                <Link href="/settings">
                  <Pencil className="h-3.5 w-3.5" aria-hidden="true" />
                  Edit profile
                </Link>
              </Button>
            ) : (
              <div className="flex flex-col items-start gap-1.5">
                <Button
                  type="button"
                  size="sm"
                  variant={
                    relationshipStatus === 'following' ||
                    relationshipStatus === 'pending_outgoing'
                      ? 'outline'
                      : 'default'
                  }
                  className="gap-2"
                  disabled={followBusy || sessionStatus === 'loading'}
                  onClick={toggleFollow}
                  title={
                    relationshipStatus === 'following'
                      ? 'Unfollow'
                      : relationshipStatus === 'pending_outgoing'
                        ? 'Cancel follow request'
                        : undefined
                  }
                >
                  {followBusy ? (
                    <Loader2 className="h-3.5 w-3.5 animate-spin" aria-hidden="true" />
                  ) : relationshipStatus === 'following' ? (
                    <Check className="h-3.5 w-3.5" aria-hidden="true" />
                  ) : relationshipStatus === 'pending_outgoing' ? (
                    <Clock className="h-3.5 w-3.5" aria-hidden="true" />
                  ) : (
                    <UserPlus className="h-3.5 w-3.5" aria-hidden="true" />
                  )}
                  {followLabel}
                </Button>
                {followNotice && (
                  <p className="max-w-xs text-xs text-emerald-600 dark:text-emerald-400" role="status">
                    {followNotice}
                  </p>
                )}
                {followError && (
                  <p className="max-w-xs text-xs text-destructive" role="alert">
                    {followError}
                  </p>
                )}
                <ReportButton targetType="profile" targetId={profile.id} />
              </div>
            )}
          </div>

          {profile.bio && (
            <p className="mt-5 max-w-2xl whitespace-pre-wrap text-sm leading-relaxed text-foreground/90">
              {profile.bio}
            </p>
          )}

          {links.length > 0 && (
            <div className="mt-4 flex flex-wrap gap-3">
              {links.map((link) => (
                <a
                  key={link.href}
                  href={link.href}
                  target="_blank"
                  rel="noopener noreferrer"
                  className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 text-sm text-muted-foreground transition-colors hover:text-foreground"
                >
                  <link.icon className="h-4 w-4" aria-hidden="true" />
                  {link.label}
                </a>
              ))}
            </div>
          )}

          <div className="mt-6 flex flex-wrap gap-5 border-t border-border/50 pt-4 text-sm">
            <span className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 text-muted-foreground">
              <Users className="h-4 w-4" aria-hidden="true" />
              <strong className="text-foreground">{followers}</strong> followers
            </span>
            <span className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 text-muted-foreground">
              <UserPlus className="h-4 w-4" aria-hidden="true" />
              <strong className="text-foreground">{following}</strong> following
            </span>
            <span className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 text-muted-foreground">
              <Rocket className="h-4 w-4" aria-hidden="true" />
              <strong className="text-foreground">{profile.contributions.projects}</strong> projects
            </span>
            <span className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 text-muted-foreground">
              <MessageSquare className="h-4 w-4" aria-hidden="true" />
              <strong className="text-foreground">{profile.contributions.comments}</strong> comments
            </span>
            <span className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 text-muted-foreground">
              <ThumbsUp className="h-4 w-4" aria-hidden="true" />
              <strong className="text-foreground">{profile.contributions.upvotes}</strong> upvotes given
            </span>
          </div>

          {profile.isSelf && profile.viewCounts && (
            <div className="mt-4 flex flex-wrap items-center gap-x-4 gap-y-1 rounded-lg border border-border/50 bg-muted/20 px-3 py-2.5 text-sm">
              <span className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 font-medium">
                <Eye className="h-4 w-4 text-emerald-500" aria-hidden="true" />
                {profile.viewCounts.allTime.toLocaleString()} profile visitors
              </span>
              <span className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">
                {profile.viewCounts.month.toLocaleString()} this month ·{' '}
                {profile.viewCounts.week.toLocaleString()} last 7 days ·{' '}
                {profile.viewCounts.day.toLocaleString()} last 24h
              </span>
            </div>
          )}
        </CardContent>
      </Card>

      {profile.progress ? (
        <>
          <section aria-labelledby="progress-heading">
            <h2 id="progress-heading" className="mb-4 text-xl font-bold tracking-tight">
              Learning progress
            </h2>
            <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-3 sm:grid-cols-4">
              {[
                { icon: Zap, label: 'XP earned', value: profile.progress.xp.toLocaleString(), color: 'text-amber-500' },
                { icon: BookOpen, label: 'Lessons completed', value: profile.progress.lessonsCompleted, color: 'text-emerald-500' },
                { icon: Flame, label: 'Current streak', value: `${profile.progress.streakCurrent}d`, color: 'text-orange-500' },
                { icon: Award, label: 'Longest streak', value: `${profile.progress.streakLongest}d`, color: 'text-violet-500' },
              ].map((stat) => (
                <Card key={stat.label}>
                  <CardContent className="pt-5">
                    <stat.icon className={`h-5 w-5 ${stat.color}`} aria-hidden="true" />
                    <p className="mt-2 text-2xl font-bold">{stat.value}</p>
                    <p className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">{stat.label}</p>
                  </CardContent>
                </Card>
              ))}
            </div>
          </section>

          {showcaseBadges.length > 0 && (
            <section aria-labelledby="badges-heading">
              <div className="mb-4 flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-3">
                <h2 id="badges-heading" className="flex items-center gap-2 text-xl font-bold tracking-tight">
                  <Award className="h-5 w-5 text-amber-500" aria-hidden="true" />
                  Badge showcase
                </h2>
                <span className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">
                  {profile.progress.badges.length}{' '}
                  {profile.progress.badges.length === 1 ? 'badge' : 'badges'} earned
                </span>
              </div>
              <div className="grid grid-cols-1 gap-3 sm:grid-cols-3">
                {showcaseBadges.map((badge) => (
                  <div
                    key={badge.key}
                    className="rounded-xl border border-border/50 bg-card p-4"
                    title={badge.description}
                  >
                    <div className="flex items-start gap-3">
                      <span className="text-3xl leading-none" aria-hidden="true">{badge.icon}</span>
                      <div className="min-w-0">
                        <p className="text-sm font-semibold">{badge.name}</p>
                        <p className="mt-1 line-clamp-2 text-xs text-muted-foreground">
                          {badge.description}
                        </p>
                        <p className="mt-2 text-[10px] font-medium capitalize text-muted-foreground">
                          {tierIcons[badge.tier]} {badge.tier}
                        </p>
                      </div>
                    </div>
                  </div>
                ))}
              </div>
              {profile.isSelf && (
                <div className="mt-3 flex justify-end">
                  <Button asChild variant="outline" size="sm" className="gap-2">
                    <Link href="/badges">View all badges</Link>
                  </Button>
                </div>
              )}
            </section>
          )}
        </>
      ) : (
        <p className="rounded-xl border border-dashed border-border/60 bg-muted/20 p-5 text-sm text-muted-foreground">
          This learner keeps their progress private. Their shared projects and comments are still public.
        </p>
      )}

      <section aria-labelledby="projects-heading">
        <div className="mb-4 flex items-center justify-between gap-3">
          <h2 id="projects-heading" className="text-xl font-bold tracking-tight">
            Shared projects
          </h2>
          <Badge variant="secondary" className="text-xs">
            {profile.contributions.projects}
          </Badge>
        </div>

        {profile.projects.length === 0 ? (
          <p className="rounded-xl border border-dashed border-border/50 p-6 text-center text-sm text-muted-foreground">
            No projects shared yet.
          </p>
        ) : (
          <div className="grid grid-cols-1 gap-4 sm:grid-cols-2">
            {profile.projects.map((project) => (
              <Card key={project.id} className="transition-shadow hover:shadow-md">
                <CardContent className="pt-5">
                  <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                    <Badge variant="outline" className="text-xs capitalize sm:text-[10px]">
                      {project.track}
                    </Badge>
                    <span className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">{timeAgo(project.createdAt)}</span>
                  </div>
                  <h3 className="mt-2 font-semibold">
                    <Link href={`/community/showcase/${project.id}`} className="hover:text-primary transition-colors">
                      {project.title}
                    </Link>
                  </h3>
                  <p className="mt-1 line-clamp-2 text-sm text-muted-foreground">{project.description}</p>
                  <div className="mt-3 flex items-center gap-3 text-xs text-muted-foreground">
                    <span className="inline-flex items-center gap-1">
                      <ThumbsUp className="h-3.5 w-3.5" aria-hidden="true" />
                      {project.upvotes}
                    </span>
                    {project.tags.slice(0, 3).map((tag) => (
                      <span key={tag}>#{tag}</span>
                    ))}
                  </div>
                </CardContent>
              </Card>
            ))}
          </div>
        )}
      </section>

      {profile.isSelf && (
        <>
          <Separator />
          <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-3 rounded-xl border border-border/50 bg-muted/20 p-4 text-sm">
            <p className="text-muted-foreground">
              This is your public profile. Add a bio and links so other learners can find you.
            </p>
            <Button asChild size="sm" variant="outline" className="gap-2">
              <Link href="/settings">
                <Pencil className="h-3.5 w-3.5" aria-hidden="true" />
                Edit settings
              </Link>
            </Button>
          </div>
        </>
      )}
    </div>
  )
}


===== FILE: src/components/community/project-card.tsx =====
/* eslint-disable @next/next/no-img-element */
'use client'

import { memo } from 'react'
import Link from 'next/link'
import { ExternalLink, MessageSquare, Rocket } from 'lucide-react'
import { Badge } from '@/components/ui/badge'
import { UserChip } from '@/components/community/user-chip'
import { UpvoteButton } from '@/components/community/upvote-button'
import type { ProjectListItem } from '@/lib/community-types'
import { timeAgo } from '@/lib/time'
import { cn, getAccent, getTrackColor } from '@/lib/utils'
import { GithubIcon } from '@/components/icons/brand'

export const ProjectCard = memo(function ProjectCard({ project }: { project: ProjectListItem }) {
  return (
    <article className="group flex h-full flex-col overflow-hidden rounded-xl border border-border/50 bg-card transition-all motion-safe:hover:-translate-y-0.5 hover:border-border hover:shadow-lg">
      {project.imageUrl ? (
        <Link href={`/community/showcase/${project.id}`} className="block overflow-hidden bg-muted">
          <img
            src={project.imageUrl}
            alt=""
            loading="lazy"
            decoding="async"
            className="aspect-[16/9] w-full object-cover transition-transform duration-300 group-hover:scale-[1.02]"
          />
        </Link>
      ) : (
        <Link
          href={`/community/showcase/${project.id}`}
          className="flex aspect-[16/9] items-center justify-center bg-gradient-to-br from-emerald-500/10 via-transparent to-violet-500/10"
          aria-hidden="true"
        >
          <Rocket className="h-9 w-9 text-muted-foreground/40" />
        </Link>
      )}

      <div className="flex flex-1 flex-col p-5">
        <div className="mb-2 flex flex-wrap items-center gap-1.5">
          <Badge variant="outline" className={cn('text-xs capitalize sm:text-[10px]', getAccent(getTrackColor(project.track)).text)}>
            {project.track}
          </Badge>
          {project.tags.slice(0, 3).map((tag) => (
            <Badge key={tag} variant="secondary" className="text-xs font-normal sm:text-[10px]">
              {tag}
            </Badge>
          ))}
        </div>

        <h3 className="text-base font-semibold leading-snug">
          <Link href={`/community/showcase/${project.id}`} className="hover:text-primary transition-colors">
            {project.title}
          </Link>
        </h3>
        <p className="mt-1.5 line-clamp-3 text-sm text-muted-foreground">{project.description}</p>

        <div className="mt-4 flex items-center gap-3 text-xs text-muted-foreground">
          <UpvoteButton
            projectId={project.id}
            initialUpvotes={project.upvotes}
            initialUpvoted={project.upvoted}
          />
          <Link
            href={`/community/showcase/${project.id}`}
            className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 rounded-lg border border-border/60 px-2.5 py-1.5 font-semibold transition-colors hover:text-foreground"
          >
            <MessageSquare className="h-3.5 w-3.5" aria-hidden="true" />
            {project.commentCount}
          </Link>
          {project.demoUrl && (
            <a
              href={project.demoUrl}
              target="_blank"
              rel="noopener noreferrer"
              className="inline-flex items-center gap-1 transition-colors hover:text-foreground"
              title="Live demo"
            >
              <ExternalLink className="h-3.5 w-3.5" aria-hidden="true" />
              Demo
            </a>
          )}
          {project.githubUrl && (
            <a
              href={project.githubUrl}
              target="_blank"
              rel="noopener noreferrer"
              className="inline-flex items-center gap-1 transition-colors hover:text-foreground"
              title="Source code"
            >
              <GithubIcon className="h-3.5 w-3.5" aria-hidden="true" />
              Code
            </a>
          )}
        </div>

        <div className="mt-auto border-t border-border/40 pt-3">
          <UserChip user={project.author} meta={timeAgo(project.createdAt)} />
        </div>
      </div>
    </article>
  )
})


===== FILE: src/components/community/project-detail-client.tsx =====
'use client'

/* eslint-disable @next/next/no-img-element */

import { useEffect, useState } from 'react'
import Link from 'next/link'
import { useRouter } from 'next/navigation'
import {
  ArrowLeft,
  Calendar,
  ExternalLink,
  Loader2,
  MessageSquare,
  Pencil,
  Trash2,
} from 'lucide-react'
import { Badge } from '@/components/ui/badge'
import { Button } from '@/components/ui/button'
import { Card, CardContent } from '@/components/ui/card'
import { ConfirmDialog } from '@/components/ui/confirm-dialog'
import { Separator } from '@/components/ui/separator'
import { UserChip } from '@/components/community/user-chip'
import { UpvoteButton } from '@/components/community/upvote-button'
import { CommentThread } from '@/components/community/comment-thread'
import { ProjectFormDialog } from '@/components/community/project-form-dialog'
import { ReportButton } from '@/components/community/report-button'
import type { ProjectDetail } from '@/lib/community-types'
import { shortDate, timeAgo } from '@/lib/time'
import { getAccent, getTrackColor } from '@/lib/utils'
import { GithubIcon } from '@/components/icons/brand'

export function ProjectDetailClient({ id }: { id: string }) {
  const router = useRouter()
  const [project, setProject] = useState<ProjectDetail | null>(null)
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true)
  const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null)
  const [deleting, setDeleting] = useState(false)
  const [deleteConfirmOpen, setDeleteConfirmOpen] = useState(false)
  const [deleteError, setDeleteError] = useState<string | null>(null)
  const [editOpen, setEditOpen] = useState(false)

  useEffect(() => {
    const controller = new AbortController()

    async function load() {
      setLoading(true)
      setError(null)
      try {
        const response = await fetch(`/api/community/projects/${id}`, {
          signal: controller.signal,
        })
        const payload = await response.json().catch(() => null)
        if (!response.ok) {
          throw new Error(payload?.error?.message ?? 'Could not load this project.')
        }
        setProject(payload.data as ProjectDetail)
      } catch (err) {
        if ((err as Error).name === 'AbortError') return
        setError(err instanceof Error ? err.message : 'Could not load this project.')
      } finally {
        if (!controller.signal.aborted) setLoading(false)
      }
    }

    load()
    return () => controller.abort()
  }, [id])

  async function handleDelete() {
    if (!project || deleting) return
    setDeleting(true)
    setDeleteError(null)
    try {
