      const response = await fetch(`/api/community/projects/${project.id}`, { method: 'DELETE' })
      if (response.ok) {
        setDeleteConfirmOpen(false)
        router.push('/community/showcase')
        router.refresh()
        return
      }
      const payload = await response.json().catch(() => null)
      setDeleteError(payload?.error?.message ?? 'Could not delete the project.')
    } catch {
      setDeleteError('Could not delete the project. Check your connection and try again.')
    } finally {
      setDeleting(false)
      setDeleteConfirmOpen(false)
    }
  }

  if (loading) {
    return (
      <div className="space-y-4" aria-busy="true" aria-label="Loading project">
        <div className="h-8 w-40 animate-pulse rounded-md bg-muted" />
        <div className="h-72 animate-pulse rounded-xl bg-muted/60" />
        <div className="h-32 animate-pulse rounded-xl bg-muted/40" />
      </div>
    )
  }

  if (error || !project) {
    return (
      <div className="rounded-xl border border-border/60 bg-muted/20 p-10 text-center">
        <h1 className="text-xl font-semibold">Project not available</h1>
        <p className="mx-auto mt-2 max-w-md text-sm text-muted-foreground">
          {error ?? 'This project may have been removed.'}
        </p>
        <Button asChild variant="outline" className="mt-5 gap-2">
          <Link href="/community/showcase">
            <ArrowLeft className="h-4 w-4" aria-hidden="true" />
            Back to the showcase
          </Link>
        </Button>
      </div>
    )
  }

  return (
    <div className="space-y-10">
      <Link
        href="/community/showcase"
        className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 text-sm text-muted-foreground transition-colors hover:text-foreground"
      >
        <ArrowLeft className="h-4 w-4" aria-hidden="true" />
        All projects
      </Link>

      <div className="grid grid-cols-1 gap-8 lg:grid-cols-[minmax(0,1fr)_320px]">
        <div className="min-w-0">
          <div className="mb-3 flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2">
            <Badge
              variant="outline"
              className={`capitalize ${getAccent(getTrackColor(project.track)).text}`}
            >
              {project.track} track
            </Badge>
            {project.tags.map((tag) => (
              <Badge key={tag} variant="secondary" className="font-normal">
                {tag}
              </Badge>
            ))}
          </div>

          <h1 className="text-3xl font-bold tracking-tight sm:text-4xl">{project.title}</h1>

          {project.imageUrl && (
            <div className="mt-6 overflow-hidden rounded-xl border border-border/50 bg-muted">
              <img
                src={project.imageUrl}
                alt=""
                loading="eager"
                decoding="async"
                className="max-h-[480px] w-full object-cover"
              />
            </div>
          )}

          <div className="mt-6 whitespace-pre-wrap text-base leading-relaxed text-foreground/90">
            {project.description}
          </div>

          <div className="mt-7 flex flex-wrap gap-3">
            {project.demoUrl && (
              <Button asChild className="gap-2">
                <a href={project.demoUrl} target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">
                  <ExternalLink className="h-4 w-4" aria-hidden="true" />
                  Open live demo
                </a>
              </Button>
            )}
            {project.githubUrl && (
              <Button asChild variant="outline" className="gap-2">
                <a href={project.githubUrl} target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">
                  <GithubIcon className="h-4 w-4" aria-hidden="true" />
                  View source
                </a>
              </Button>
            )}
          </div>
        </div>

        <aside className="space-y-4">
          <Card>
            <CardContent className="space-y-4 pt-6">
              <div>
                <p className="text-xs font-medium uppercase tracking-wide text-muted-foreground">Shared by</p>
                <UserChip user={project.author} meta={`Joined ${shortDate(project.author.createdAt)}`} size="md" className="mt-2" />
              </div>

              <Separator />

              <div className="flex items-center justify-between gap-3">
                <UpvoteButton
                  projectId={project.id}
                  initialUpvotes={project.upvotes}
                  initialUpvoted={project.upvoted}
                  size="lg"
                />
                <a
                  href="#discussion-heading"
                  className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 text-sm text-muted-foreground transition-colors hover:text-foreground"
                >
                  <MessageSquare className="h-4 w-4" aria-hidden="true" />
                  {project.commentCount} {project.commentCount === 1 ? 'comment' : 'comments'}
                </a>
              </div>

              <div className="space-y-1 text-xs text-muted-foreground">
                <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5">
                  <Calendar className="h-3.5 w-3.5" aria-hidden="true" />
                  Published {timeAgo(project.createdAt)}
                </div>
                {new Date(project.updatedAt).getTime() - new Date(project.createdAt).getTime() > 60_000 && (
                  <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5">
                    <Pencil className="h-3.5 w-3.5" aria-hidden="true" />
                    Updated {timeAgo(project.updatedAt)}
                  </div>
                )}
              </div>

              {!project.isOwner && (
                <ReportButton targetType="project" targetId={project.id} className="w-full justify-start" />
              )}

              {project.isOwner && (
                <>
                  <Separator />
                  <div className="space-y-2">
                    <Button
                      variant="outline"
                      size="sm"
                      onClick={() => setEditOpen(true)}
                      className="w-full gap-2"
                    >
                      <Pencil className="h-4 w-4" aria-hidden="true" />
                      Edit project
                    </Button>
                    <Button
                      variant="outline"
                      size="sm"
                      onClick={() => {
                        setDeleteError(null)
                        setDeleteConfirmOpen(true)
                      }}
                      disabled={deleting}
                      className="w-full gap-2 text-destructive hover:bg-destructive/10 hover:text-destructive"
                    >
                      {deleting ? (
                        <Loader2 className="h-4 w-4 animate-spin" aria-hidden="true" />
                      ) : (
                        <Trash2 className="h-4 w-4" aria-hidden="true" />
                      )}
                      Delete project
                    </Button>
                    {deleteError && (
                      <p className="text-sm text-destructive" role="alert">
                        {deleteError}
                      </p>
                    )}
                  </div>
                </>
              )}
            </CardContent>
          </Card>

          <Card className="border-dashed">
            <CardContent className="pt-6 text-sm text-muted-foreground">
              <p className="font-medium text-foreground">Built something too?</p>
              <p className="mt-1">
                Share the project you learned on. Feedback from other learners is the fastest review you
                will ever get.
              </p>
              <Button asChild size="sm" variant="outline" className="mt-3">
                <Link href="/community/showcase">Share a project</Link>
              </Button>
            </CardContent>
          </Card>
        </aside>
      </div>

      <ProjectFormDialog
        open={editOpen}
        onOpenChange={setEditOpen}
        project={project}
        onSaved={(updated) => {
          setProject(updated)
          setEditOpen(false)
          router.refresh()
        }}
      />

      <ConfirmDialog
        open={deleteConfirmOpen}
        onOpenChange={setDeleteConfirmOpen}
        title="Delete this project?"
        description="The project, its screenshot, and all of its comments will be removed. This cannot be undone."
        confirmLabel="Delete project"
        destructive
        pending={deleting}
        onConfirm={handleDelete}
      />

      <Separator />

      <CommentThread
        targetType="project"
        targetId={project.id}
        title="Feedback & questions"
        description="Ask how something was built, leave a suggestion, or cheer the builder on."
      />
    </div>
  )
}


===== FILE: src/components/community/project-form-dialog.tsx =====
'use client'

import { useRef, useState } from 'react'
import { useRouter } from 'next/navigation'
import { ImageUp, Loader2, Save, Send } from 'lucide-react'
import { Button } from '@/components/ui/button'
import {
  Dialog,
  DialogContent,
  DialogDescription,
  DialogFooter,
  DialogHeader,
  DialogTitle,
} from '@/components/ui/dialog'
import { Input } from '@/components/ui/input'
import { Label } from '@/components/ui/label'
import { Textarea } from '@/components/ui/textarea'
import type { ProjectDetail } from '@/lib/community-types'
import { tracks } from '@/lib/curriculum'

const emptyForm = {
  title: '',
  description: '',
  track: 'developer',
  githubUrl: '',
  demoUrl: '',
  imageUrl: '',
  tags: '',
}

function formFromProject(project: ProjectDetail | null | undefined) {
  if (!project) return emptyForm
  return {
    title: project.title,
    description: project.description,
    track: project.track,
    githubUrl: project.githubUrl ?? '',
    demoUrl: project.demoUrl ?? '',
    imageUrl: project.imageUrl ?? '',
    tags: project.tags.join(', '),
  }
}

export function ProjectFormDialog({
  open,
  onOpenChange,
  project,
  onSaved,
}: {
  open: boolean
  onOpenChange: (open: boolean) => void
  project?: ProjectDetail | null
  onSaved: (project: ProjectDetail) => void
}) {
  const editing = Boolean(project)

  return (
    <Dialog open={open} onOpenChange={onOpenChange}>
      <DialogContent className="max-h-[90vh] overflow-y-auto sm:max-w-xl">
        <DialogHeader>
          <DialogTitle>{editing ? 'Edit project' : 'Share your project'}</DialogTitle>
          <DialogDescription>
            {editing
              ? 'Fix a typo, update a link, or refresh the screenshot. Your comments and upvotes stay intact.'
              : 'Show what you built with the MERN stack. A live demo and a repo link help people learn from your work — rough edges are welcome.'}
          </DialogDescription>
        </DialogHeader>

        {/* Radix unmounts the dialog content when closed, so the form starts from
            the current project values every time it opens. */}
        <ProjectForm
          key={project?.id ?? 'new'}
          project={project}
          onSaved={onSaved}
          onClose={() => onOpenChange(false)}
        />
      </DialogContent>
    </Dialog>
  )
}

function ProjectForm({
  project,
  onSaved,
  onClose,
}: {
  project?: ProjectDetail | null
  onSaved: (project: ProjectDetail) => void
  onClose: () => void
}) {
  const router = useRouter()
  const editing = Boolean(project)
  const [form, setForm] = useState(() => formFromProject(project))
  const [submitting, setSubmitting] = useState(false)
  const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null)
  const [uploadingImage, setUploadingImage] = useState(false)
  const [uploadError, setUploadError] = useState<string | null>(null)
  const imageInputRef = useRef<HTMLInputElement>(null)

  function update<K extends keyof typeof form>(key: K, value: string) {
    setForm((current) => ({ ...current, [key]: value }))
  }

  async function handleImageUpload(event: React.ChangeEvent<HTMLInputElement>) {
    const file = event.target.files?.[0]
    event.target.value = ''
    if (!file || uploadingImage) return

    if (!file.type.startsWith('image/')) {
      setUploadError('Choose an image file.')
      return
    }
    if (file.size > 5 * 1024 * 1024) {
      setUploadError('Choose an image smaller than 5 MB.')
      return
    }

    setUploadingImage(true)
    setUploadError(null)

    try {
      const body = new FormData()
      body.append('file', file)
      const response = await fetch('/api/community/uploads', {
        method: 'POST',
        body,
      })
      const payload = await response.json().catch(() => null)
      if (!response.ok) {
        throw new Error(payload?.error?.message ?? 'Could not upload that image.')
      }
      update('imageUrl', payload.data.url)
    } catch (err) {
      setUploadError(err instanceof Error ? err.message : 'Could not upload that image.')
    } finally {
      setUploadingImage(false)
    }
  }

  async function handleSubmit(event: React.FormEvent<HTMLFormElement>) {
    event.preventDefault()
    if (submitting) return
    setSubmitting(true)
    setError(null)

    try {
      const response = await fetch(
        editing && project ? `/api/community/projects/${project.id}` : '/api/community/projects',
        {
          method: editing ? 'PATCH' : 'POST',
          headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
          body: JSON.stringify({
            ...form,
            tags: form.tags
              .split(',')
              .map((tag) => tag.trim())
              .filter(Boolean),
          }),
        }
      )

      const body = await response.json().catch(() => null)
      if (response.status === 401) {
        const callbackUrl = project
          ? `/community/showcase/${project.id}`
          : '/community/showcase'
        router.push(`/auth/signin?callbackUrl=${encodeURIComponent(callbackUrl)}`)
        return
      }
      if (!response.ok) {
        throw new Error(
          body?.error?.message ??
            (editing ? 'Could not save your changes.' : 'Could not share your project.')
        )
      }

      onSaved(body.data as ProjectDetail)
      onClose()
    } catch (err) {
      setError(
        err instanceof Error
          ? err.message
          : editing
            ? 'Could not save your changes.'
            : 'Could not share your project.'
      )
    } finally {
      setSubmitting(false)
    }
  }

  return (
    <form onSubmit={handleSubmit} className="space-y-4">
      <div className="space-y-1.5">
        <Label htmlFor="project-title">Title</Label>
        <Input
          id="project-title"
          value={form.title}
          onChange={(event) => update('title', event.target.value)}
          placeholder="Movie watchlist with reviews"
          minLength={4}
          maxLength={100}
          required
        />
      </div>

      <div className="space-y-1.5">
        <Label htmlFor="project-description">What did you build?</Label>
        <Textarea
          id="project-description"
          value={form.description}
          onChange={(event) => update('description', event.target.value)}
          placeholder="The problem, the stack, and the one thing you are proud of. Links to the tricky parts are gold."
          rows={5}
          minLength={30}
          maxLength={4000}
          required
        />
        <p className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">
          {form.description.trim().length} / 4000 characters (minimum 30)
        </p>
      </div>

      <div className="grid grid-cols-1 gap-4 sm:grid-cols-2">
        <div className="space-y-1.5">
          <Label htmlFor="project-track">Track</Label>
          <select
            id="project-track"
            value={form.track}
            onChange={(event) => update('track', event.target.value)}
            className="flex h-9 w-full rounded-md border border-input bg-transparent px-3 py-1 text-sm shadow-xs outline-none focus-visible:border-ring focus-visible:ring-ring/50 focus-visible:ring-[3px]"
          >
            {tracks.map((track) => (
              <option key={track.key} value={track.key}>
                {track.name}
              </option>
            ))}
          </select>
        </div>
        <div className="space-y-1.5">
          <Label htmlFor="project-tags">Tags</Label>
          <Input
            id="project-tags"
            value={form.tags}
            onChange={(event) => update('tags', event.target.value)}
            placeholder="react, express, mongodb"
          />
          <p className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">Comma separated, up to 8.</p>
        </div>
      </div>

      <div className="grid grid-cols-1 gap-4 sm:grid-cols-2">
        <div className="space-y-1.5">
          <Label htmlFor="project-github">GitHub URL</Label>
          <Input
            id="project-github"
            type="url"
            value={form.githubUrl}
            onChange={(event) => update('githubUrl', event.target.value)}
            placeholder="https://github.com/you/project"
          />
        </div>
        <div className="space-y-1.5">
          <Label htmlFor="project-demo">Live demo URL</Label>
          <Input
            id="project-demo"
            type="url"
            value={form.demoUrl}
            onChange={(event) => update('demoUrl', event.target.value)}
            placeholder="https://your-project.vercel.app"
          />
        </div>
      </div>

      <div className="space-y-1.5">
        <Label htmlFor="project-image">Screenshot</Label>
        <div className="flex flex-col gap-2 sm:flex-row">
          <Input
            id="project-image"
            type="url"
            value={form.imageUrl}
            onChange={(event) => update('imageUrl', event.target.value)}
            placeholder="https://.../screenshot.png"
          />
          <input
            ref={imageInputRef}
            type="file"
            accept="image/jpeg,image/png,image/webp,image/gif,image/avif"
            className="sr-only"
            onChange={handleImageUpload}
            tabIndex={-1}
          />
          <Button
            type="button"
            variant="outline"
            className="gap-2 sm:shrink-0"
            disabled={uploadingImage}
            onClick={() => imageInputRef.current?.click()}
          >
            {uploadingImage ? (
              <Loader2 className="h-4 w-4 animate-spin" aria-hidden="true" />
            ) : (
              <ImageUp className="h-4 w-4" aria-hidden="true" />
            )}
            {uploadingImage ? 'Uploading...' : 'Upload'}
          </Button>
        </div>
        <p className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">
          Optional. Upload an image up to 5 MB, or paste a direct image URL (16:9 works best).
        </p>
        {uploadError && (
          <p className="text-xs text-destructive" role="alert">
            {uploadError}
          </p>
        )}
        {form.imageUrl && (
          <div className="mt-2 overflow-hidden rounded-lg border border-border/50 bg-muted">
            {/* eslint-disable-next-line @next/next/no-img-element */}
            <img src={form.imageUrl} alt="Screenshot preview" className="max-h-44 w-full object-cover" />
          </div>
        )}
        <details className="rounded-lg border border-border/60 bg-muted/20 px-3 py-2 text-xs">
          <summary className="cursor-pointer font-medium">How do I get a screenshot URL?</summary>
          <div className="mt-2 space-y-2 text-muted-foreground">
            <p>
              <strong className="text-foreground">GitHub (easiest):</strong> commit the image to
              your project repo, open it on GitHub, click <em>Raw</em>, and copy the address bar
              URL. It should look like{' '}
              <code className="rounded bg-background px-1 py-0.5">
                raw.githubusercontent.com/you/repo/main/screenshot.png
              </code>
              .
            </p>
            <p>
              You can also drag the image into a GitHub issue or comment and copy the generated
              image link, or upload it to any image host that gives you a direct{' '}
              <code className="rounded bg-background px-1 py-0.5">https://...png</code> URL.
            </p>
            <p>
              Paste the <strong className="text-foreground">image</strong> URL, not the page that
              displays the image.
            </p>
          </div>
        </details>
      </div>

      {error && (
        <p
          className="rounded-lg border border-destructive/30 bg-destructive/5 px-3 py-2 text-sm text-destructive"
          role="alert"
        >
          {error}
        </p>
      )}

      <DialogFooter>
        <Button type="button" variant="ghost" onClick={onClose} disabled={submitting}>
          Cancel
        </Button>
        <Button type="submit" disabled={submitting} className="gap-2">
          {submitting ? (
            <Loader2 className="h-4 w-4 animate-spin" aria-hidden="true" />
          ) : editing ? (
            <Save className="h-4 w-4" aria-hidden="true" />
          ) : (
            <Send className="h-4 w-4" aria-hidden="true" />
          )}
          {editing ? 'Save changes' : 'Publish project'}
        </Button>
      </DialogFooter>
    </form>
  )
}


===== FILE: src/components/community/report-button.tsx =====
'use client'

import { useState } from 'react'
import { Flag, Loader2 } from 'lucide-react'
import { useSession } from 'next-auth/react'
import { Button } from '@/components/ui/button'
import {
  Dialog,
  DialogContent,
  DialogDescription,
  DialogFooter,
  DialogHeader,
  DialogTitle,
  DialogTrigger,
} from '@/components/ui/dialog'
import { Label } from '@/components/ui/label'
import { Textarea } from '@/components/ui/textarea'

type ReportTarget = 'project' | 'comment' | 'profile'

const REASONS = [
  { value: 'spam', label: 'Spam or advertising' },
  { value: 'harassment', label: 'Harassment or hate' },
  { value: 'inappropriate', label: 'Inappropriate content' },
  { value: 'copyright', label: 'Copyright violation' },
  { value: 'other', label: 'Something else' },
] as const

export function ReportButton({
  targetType,
  targetId,
  className,
}: {
  targetType: ReportTarget
  targetId: string
  className?: string
}) {
  const { status } = useSession()
  const [open, setOpen] = useState(false)
  const [reason, setReason] = useState<string>('spam')
  const [details, setDetails] = useState('')
  const [busy, setBusy] = useState(false)
  const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null)
  const [submitted, setSubmitted] = useState(false)

  if (status !== 'authenticated') return null

  async function submit(event: React.FormEvent) {
    event.preventDefault()
    if (busy) return

    setBusy(true)
    setError(null)

    try {
      const response = await fetch('/api/community/reports', {
        method: 'POST',
        headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
        body: JSON.stringify({
          targetType,
          targetId,
          reason,
          details: details.trim() || undefined,
        }),
      })
      const payload = await response.json().catch(() => null)

      if (!response.ok) {
        throw new Error(
          payload?.error?.message ?? 'Could not submit this report right now.'
        )
      }

      setSubmitted(true)
      setDetails('')
    } catch (err) {
      setError(err instanceof Error ? err.message : 'Could not submit this report.')
    } finally {
      setBusy(false)
    }
  }

  return (
    <Dialog
      open={open}
      onOpenChange={(next) => {
        setOpen(next)
        if (!next) {
          setError(null)
          setSubmitted(false)
        }
      }}
    >
      <DialogTrigger asChild>
        <Button
          type="button"
          variant="ghost"
          size="sm"
          className={`gap-1.5 text-muted-foreground ${className ?? ''}`}
        >
          <Flag className="h-3.5 w-3.5" aria-hidden="true" />
          Report
        </Button>
      </DialogTrigger>
      <DialogContent className="max-h-[90vh] overflow-y-auto">
        {submitted ? (
          <>
            <DialogHeader>
              <DialogTitle>Report submitted</DialogTitle>
              <DialogDescription>
                Thanks. A maintainer will review it. We do not share reporter identities
                with the person you reported.
              </DialogDescription>
            </DialogHeader>
            <DialogFooter>
              <Button type="button" onClick={() => setOpen(false)}>
                Close
              </Button>
            </DialogFooter>
          </>
        ) : (
          <form onSubmit={submit} className="space-y-4">
            <DialogHeader>
              <DialogTitle>Report this {targetType}</DialogTitle>
              <DialogDescription>
                Tell us what is wrong. Reports are private and reviewed by a maintainer.
              </DialogDescription>
            </DialogHeader>

            <div className="space-y-2">
              <Label htmlFor="report-reason">Reason</Label>
              <select
                id="report-reason"
                value={reason}
                onChange={(event) => setReason(event.target.value)}
                className="flex h-10 w-full rounded-md border border-input bg-background px-3 py-2 text-sm ring-offset-background focus-visible:outline-none focus-visible:ring-2 focus-visible:ring-ring focus-visible:ring-offset-2"
              >
                {REASONS.map((item) => (
                  <option key={item.value} value={item.value}>
                    {item.label}
                  </option>
                ))}
              </select>
            </div>

            <div className="space-y-2">
              <Label htmlFor="report-details">Details (optional)</Label>
              <Textarea
                id="report-details"
                value={details}
                onChange={(event) => setDetails(event.target.value)}
                placeholder="Add context that helps us review this quickly."
                maxLength={1000}
                rows={4}
              />
              <p className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">{details.length} / 1000</p>
            </div>

            {error && (
              <p role="alert" className="text-sm text-destructive">
                {error}
              </p>
            )}

            <DialogFooter>
              <Button type="button" variant="outline" onClick={() => setOpen(false)}>
                Cancel
              </Button>
              <Button type="submit" disabled={busy}>
                {busy && <Loader2 className="mr-2 h-4 w-4 animate-spin" aria-hidden="true" />}
                Submit report
              </Button>
            </DialogFooter>
          </form>
        )}
      </DialogContent>
    </Dialog>
  )
}


===== FILE: src/components/community/showcase-client.tsx =====
'use client'


import { useEffect, useState } from 'react'
import dynamic from 'next/dynamic'
import Link from 'next/link'
import { useSession } from 'next-auth/react'
import { Loader2, Plus, Rocket, Search, Share2 } from 'lucide-react'
import { Button } from '@/components/ui/button'
import { Input } from '@/components/ui/input'
import { ProjectCard } from '@/components/community/project-card'
import { Reveal } from '@/components/motion/reveal'
import type { ProjectListItem } from '@/lib/community-types'
import { tracks } from '@/lib/curriculum'

const ProjectFormDialog = dynamic(
  () =>
    import('@/components/community/project-form-dialog').then(
      (mod) => mod.ProjectFormDialog
    ),
  { ssr: false }
)

type TrackFilter = 'all' | string
type SortFilter = 'newest' | 'popular'

export function ShowcaseClient() {
  const { status } = useSession()
  const [projects, setProjects] = useState<ProjectListItem[]>([])
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true)
  const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null)
  const [track, setTrack] = useState<TrackFilter>('all')
  const [sort, setSort] = useState<SortFilter>('newest')
  const [query, setQuery] = useState('')
  const [debouncedQuery, setDebouncedQuery] = useState('')
  const [nextCursor, setNextCursor] = useState<string | null>(null)
  const [loadingMore, setLoadingMore] = useState(false)
  const [loadMoreError, setLoadMoreError] = useState<string | null>(null)
  const [shareOpen, setShareOpen] = useState(false)
  const [reloadKey, setReloadKey] = useState(0)

  useEffect(() => {
    const timer = setTimeout(() => setDebouncedQuery(query.trim()), 350)
    return () => clearTimeout(timer)
  }, [query])

  useEffect(() => {
    const controller = new AbortController()

    async function load() {
      setLoading(true)
      setError(null)
      setLoadMoreError(null)
      try {
        const params = new URLSearchParams({ limit: '12', sort })
        if (track !== 'all') params.set('track', track)
        if (debouncedQuery) params.set('q', debouncedQuery)

        const response = await fetch(`/api/community/projects?${params.toString()}`, {
          signal: controller.signal,
        })
        const body = await response.json().catch(() => null)
        if (!response.ok) {
          throw new Error(body?.error?.message ?? 'Could not load projects.')
        }
        setProjects(body.data as ProjectListItem[])
        setNextCursor(body.nextCursor ?? null)
      } catch (err) {
        if ((err as Error).name === 'AbortError') return
        setError(err instanceof Error ? err.message : 'Could not load projects.')
        setProjects([])
        setNextCursor(null)
      } finally {
        if (!controller.signal.aborted) setLoading(false)
      }
    }

    load()
    return () => controller.abort()
  }, [track, sort, debouncedQuery, reloadKey])

  async function loadMore() {
    if (!nextCursor || loadingMore) return
    setLoadingMore(true)
    try {
      const params = new URLSearchParams({ limit: '12', sort, cursor: nextCursor })
      if (track !== 'all') params.set('track', track)
      if (debouncedQuery) params.set('q', debouncedQuery)
      const response = await fetch(`/api/community/projects?${params.toString()}`)
      const body = await response.json().catch(() => null)
      if (!response.ok) throw new Error(body?.error?.message ?? 'Could not load more projects.')
      setProjects((current) => [...current, ...(body.data as ProjectListItem[])])
      setNextCursor(body.nextCursor ?? null)
    } catch (err) {
      setLoadMoreError(err instanceof Error ? err.message : 'Could not load more projects.')
    } finally {
      setLoadingMore(false)
    }
  }

  return (
    <div className="space-y-6">
      <div className="flex flex-col gap-4 lg:flex-row lg:items-center lg:justify-between">
        <div className="flex flex-col gap-3 sm:flex-row sm:items-center">
          <div className="relative">
            <Search className="pointer-events-none absolute left-3 top-1/2 h-4 w-4 -translate-y-1/2 text-muted-foreground" aria-hidden="true" />
            <Input
              value={query}
              onChange={(event) => setQuery(event.target.value)}
              placeholder="Search projects..."
              className="w-full pl-9 sm:w-64"
              aria-label="Search projects"
            />
          </div>

          <select
            value={track}
            onChange={(event) => setTrack(event.target.value)}
            aria-label="Filter by track"
            className="h-9 rounded-md border border-input bg-transparent px-3 text-sm shadow-xs outline-none focus-visible:border-ring focus-visible:ring-ring/50 focus-visible:ring-[3px]"
          >
            <option value="all">All tracks</option>
            {tracks.map((entry) => (
              <option key={entry.key} value={entry.key}>
                {entry.name}
              </option>
            ))}
          </select>

          <select
            value={sort}
            onChange={(event) => setSort(event.target.value as SortFilter)}
            aria-label="Sort projects"
            className="h-9 rounded-md border border-input bg-transparent px-3 text-sm shadow-xs outline-none focus-visible:border-ring focus-visible:ring-ring/50 focus-visible:ring-[3px]"
          >
            <option value="newest">Newest first</option>
            <option value="popular">Most upvoted</option>
          </select>
        </div>

        {status === 'authenticated' ? (
          <Button onClick={() => setShareOpen(true)} className="gap-2">
            <Share2 className="h-4 w-4" aria-hidden="true" />
            Share your project
          </Button>
        ) : (
          <Button asChild variant="outline" className="gap-2">
            <Link href="/auth/signin?callbackUrl=/community/showcase">
              <Plus className="h-4 w-4" aria-hidden="true" />
              Sign in to share
            </Link>
          </Button>
        )}
      </div>

      {loading ? (
        <div className="grid grid-cols-1 gap-5 sm:grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-3" aria-busy="true" aria-label="Loading projects">
          {Array.from({ length: 6 }).map((_, index) => (
            <div key={index} className="h-80 animate-pulse rounded-xl border border-border/50 bg-muted/40" />
          ))}
        </div>
      ) : error ? (
        <div className="rounded-xl border border-destructive/30 bg-destructive/5 p-8 text-center">
          <p className="font-semibold">The showcase is taking a break</p>
          <p className="mt-1 text-sm text-muted-foreground">{error}</p>
          <Button variant="outline" className="mt-4" onClick={() => setReloadKey((key) => key + 1)}>
            Try again
          </Button>
        </div>
      ) : projects.length === 0 ? (
        <div className="rounded-xl border border-dashed border-border/60 bg-muted/20 p-12 text-center">
          <Rocket className="mx-auto h-10 w-10 text-muted-foreground/50" aria-hidden="true" />
          <h3 className="mt-4 text-lg font-semibold">
            {debouncedQuery || track !== 'all' ? 'No projects match yet' : 'Be the first to share'}
          </h3>
          <p className="mx-auto mt-2 max-w-md text-sm text-muted-foreground">
            {debouncedQuery || track !== 'all'
              ? 'Try a different search or track — or add the project that is missing.'
              : 'Built something with the MERN stack? A course project, a side app, a tool you use — share it and get feedback.'}
          </p>
          {status === 'authenticated' ? (
            <Button className="mt-5 gap-2" onClick={() => setShareOpen(true)}>
              <Share2 className="h-4 w-4" aria-hidden="true" />
              Share your project
            </Button>
          ) : (
            <Button asChild className="mt-5">
              <Link href="/auth/signin?callbackUrl=/community/showcase">Sign in to share</Link>
            </Button>
          )}
        </div>
      ) : (
        <>
          <p className="text-sm text-muted-foreground" aria-live="polite">
            {projects.length} project{projects.length === 1 ? '' : 's'}
            {debouncedQuery ? ` matching “${debouncedQuery}”` : ''}
          </p>
          <div className="grid grid-cols-1 gap-5 sm:grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-3">
            {projects.map((project, index) => (
              <Reveal key={project.id} className="h-full" delay={Math.min(index * 0.035, 0.2)} distance={12}>
                <ProjectCard project={project} />
              </Reveal>
            ))}
          </div>
          {nextCursor && (
            <div className="flex justify-center pt-2">
              <Button variant="outline" onClick={loadMore} disabled={loadingMore} className="gap-2">
                {loadingMore && <Loader2 className="h-4 w-4 animate-spin" aria-hidden="true" />}
                {loadingMore ? 'Loading...' : 'Load more projects'}
              </Button>
            </div>
          )}
          {loadMoreError && (
            <p className="text-center text-sm text-destructive" role="alert">
              {loadMoreError}
            </p>
          )}
        </>
      )}

      {shareOpen && (
        <ProjectFormDialog
          open
          onOpenChange={setShareOpen}
          onSaved={(project) => {
            setProjects((current) => [project, ...current])
            setTrack('all')
            setQuery('')
          }}
        />
      )}
    </div>
  )
}


===== FILE: src/components/community/upvote-button.tsx =====
'use client'

import { useState } from 'react'
import { useRouter } from 'next/navigation'
import { useSession } from 'next-auth/react'
import { ArrowUp } from 'lucide-react'
import { cn } from '@/lib/utils'

interface UpvoteButtonProps {
  projectId: string
  initialUpvotes: number
  initialUpvoted: boolean
  size?: 'sm' | 'lg'
  className?: string
}

export function UpvoteButton({
  projectId,
  initialUpvotes,
  initialUpvoted,
  size = 'sm',
  className,
}: UpvoteButtonProps) {
  const { status } = useSession()
  const router = useRouter()
  const [upvoted, setUpvoted] = useState(initialUpvoted)
  const [upvotes, setUpvotes] = useState(initialUpvotes)
  const [pending, setPending] = useState(false)
  const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null)

  async function handleClick() {
    if (status !== 'authenticated') {
      router.push(`/auth/signin?callbackUrl=${encodeURIComponent(window.location.pathname)}`)
      return
    }
    if (pending) return

    const previous = { upvoted, upvotes }
    setUpvoted(!upvoted)
    setUpvotes(upvotes + (upvoted ? -1 : 1))
    setPending(true)
    setError(null)

    try {
      const response = await fetch(`/api/community/projects/${projectId}/upvote`, {
        method: 'POST',
      })
      if (!response.ok) {
        const body = await response.json().catch(() => null)
        throw new Error(body?.error?.message ?? 'Could not save your upvote.')
      }
      const body = await response.json()
      setUpvoted(body.data.upvoted)
      setUpvotes(body.data.upvotes)
    } catch (err) {
      setUpvoted(previous.upvoted)
      setUpvotes(previous.upvotes)
      setError(err instanceof Error ? err.message : 'Could not save your upvote.')
    } finally {
      setPending(false)
    }
  }

  return (
    <div className={cn('flex flex-col items-start', className)}>
      <button
        type="button"
        onClick={handleClick}
        disabled={pending}
        aria-pressed={upvoted}
        aria-label={upvoted ? 'Remove upvote' : 'Upvote this project'}
        title={error ?? undefined}
        className={cn(
          'inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 rounded-lg border font-semibold transition-colors',
          size === 'lg' ? 'px-3 py-2 text-sm' : 'px-2.5 py-1.5 text-xs',
          upvoted
            ? 'border-emerald-500/40 bg-emerald-500/10 text-emerald-600 dark:text-emerald-400'
            : 'border-border/60 bg-background text-muted-foreground hover:border-emerald-500/40 hover:text-emerald-600',
          pending && 'opacity-70'
        )}
      >
        <ArrowUp className={cn(size === 'lg' ? 'h-4 w-4' : 'h-3.5 w-3.5', pending && 'animate-pulse')} aria-hidden="true" />
        {upvotes}
      </button>
      {error && <span className="mt-1 text-xs text-destructive sm:text-[11px]">{error}</span>}
    </div>
  )
}

import Link from 'next/link'

===== FILE: src/components/contact-form.tsx =====
'use client'

import { useId, useState } from 'react'
import Link from 'next/link'
import { CheckCircle2, Loader2, Send } from 'lucide-react'
import { Button } from '@/components/ui/button'
import { Input } from '@/components/ui/input'
import { Label } from '@/components/ui/label'
import { Textarea } from '@/components/ui/textarea'

const topics = [
  { value: 'general', label: 'General question' },
  { value: 'bug', label: 'Bug or broken page' },
  { value: 'content', label: 'Lesson correction' },
  { value: 'conduct', label: 'Code of conduct report' },
  { value: 'privacy', label: 'Privacy or account request' },
  { value: 'security', label: 'Security concern' },
]

export function ContactForm() {
  const formId = useId()
  const emailId = `${formId}-email`
  const nameId = `${formId}-name`
  const topicId = `${formId}-topic`
  const messageId = `${formId}-message`
  const websiteId = `${formId}-website`

  const [form, setForm] = useState({
    name: '',
    email: '',
    topic: 'general',
    message: '',
    website: '',
  })
  const [status, setStatus] = useState<'idle' | 'loading' | 'success' | 'error'>('idle')
  const [error, setError] = useState('')

  function update<K extends keyof typeof form>(key: K, value: string) {
    setForm((current) => ({ ...current, [key]: value }))
    if (status === 'error') setStatus('idle')
  }

  async function handleSubmit(event: React.FormEvent<HTMLFormElement>) {
    event.preventDefault()
    if (status === 'loading') return
    setStatus('loading')
    setError('')

    try {
      const response = await fetch('/api/contact', {
        method: 'POST',
        headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
        body: JSON.stringify(form),
      })
      const payload = await response.json().catch(() => null)
      if (!response.ok) {
        throw new Error(payload?.error?.message ?? 'Could not send your message. Try again.')
      }
      setStatus('success')
      window.scrollTo({ top: 0 })
    } catch (err) {
      setStatus('error')
      setError(err instanceof Error ? err.message : 'Could not send your message. Try again.')
    }
  }

  if (status === 'success') {
    return (
      <div className="rounded-xl border border-emerald-500/30 bg-emerald-500/5 p-6" role="status">
        <CheckCircle2 className="h-8 w-8 text-emerald-500" aria-hidden="true" />
        <h2 className="mt-3 text-lg font-semibold">Message received</h2>
        <p className="mt-2 text-sm text-muted-foreground">
          Thanks for taking the time. Messages are read by the maintainers, best effort within a
          week. If you reported a security issue, please also use GitHub&apos;s private reporting
          so it does not sit in an inbox.
        </p>
        <Button asChild variant="outline" className="mt-4">
          <Link href="/">Back to the lessons</Link>
        </Button>
      </div>
    )
  }

  return (
    <form onSubmit={handleSubmit} className="space-y-5">
      <div className="grid grid-cols-1 gap-4 sm:grid-cols-2">
        <div className="space-y-1.5">
          <Label htmlFor={nameId}>Name (optional)</Label>
          <Input
            id={nameId}
            value={form.name}
            onChange={(event) => update('name', event.target.value)}
            autoComplete="name"
            maxLength={80}
            placeholder="Ada Lovelace"
          />
        </div>
        <div className="space-y-1.5">
          <Label htmlFor={emailId}>Email</Label>
          <Input
            id={emailId}
            type="email"
            required
            value={form.email}
            onChange={(event) => update('email', event.target.value)}
            autoComplete="email"
            maxLength={254}
            placeholder="you@example.com"
          />
        </div>
      </div>

      <div className="space-y-1.5">
        <Label htmlFor={topicId}>Topic</Label>
        <select
          id={topicId}
          value={form.topic}
          onChange={(event) => update('topic', event.target.value)}
          className="flex h-9 w-full rounded-md border border-input bg-transparent px-3 py-1 text-sm shadow-xs outline-none focus-visible:border-ring focus-visible:ring-ring/50 focus-visible:ring-[3px]"
        >
          {topics.map((topic) => (
            <option key={topic.value} value={topic.value}>
              {topic.label}
            </option>
          ))}
        </select>
      </div>

      <div className="space-y-1.5">
        <Label htmlFor={messageId}>Message</Label>
        <Textarea
          id={messageId}
          required
          minLength={20}
          maxLength={4000}
          rows={7}
          value={form.message}
          onChange={(event) => update('message', event.target.value)}
          placeholder="Include the page, what you expected, and what happened. Do not paste passwords or API keys."
        />
        <p className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">
          {form.message.trim().length} / 4000 characters (minimum 20)
        </p>
      </div>

      <div className="hidden" aria-hidden="true">
        <label htmlFor={websiteId}>Website</label>
        <input
          id={websiteId}
          name="website"
          type="text"
          tabIndex={-1}
          autoComplete="off"
          value={form.website}
          onChange={(event) => update('website', event.target.value)}
        />
      </div>

      {status === 'error' && (
        <p className="rounded-lg border border-destructive/30 bg-destructive/5 px-3 py-2 text-sm text-destructive" role="alert">
          {error}
        </p>
      )}

      <div className="space-y-3">
        <Button type="submit" disabled={status === 'loading'} className="gap-2">
          {status === 'loading' ? (
            <Loader2 className="h-4 w-4 animate-spin" aria-hidden="true" />
          ) : (
            <Send className="h-4 w-4" aria-hidden="true" />
          )}
          {status === 'loading' ? 'Sending...' : 'Send message'}
        </Button>
        <p className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">
          Your message is stored privately so the maintainers can reply. See the{' '}
          <Link href="/privacy" className="underline underline-offset-2 hover:text-foreground">
            privacy policy
          </Link>
          .
        </p>
      </div>
    </form>
  )
}


===== FILE: src/components/dashboard-client.tsx =====
'use client'

import Link from 'next/link'
import { useState } from 'react'
import { useSession } from 'next-auth/react'
import { useProgress } from '@/hooks/use-progress'
import { useCommunityStats } from '@/hooks/use-community-stats'
import { tracks, type Lesson } from '@/lib/curriculum'
import {
  badgeDefinitions,
  getEarnedBadges,
  sortBadgesByEarned,
  TOTAL_LESSON_COUNT,
} from '@/lib/badges'
import { BadgeGrid } from '@/components/badge-grid'
import { Card, CardContent, CardHeader, CardTitle } from '@/components/ui/card'
import { Button } from '@/components/ui/button'
import { ConfirmDialog } from '@/components/ui/confirm-dialog'
import { Progress } from '@/components/ui/progress'
import { Badge } from '@/components/ui/badge'
import { Separator } from '@/components/ui/separator'
import {
  Trophy, Zap, Flame, CheckCircle2, ArrowRight,
  Bookmark, RotateCcw, Play, Cloud, CloudOff,
} from 'lucide-react'
import { calculateLevel, cn } from '@/lib/utils'

const DASHBOARD_BADGE_PREVIEW = 9

export function DashboardClient() {
  const { state, isLoaded, xp, streak, resetProgress, bookmarks } = useProgress()
  const { data: session } = useSession()
  const { stats: community } = useCommunityStats()
  const [resetConfirmOpen, setResetConfirmOpen] = useState(false)

  if (!isLoaded) {
    return (
      <div className="grid grid-cols-1 gap-6 md:grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-4">
        {Array.from({ length: 4 }).map((_, i) => (
          <Card key={i}>
            <CardContent className="pt-6">
              <div className="h-20 animate-pulse rounded bg-muted" />
            </CardContent>
          </Card>
        ))}
      </div>
    )
  }

  const completedLessons = Object.values(state.lessons).filter((l) => l.completed).length
  const totalLessons = TOTAL_LESSON_COUNT
  const { level, xpForNext, progress: levelProgress } = calculateLevel(xp)
  const earnedBadges = getEarnedBadges({ state, ...community })
  const earnedBadgeKeys = new Set(earnedBadges.map((badge) => badge.key))
  const earnedPreview = sortBadgesByEarned(
    badgeDefinitions.filter((badge) => earnedBadgeKeys.has(badge.key)),
    earnedBadgeKeys
  )
  const nextBadges = badgeDefinitions.filter((badge) => !earnedBadgeKeys.has(badge.key))
  const previewBadges = [...earnedPreview, ...nextBadges].slice(0, DASHBOARD_BADGE_PREVIEW)

  // Find continue learning target
  let continueTarget: { track: string; module: string; lesson: string; title: string } | null = null
  if (state.lastVisited) {
    const { track: tKey, module: mKey, lesson: lId } = state.lastVisited
    const track = tracks.find((t) => t.key === tKey)
    const mod = track?.modules.find((m) => m.key === mKey)
    const lesson = mod?.lessons.find((l) => l.id === lId)
    if (track && mod && lesson) {
      continueTarget = { track: tKey, module: mKey, lesson: lId, title: lesson.title }
    }
  }

  // Per-track stats
  const trackStats = tracks.map((track) => {
    const lessons = track.modules.flatMap((m) => m.lessons)
    const done = lessons.filter((l) => state.lessons[l.id]?.completed).length
    const percentage = lessons.length ? Math.round((done / lessons.length) * 100) : 0
    return { track, done, total: lessons.length, percentage }
  })

  const bookmarkedLessons = bookmarks
    .map((id) => {
      for (const track of tracks) {
        for (const m of track.modules) {
          const lesson = m.lessons.find((l) => l.id === id)
          if (lesson) return { track: track.key, module: m.key, lesson }
        }
      }
      return null
    })
    .filter(Boolean) as { track: string; module: string; lesson: Lesson }[]

  return (
    <div className="space-y-10">
      {/* Sync status */}
      <div
        className={cn(
          'flex flex-col gap-3 rounded-xl border p-4 sm:flex-row sm:items-center sm:justify-between',
          session
            ? 'border-emerald-500/20 bg-emerald-500/5'
            : 'border-border bg-muted/40'
        )}
      >
        <div className="flex items-start gap-3">
          {session ? (
            <Cloud className="mt-0.5 h-5 w-5 shrink-0 text-emerald-500" aria-hidden="true" />
          ) : (
            <CloudOff className="mt-0.5 h-5 w-5 shrink-0 text-muted-foreground" aria-hidden="true" />
          )}
          <div>
            <p className="text-sm font-medium">
              {session
                ? `Synced to ${session.user?.email}`
                : 'Saved in this browser only'}
            </p>
            <p className="text-sm text-muted-foreground">
              {session
                ? 'Your progress, XP, streaks, notes, and bookmarks follow you to any device.'
                : 'Sign in to sync your progress across devices — everything you have now will be merged into your account.'}
            </p>
          </div>
        </div>
        {!session && (
          <Button asChild variant="outline" className="shrink-0 gap-2">
            <Link href="/auth/signin?callbackUrl=/dashboard">Sign in to sync</Link>
          </Button>
        )}
      </div>

      {/* Stats Grid */}
      <div className="grid grid-cols-1 gap-6 sm:grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-4">
        <Card>
          <CardContent className="pt-6">
            <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
              <div>
                <p className="text-sm text-muted-foreground">Total XP</p>
                <p className="text-3xl font-bold mt-1">{xp}</p>
              </div>
              <div className="flex h-12 w-12 items-center justify-center rounded-xl bg-amber-500/15 text-amber-500">
                <Zap className="h-6 w-6" aria-hidden="true" />
              </div>
            </div>
            <div className="mt-4">
              <div className="flex items-center justify-between text-xs text-muted-foreground mb-1.5">
                <span>Level {level}</span>
                <span>{xpForNext - xp} XP to Level {level + 1}</span>
              </div>
              <Progress value={levelProgress} className="h-2" />
            </div>
          </CardContent>
        </Card>

        <Card>
          <CardContent className="pt-6">
            <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
              <div>
                <p className="text-sm text-muted-foreground">Lessons Completed</p>
                <p className="text-3xl font-bold mt-1">
                  {completedLessons}
                  <span className="text-lg text-muted-foreground font-normal">/{totalLessons}</span>
                </p>
              </div>
              <div className="flex h-12 w-12 items-center justify-center rounded-xl bg-emerald-500/15 text-emerald-500">
                <CheckCircle2 className="h-6 w-6" aria-hidden="true" />
              </div>
            </div>
            <p className="mt-4 text-xs text-muted-foreground">
              {totalLessons ? Math.round((completedLessons / totalLessons) * 100) : 0}% of all content
            </p>
          </CardContent>
        </Card>

        <Card>
          <CardContent className="pt-6">
            <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
              <div>
                <p className="text-sm text-muted-foreground">Current Streak</p>
                <p className="text-3xl font-bold mt-1">
                  {streak.current}
                  <span className="text-lg text-muted-foreground font-normal"> days</span>
                </p>
              </div>
              <div className="flex h-12 w-12 items-center justify-center rounded-xl bg-rose-500/15 text-rose-500">
                <Flame className="h-6 w-6" aria-hidden="true" />
              </div>
            </div>
            <p className="mt-4 text-xs text-muted-foreground">
              Longest streak: {streak.longest} {streak.longest === 1 ? 'day' : 'days'}
            </p>
          </CardContent>
        </Card>

        <Card>
          <CardContent className="pt-6">
            <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
              <div>
                <p className="text-sm text-muted-foreground">Badges Earned</p>
                <p className="text-3xl font-bold mt-1">
                  {earnedBadges.length}
                  <span className="text-lg text-muted-foreground font-normal">/{badgeDefinitions.length}</span>
                </p>
              </div>
              <div className="flex h-12 w-12 items-center justify-center rounded-xl bg-violet-500/15 text-violet-500">
                <Trophy className="h-6 w-6" aria-hidden="true" />
              </div>
            </div>
            <p className="mt-4 text-xs text-muted-foreground">
              {badgeDefinitions.length - earnedBadges.length} more to unlock
            </p>
          </CardContent>
        </Card>
      </div>

      {/* Continue Learning */}
      {continueTarget && (
        <Card className="border-emerald-500/20 bg-gradient-to-br from-emerald-500/5 to-violet-500/5">
          <CardContent className="pt-6">
            <div className="flex flex-col gap-4 sm:flex-row sm:items-center sm:justify-between">
              <div className="flex items-center gap-4">
                <div className="flex h-12 w-12 flex-shrink-0 items-center justify-center rounded-xl bg-emerald-500/15 text-emerald-500">
                  <Play className="h-6 w-6" aria-hidden="true" />
                </div>
                <div>
                  <p className="text-sm text-muted-foreground">Continue where you left off</p>
                  <p className="font-semibold">{continueTarget.title}</p>
                </div>
              </div>
              <Button asChild className="flex-shrink-0">
                <Link href={`/learn/${continueTarget.track}/${continueTarget.module}/${continueTarget.lesson}`}>
                  Resume <ArrowRight className="ml-2 h-4 w-4" aria-hidden="true" />
                </Link>
              </Button>
            </div>
          </CardContent>
        </Card>
      )}

      {/* Track Progress */}
      <div>
        <h2 className="text-2xl font-bold tracking-tight mb-4">Track Progress</h2>
        <div className="grid grid-cols-1 gap-6 md:grid-cols-2">
          {trackStats.map(({ track, done, total, percentage }) => (
            <Card key={track.key}>
              <CardHeader>
                <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
                  <CardTitle className="text-lg">{track.name}</CardTitle>
                  <Badge variant="outline">{percentage}%</Badge>
                </div>
              </CardHeader>
              <CardContent className="space-y-4">
                <Progress value={percentage} className="h-2" />
                <div className="flex items-center justify-between text-sm text-muted-foreground">
                  <span>{done} of {total} lessons completed</span>
                  <Link
                    href={`/learn/${track.key}`}
                    className="inline-flex items-center gap-1 font-medium text-foreground hover:text-primary transition-colors"
                  >
                    View track <ArrowRight className="h-3.5 w-3.5" aria-hidden="true" />
                  </Link>
                </div>
              </CardContent>
            </Card>
          ))}
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* Badges */}
      <div>
        <div className="mb-4 flex flex-wrap items-end justify-between gap-3">
          <div>
            <h2 className="text-2xl font-bold tracking-tight">Badges</h2>
            <p className="mt-1 text-sm text-muted-foreground">
              {earnedBadges.length} of {badgeDefinitions.length} unlocked. Showing {DASHBOARD_BADGE_PREVIEW}.
            </p>
          </div>
          <Button asChild variant="outline" size="sm" className="gap-2">
            <Link href="/badges">
              See all badges
              <ArrowRight className="h-4 w-4" aria-hidden="true" />
            </Link>
          </Button>
        </div>
        <BadgeGrid badges={previewBadges} earnedKeys={earnedBadgeKeys} />
      </div>

      {/* Bookmarks */}
      {bookmarkedLessons.length > 0 && (
        <div>
          <h2 className="text-2xl font-bold tracking-tight mb-4 flex items-center gap-2">
            <Bookmark className="h-5 w-5 text-amber-500" aria-hidden="true" />
            Bookmarked Lessons
          </h2>
          <div className="space-y-2">
            {bookmarkedLessons.map(({ track, module, lesson }) => (
              <Link
                key={lesson.id}
                href={`/learn/${track}/${module}/${lesson.id}`}
                className="flex items-center justify-between rounded-lg border border-border/50 bg-card p-4 hover:border-border hover:shadow-sm transition-all group"
              >
                <div className="flex items-center gap-3 min-w-0">
                  <Bookmark className="h-4 w-4 text-amber-500 flex-shrink-0" aria-hidden="true" />
                  <div className="min-w-0">
                    <p className="font-medium text-sm truncate group-hover:text-primary transition-colors">{lesson.title}</p>
                    <p className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">{track} · {lesson.estimatedMinutes} min</p>
                  </div>
                </div>
                <ArrowRight className="h-4 w-4 text-muted-foreground flex-shrink-0 group-hover:translate-x-0.5 transition-transform" aria-hidden="true" />
              </Link>
            ))}
          </div>
        </div>
      )}

      {/* Reset */}
      <Separator />
      <div data-print-hide className="flex flex-col items-start gap-3 sm:flex-row sm:items-center sm:justify-between">
        <div>
          <p className="font-medium text-sm">Reset progress</p>
          <p className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">
            This clears lesson completion, XP, streaks, bookmarks, and notes. Signed-in resets also
            sync to your account. Cannot be undone.
          </p>
        </div>
        <Button
          variant="outline"
          size="sm"
          className="text-destructive hover:text-destructive"
          onClick={() => setResetConfirmOpen(true)}
        >
          <RotateCcw className="mr-2 h-4 w-4" aria-hidden="true" />
          Reset Progress
        </Button>
      </div>

      <ConfirmDialog
        open={resetConfirmOpen}
        onOpenChange={setResetConfirmOpen}
        title="Reset all progress?"
        description="This clears lesson completion, XP, streaks, bookmarks, and notes. If you are signed in, the reset also syncs to your account. It cannot be undone."
        confirmLabel="Reset progress"
        destructive
        onConfirm={() => {
          resetProgress()
          setResetConfirmOpen(false)
        }}
      />
    </div>
  )
}


===== FILE: src/components/faq/faq-hash-opener.tsx =====
'use client'

import { useEffect } from 'react'

/**
 * Search results link to individual FAQ answers with a hash. Native
 * <details> elements do not open just because they are the target of a hash,
 * so open the matching item before scrolling to it.
 */
export function FaqHashOpener() {
  useEffect(() => {
    function openFromHash() {
      const raw = window.location.hash.slice(1)
      if (!raw) return

      let id = raw
      try {
        id = decodeURIComponent(raw)
      } catch {
        // Keep the raw fragment if it is not valid percent-encoding.
      }

      const target = document.getElementById(id)
      if (target instanceof HTMLDetailsElement) {
        target.open = true
        target.scrollIntoView({ block: 'start' })
      }
    }

    openFromHash()
    window.addEventListener('hashchange', openFromHash)
    return () => window.removeEventListener('hashchange', openFromHash)
  }, [])

  return null
}
