===== FILE: src/components/header.tsx =====
'use client'

import { useEffect, useState } from 'react'
import Link from 'next/link'
import Image from 'next/image'
import { usePathname } from 'next/navigation'
import { useSession, signOut } from 'next-auth/react'
import { useTheme } from 'next-themes'
import { AnimatePresence, motion, useReducedMotion, useScroll, useSpring } from 'motion/react'
import { Menu, X, Sun, Moon, User, LogOut, BookOpen, Trophy, Users, Code2, Rocket, Search, Layers, ChevronDown, CircleUser, ShieldCheck, Flag, Award } from 'lucide-react'
import { Button } from '@/components/ui/button'
import { Avatar, AvatarFallback, AvatarImage } from '@/components/ui/avatar'
import { NotificationBell } from '@/components/notification-bell'
import { DropdownMenu, DropdownMenuContent, DropdownMenuItem, DropdownMenuLabel, DropdownMenuSeparator, DropdownMenuTrigger } from '@/components/ui/dropdown-menu'
import { Tooltip, TooltipContent, TooltipProvider, TooltipTrigger } from '@/components/ui/tooltip'
import { cn } from '@/lib/utils'
import { trackCategories } from '@/lib/curriculum'
import { Globe, Code2 as Code2Icon, Cpu, Smartphone, Brain, Cloud, Gamepad2, Monitor } from 'lucide-react'

const categoryIcons: Record<string, typeof Globe> = {
  'web-development': Globe,
  'programming-languages': Code2Icon,
  'embedded-systems': Cpu,
  'mobile-development': Smartphone,
  'data-science': Brain,
  'devops-cloud': Cloud,
  'game-development': Gamepad2,
  'desktop-apps': Monitor,
}

const navigation = [
  { name: 'Learn', href: '/learn', icon: BookOpen },
  { name: 'Categories', href: '/categories', icon: Layers },
  { name: 'Progress', href: '/dashboard', icon: Trophy, auth: true },
  { name: 'Showcase', href: '/community/showcase', icon: Rocket },
  { name: 'Community', href: '/community', icon: Users },
  { name: 'Playground', href: '/playground', icon: Code2 },
]

export function Header() {
  const { data: session, status } = useSession()
  const { setTheme } = useTheme()
  const pathname = usePathname()
  const [mobileMenuOpen, setMobileMenuOpen] = useState(false)
  const [isDark, setIsDark] = useState(false)
  const reduceMotion = useReducedMotion()
  const { scrollYProgress } = useScroll()
  const progress = useSpring(scrollYProgress, { stiffness: 140, damping: 30, mass: 0.2 })

  // next-themes owns the class on <html>; mirror it so the toggle exposes an
  // accurate pressed state for assistive technology.
  useEffect(() => {
    const root = document.documentElement
    const sync = () => setIsDark(root.classList.contains('dark'))
    sync()
    const observer = new MutationObserver(sync)
    observer.observe(root, { attributes: true, attributeFilter: ['class'] })
    return () => observer.disconnect()
  }, [])

  const activeNavigationHref = navigation
    .filter((item) => pathname === item.href || pathname.startsWith(`${item.href}/`))
    .sort((a, b) => b.href.length - a.href.length)[0]?.href

  return (
    <header
      data-site-header
      data-print-hide
      className="sticky top-0 z-50 w-full border-b border-border/50 bg-background/95 backdrop-blur supports-[backdrop-filter]:bg-background/60"
    >
      <nav className="mx-auto flex max-w-7xl items-center justify-between px-4 py-4 sm:px-6 lg:px-8" aria-label="Main navigation">
        <div className="flex items-center gap-8">
          <Link href="/" className="flex shrink-0 items-center gap-2" aria-label="Nyxora Sync Home">
            <Image
              src="/site-mark.webp"
              alt=""
              width={36}
              height={36}
              className="h-9 w-9 rounded-lg object-cover transition-transform duration-300 hover:scale-[1.03]"
              priority
            />
            <span className="hidden truncate font-bold text-xl font-sans sm:inline">Nyxora Sync</span>
          </Link>

          <div className="hidden lg:flex lg:items-center lg:gap-6">
            {navigation.map((item) => {
              // Categories gets a dropdown menu
              if (item.name === 'Categories') {
                return (
                  <DropdownMenu key={item.name}>
                    <DropdownMenuTrigger asChild>
                      <Link
                        href={item.href}
                        className={cn(
                          `relative flex items-center gap-1.5 text-sm font-medium transition-colors after:absolute after:-bottom-1 after:left-0 after:h-px after:w-full after:origin-left after:scale-x-0 after:bg-primary after:transition-transform after:duration-200 after:content-[''] hover:text-primary hover:after:scale-x-100`,
                          session || !item.auth ? 'text-foreground' : 'text-muted-foreground'
                        )}
                        aria-current={activeNavigationHref === item.href ? 'page' : undefined}
                      >
                        <item.icon className="h-4 w-4" aria-hidden="true" />
                        {item.name}
                        <ChevronDown className="h-3.5 w-3.5 ml-0.5" aria-hidden="true" />
                      </Link>
                    </DropdownMenuTrigger>
                    <DropdownMenuContent className="w-72" align="start" forceMount>
                      <DropdownMenuLabel className="font-semibold">Browse by Category</DropdownMenuLabel>
                      {trackCategories
                        .filter((cat) => cat.order <= 8)
                        .map((cat) => {
                          const Icon = categoryIcons[cat.id];
                          return (
                            <DropdownMenuItem key={cat.id} asChild>
                              <Link
                                href={`/categories/${cat.id}`}
                                className="flex items-center gap-2"
                              >
                                <Icon className="h-4 w-4" aria-hidden="true" />
                                {cat.name}
                              </Link>
                            </DropdownMenuItem>
                          )
                        })}
                      <DropdownMenuSeparator />
                      <DropdownMenuItem asChild>
                        <Link href="/categories" className="flex items-center gap-2 font-medium">
                          <Layers className="h-4 w-4" aria-hidden="true" />
                          View All Categories
                        </Link>
                      </DropdownMenuItem>
                    </DropdownMenuContent>
                  </DropdownMenu>
                )
              }
              
              return (
                <Link
                  key={item.name}
                  href={item.href}
                  className={cn(
                    `relative flex items-center gap-1.5 text-sm font-medium transition-colors after:absolute after:-bottom-1 after:left-0 after:h-px after:w-full after:origin-left after:scale-x-0 after:bg-primary after:transition-transform after:duration-200 after:content-[''] hover:text-primary hover:after:scale-x-100`,
                    session || !item.auth ? 'text-foreground' : 'text-muted-foreground'
                  )}
                  aria-current={activeNavigationHref === item.href ? 'page' : undefined}
                >
                  <item.icon className="h-4 w-4" aria-hidden="true" />
                  {item.name}
                </Link>
              )
            })}
          </div>
        </div>

        <div className="flex items-center gap-4">
          <TooltipProvider>
            <Tooltip>
              <TooltipTrigger asChild>
                <Button variant="ghost" size="icon" asChild>
                  <Link href="/search" aria-label="Search the site">
                    <Search className="h-5 w-5" aria-hidden="true" />
                  </Link>
                </Button>
              </TooltipTrigger>
              <TooltipContent side="bottom" align="center">
                Search the site
              </TooltipContent>
            </Tooltip>
            <Tooltip>
              <TooltipTrigger asChild>
                <Button
                  variant="ghost"
                  size="icon"
                  onClick={() => setTheme(isDark ? 'light' : 'dark')}
                  aria-label="Dark mode"
                  aria-pressed={isDark}
                >
                  <Sun className="h-5 w-5 rotate-0 scale-100 transition-all dark:-rotate-90 dark:scale-0" aria-hidden="true" />
                  <Moon className="absolute h-5 w-5 rotate-90 scale-0 transition-all dark:rotate-0 dark:scale-100" aria-hidden="true" />
                </Button>
              </TooltipTrigger>
              <TooltipContent side="bottom" align="center">
                Toggle dark mode
              </TooltipContent>
            </Tooltip>
          </TooltipProvider>

          <NotificationBell />

          {status === 'loading' ? (
            <div className="h-10 w-10 animate-pulse rounded-full bg-muted" aria-hidden="true" />
          ) : session ? (
            <DropdownMenu>
              <DropdownMenuTrigger asChild>
                <Button variant="ghost" className="relative h-10 w-10 rounded-full">
                  <Avatar className="h-10 w-10">
                    <AvatarImage src={session.user?.image || (session.user?.id ? `/api/community/avatar/${session.user.id}` : '')} alt={session.user?.name || ''} />
                    <AvatarFallback>{session.user?.name?.charAt(0) || 'U'}</AvatarFallback>
                  </Avatar>
                </Button>
              </DropdownMenuTrigger>
              <DropdownMenuContent className="w-56" align="end" forceMount>
                <DropdownMenuLabel className="font-normal">
                  <div className="flex flex-col space-y-1">
                    <p className="text-sm font-medium">{session.user?.name}</p>
                    <p className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">{session.user?.email}</p>
                  </div>
                </DropdownMenuLabel>
                <DropdownMenuSeparator />
                <DropdownMenuItem asChild>
                  <Link
                    href={`/community/people/${session.user.id}`}
                    className="flex items-center gap-2"
                  >
                    <CircleUser className="h-4 w-4" aria-hidden="true" />
                    My Profile
                  </Link>
                </DropdownMenuItem>
                <DropdownMenuItem asChild>
                  <Link href="/dashboard" className="flex items-center gap-2">
                    <Trophy className="h-4 w-4" aria-hidden="true" />
                    My Progress
                  </Link>
                </DropdownMenuItem>
                <DropdownMenuItem asChild>
                  <Link href="/certificates" className="flex items-center gap-2">
                    <Award className="h-4 w-4" aria-hidden="true" />
                    Certificates
                  </Link>
                </DropdownMenuItem>
                <DropdownMenuItem asChild>
                  <Link href="/settings" className="flex items-center gap-2">
                    <User className="h-4 w-4" aria-hidden="true" />
                    Settings
                  </Link>
                </DropdownMenuItem>
                {session.user.isAdmin && (
                  <>
                    <DropdownMenuItem asChild>
                      <Link href="/admin/reports" className="flex items-center gap-2">
                        <Flag className="h-4 w-4" aria-hidden="true" />
                        Moderation queue
                      </Link>
                    </DropdownMenuItem>
                    <DropdownMenuItem asChild>
                      <Link href="/admin/messages" className="flex items-center gap-2">
                        <ShieldCheck className="h-4 w-4" aria-hidden="true" />
                        Contact inbox
                      </Link>
                    </DropdownMenuItem>
                  </>
                )}
                <DropdownMenuSeparator />
                <DropdownMenuItem onClick={() => signOut({ callbackUrl: '/' })} className="flex items-center gap-2 text-destructive focus:text-destructive">
                  <LogOut className="h-4 w-4" aria-hidden="true" />
                  Sign out
                </DropdownMenuItem>
              </DropdownMenuContent>
            </DropdownMenu>
          ) : (
            <div className="hidden gap-2 sm:flex">
              <Button variant="ghost" asChild>
                <Link href="/auth/signin">Sign in</Link>
              </Button>
              <Button asChild>
                <Link href="/auth/signin?callbackUrl=/learn">Get Started</Link>
              </Button>
            </div>
          )}

          <Button
            variant="ghost"
            size="icon"
            className="lg:hidden"
            onClick={() => setMobileMenuOpen(!mobileMenuOpen)}
            aria-expanded={mobileMenuOpen}
            aria-controls="mobile-menu"
            aria-label="Toggle menu"
          >
            {mobileMenuOpen ? <X className="h-6 w-6" /> : <Menu className="h-6 w-6" />}
          </Button>
        </div>
      </nav>

      <AnimatePresence initial={false}>
        {mobileMenuOpen && (
          <motion.div
            id="mobile-menu"
            className="overflow-hidden border-t border-border/50 lg:hidden"
            initial={reduceMotion ? false : { height: 0, opacity: 0 }}
            animate={{ height: 'auto', opacity: 1 }}
            exit={reduceMotion ? { opacity: 0 } : { height: 0, opacity: 0 }}
            transition={
              reduceMotion
                ? { duration: 0 }
                : { duration: 0.24, ease: [0.22, 1, 0.36, 1] }
            }
          >
            <div className="mx-auto flex max-w-7xl flex-col items-start gap-4 px-4 py-4">
              <Link
                href="/search"
                className="flex w-full items-center gap-3 rounded-lg px-3 py-2 text-base font-medium text-foreground transition-colors hover:bg-accent"
                onClick={() => setMobileMenuOpen(false)}
              >
                <Search className="h-5 w-5" aria-hidden="true" />
                Search the site
              </Link>
              {navigation.map((item) => {
                // In mobile, Categories just links to /categories page
                if (item.name === 'Categories') {
                  return (
                    <Link
                      key={item.name}
                      href={item.href}
                      className={cn(
                        'flex w-full items-center gap-3 rounded-lg px-3 py-2 text-base font-medium transition-colors hover:bg-accent',
                        session || !item.auth ? 'text-foreground' : 'text-muted-foreground'
                      )}
                      aria-current={activeNavigationHref === item.href ? 'page' : undefined}
                      onClick={() => setMobileMenuOpen(false)}
                    >
                      <item.icon className="h-5 w-5" aria-hidden="true" />
                      {item.name}
                    </Link>
                  )
                }
                return (
                  <Link
                    key={item.name}
                    href={item.href}
                    className={cn(
                      'flex w-full items-center gap-3 rounded-lg px-3 py-2 text-base font-medium transition-colors hover:bg-accent',
                      session || !item.auth ? 'text-foreground' : 'text-muted-foreground'
                    )}
                    aria-current={activeNavigationHref === item.href ? 'page' : undefined}
                    onClick={() => setMobileMenuOpen(false)}
                  >
                    <item.icon className="h-5 w-5" aria-hidden="true" />
                    {item.name}
                  </Link>
                )
              })}
              {!session && (
                <div className="flex w-full flex-col gap-2 pt-4">
                  <Button variant="outline" asChild>
                    <Link href="/auth/signin" onClick={() => setMobileMenuOpen(false)}>Sign in</Link>
                  </Button>
                  <Button asChild>
                    <Link href="/auth/signin?callbackUrl=/learn" onClick={() => setMobileMenuOpen(false)}>Get Started</Link>
                  </Button>
                </div>
              )}
            </div>
          </motion.div>
        )}
      </AnimatePresence>

      <motion.div
        className="absolute inset-x-0 bottom-0 h-0.5 origin-left bg-gradient-to-r from-emerald-500 via-violet-500 to-amber-500"
        style={{ scaleX: progress }}
        aria-hidden="true"
      />
    </header>
  )
}

===== FILE: src/components/home/hero-scene.tsx =====
'use client'

import { useEffect, useRef } from 'react'
import * as THREE from 'three'

const emerald = 0x34d399
const violet = 0x8b5cf6
const amber = 0xf59e0b

/**
 * A deliberately quiet Three.js scene: a wireframe orbital cluster that drifts
 * slowly and leans toward the pointer. It is loaded only on the homepage and
 * pauses when the hero leaves the viewport or the tab is hidden.
 */
export default function HeroScene() {
  const hostRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null)

  useEffect(() => {
    const host = hostRef.current
    if (!host) return

    let renderer: THREE.WebGLRenderer
    try {
      renderer = new THREE.WebGLRenderer({
        alpha: true,
        antialias: true,
        powerPreference: 'low-power',
      })
    } catch {
      // WebGL is unavailable. The CSS glow behind the hero remains visible.
      return
    }

    renderer.setPixelRatio(Math.min(window.devicePixelRatio, 1.75))
    renderer.setClearAlpha(0)
    renderer.domElement.style.cssText = 'display:block;width:100%;height:100%'
    renderer.domElement.setAttribute('aria-hidden', 'true')
    host.appendChild(renderer.domElement)

    const scene = new THREE.Scene()
    const camera = new THREE.PerspectiveCamera(42, 1, 0.1, 50)
    camera.position.z = 7.4

    const root = new THREE.Group()
    const shell = new THREE.Group()
    root.add(shell)
    scene.add(root)

    const coreGeometry = new THREE.IcosahedronGeometry(1.75, 1)
    const coreMaterial = new THREE.MeshBasicMaterial({
      color: emerald,
      wireframe: true,
      transparent: true,
      opacity: 0.24,
    })
    const core = new THREE.Mesh(coreGeometry, coreMaterial)
    shell.add(core)

    const outerGeometry = new THREE.IcosahedronGeometry(2.55, 1)
    const outerMaterial = new THREE.MeshBasicMaterial({
      color: violet,
      wireframe: true,
      transparent: true,
      opacity: 0.1,
    })
    const outer = new THREE.Mesh(outerGeometry, outerMaterial)
    outer.rotation.set(0.5, 0.1, 0.35)
    shell.add(outer)

    const accentGeometry = new THREE.IcosahedronGeometry(0.68, 0)
    const accentMaterial = new THREE.MeshBasicMaterial({
      color: amber,
      wireframe: true,
      transparent: true,
      opacity: 0.16,
    })
    const accent = new THREE.Mesh(accentGeometry, accentMaterial)
    accent.rotation.set(0.2, 0.8, 0.4)
    accent.position.set(1.2, -0.65, 0.5)
    shell.add(accent)

    const particleCount = 110
    const positions = new Float32Array(particleCount * 3)
    for (let i = 0; i < particleCount; i += 1) {
      const radius = 3.1 + Math.random() * 2.1
      const theta = Math.random() * Math.PI * 2
      const phi = Math.acos(2 * Math.random() - 1)
      positions[i * 3] = radius * Math.sin(phi) * Math.cos(theta)
      positions[i * 3 + 1] = radius * Math.sin(phi) * Math.sin(theta)
      positions[i * 3 + 2] = radius * Math.cos(phi)
    }

    const particleGeometry = new THREE.BufferGeometry()
    particleGeometry.setAttribute('position', new THREE.BufferAttribute(positions, 3))
    const particleMaterial = new THREE.PointsMaterial({
      color: 0xa78bfa,
      size: 0.035,
      transparent: true,
      opacity: 0.6,
      depthWrite: false,
      sizeAttenuation: true,
    })
    const particles = new THREE.Points(particleGeometry, particleMaterial)
    shell.add(particles)

    const ringGeometry = new THREE.TorusGeometry(2.95, 0.006, 8, 180)
    const ringMaterial = new THREE.MeshBasicMaterial({
      color: emerald,
      transparent: true,
      opacity: 0.14,
    })
    const ring = new THREE.Mesh(ringGeometry, ringMaterial)
    ring.rotation.set(1.24, 0.28, 0)
    root.add(ring)

    const pointer = { x: 0, y: 0 }
    const target = { x: 0, y: 0 }

    const onPointerMove = (event: PointerEvent) => {
      const rect = host.getBoundingClientRect()
      target.x = ((event.clientX - rect.left) / rect.width - 0.5) * 2
      target.y = ((event.clientY - rect.top) / rect.height - 0.5) * 2
    }

    const resize = () => {
      const width = host.clientWidth
      const height = host.clientHeight
      if (!width || !height) return

      renderer.setSize(width, height, false)
      camera.aspect = width / height
      camera.updateProjectionMatrix()

      const fit = Math.min(1, Math.max(0.42, camera.aspect * 1.06))
      root.scale.setScalar(fit)

      if (reduceMotion) renderer.render(scene, camera)
    }

    const resizeObserver = new ResizeObserver(resize)
    resizeObserver.observe(host)
    window.addEventListener('pointermove', onPointerMove, { passive: true })

    const motionQuery = window.matchMedia('(prefers-reduced-motion: reduce)')
    let reduceMotion = motionQuery.matches
    let frame = 0
    let inView = true
    let lastTime = performance.now()

    const renderFrame = (time: number) => {
      frame = requestAnimationFrame(renderFrame)
      const delta = Math.min((time - lastTime) / 1000, 0.05)
      lastTime = time

      pointer.x += (target.x - pointer.x) * Math.min(1, delta * 2.2)
      pointer.y += (target.y - pointer.y) * Math.min(1, delta * 2.2)

      root.rotation.y = pointer.x * 0.14
      root.rotation.x = -pointer.y * 0.1
      shell.rotation.y += delta * 0.08
      core.rotation.x = time * 0.00012
      core.rotation.z = time * 0.00005
      outer.rotation.y = 0.1 + time * 0.00004
      accent.rotation.x = -time * 0.00018
      ring.rotation.z = time * 0.000045

      renderer.render(scene, camera)
    }

    const start = () => {
      if (reduceMotion || frame || !inView || document.hidden) return
      lastTime = performance.now()
      frame = requestAnimationFrame(renderFrame)
    }

    const stop = () => {
      if (frame) cancelAnimationFrame(frame)
      frame = 0
    }

    const onVisibilityChange = () => {
      if (document.hidden) stop()
      else start()
    }

    const onMotionPreferenceChange = () => {
      reduceMotion = motionQuery.matches
      if (reduceMotion) {
        stop()
        renderer.render(scene, camera)
      } else {
        start()
      }
    }

    const intersectionObserver = new IntersectionObserver(
      ([entry]) => {
        inView = entry.isIntersecting
        if (inView) start()
        else stop()
      },
      { threshold: 0.01 }
    )
    intersectionObserver.observe(host)

    document.addEventListener('visibilitychange', onVisibilityChange)
    motionQuery.addEventListener('change', onMotionPreferenceChange)

    resize()
    renderer.render(scene, camera)
    start()

    return () => {
      stop()
      resizeObserver.disconnect()
      intersectionObserver.disconnect()
      document.removeEventListener('visibilitychange', onVisibilityChange)
      motionQuery.removeEventListener('change', onMotionPreferenceChange)
      window.removeEventListener('pointermove', onPointerMove)

      coreGeometry.dispose()
      coreMaterial.dispose()
      outerGeometry.dispose()
      outerMaterial.dispose()
      accentGeometry.dispose()
      accentMaterial.dispose()
      particleGeometry.dispose()
      particleMaterial.dispose()
      ringGeometry.dispose()
      ringMaterial.dispose()
      renderer.dispose()
      renderer.domElement.remove()
    }
  }, [])

  return <div ref={hostRef} className="h-full w-full" aria-hidden="true" />
}


===== FILE: src/components/home/hero-visual.tsx =====
'use client'

import { useEffect, useState } from 'react'
import dynamic from 'next/dynamic'

const HeroScene = dynamic(() => import('./hero-scene'), {
  ssr: false,
  loading: () => null,
})

type IdleWindow = Window & {
  requestIdleCallback?: (callback: () => void, options?: { timeout?: number }) => number
  cancelIdleCallback?: (handle: number) => void
}

/**
 * The Three.js scene is decorative. Keep it out of the mobile bundle and load
 * it only on larger screens once the browser is idle (or after a short delay
 * where requestIdleCallback is unavailable). The CSS glow is always visible.
 */
export function HeroVisual() {
  const [showScene, setShowScene] = useState(false)

  useEffect(() => {
    if (
      !window.matchMedia('(min-width: 768px)').matches ||
      window.matchMedia('(prefers-reduced-motion: reduce)').matches
    ) {
      return
    }

    const idleWindow = window as IdleWindow

    if (idleWindow.requestIdleCallback) {
      const handle = idleWindow.requestIdleCallback(() => setShowScene(true), {
        timeout: 2500,
      })
      return () => idleWindow.cancelIdleCallback?.(handle)
    }

    const timer = window.setTimeout(() => setShowScene(true), 1200)
    return () => window.clearTimeout(timer)
  }, [])

  return (
    <div className="pointer-events-none absolute inset-0 overflow-hidden" aria-hidden="true">
      <div className="absolute left-1/2 top-1/2 h-[38rem] w-[38rem] -translate-x-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 rounded-full bg-[radial-gradient(circle_at_center,rgba(52,211,153,0.11),rgba(139,92,246,0.09)_45%,transparent_72%)] blur-2xl" />
      <div className="hero-canvas-mask absolute inset-0 opacity-45 dark:opacity-70">
        {showScene && <HeroScene />}
      </div>
      <div className="absolute inset-x-0 bottom-0 h-40 bg-gradient-to-t from-background via-background/60 to-transparent" />
    </div>
  )
}

===== FILE: src/components/language-paths.tsx =====
import Link from 'next/link'
import {
  ArrowRight,
  Braces,
  FileCode,
  Hash,
  Layers,
  Terminal,
  type LucideIcon,
} from 'lucide-react'
import { Badge } from '@/components/ui/badge'
import { Button } from '@/components/ui/button'
import { Card, CardContent, CardFooter, CardHeader, CardTitle } from '@/components/ui/card'
import { getTotalMinutes, getTotalXP, tracks } from '@/lib/curriculum'
import { cn, getAccent } from '@/lib/utils'

const languageIcons: Record<string, LucideIcon> = {
  c: FileCode,
  cpp: Braces,
  csharp: Hash,
  python: Terminal,
}

const languageTracks = tracks
  .filter((track) => track.category === 'programming-languages')
  .map((track) => ({
    ...track,
    modules: track.modules.filter((module) => module.status !== 'advanced' && module.status !== 'planned'),
  }))

export function LanguagePaths({ className }: { className?: string }) {
  if (languageTracks.length === 0) return null

  return (
    <div className={cn('grid grid-cols-1 gap-6 md:grid-cols-2', className)}>
      {languageTracks.map((track) => {
        const Icon = languageIcons[track.key] ?? Terminal
        const lessonCount = track.modules.reduce(
          (sum, module) => sum + module.lessons.length,
          0
        )
        const hours = Math.round(getTotalMinutes(track.key) / 60)
        const xp = getTotalXP(track.key)

        return (
          <Link key={track.key} href={`/learn/${track.key}`} className="group block min-w-0">
            <Card className="relative h-full overflow-hidden border-border/50 transition-all motion-safe:hover:-translate-y-1 hover:shadow-xl">
              <div
                className={cn(
                  'absolute inset-x-0 top-0 h-1 bg-gradient-to-r',
                  getAccent(track.color).gradient
                )}
              />
              <CardHeader>
                <div className="flex items-start justify-between gap-4">
                  <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
                    <div
                      className={cn(
                        'flex h-12 w-12 items-center justify-center rounded-xl',
                        getAccent(track.color).icon
                      )}
                    >
                      <Icon className="h-6 w-6" aria-hidden="true" />
                    </div>
                    <div>
                      <CardTitle className="text-2xl">{track.name}</CardTitle>
                      <p className="mt-1 text-xs font-medium uppercase tracking-wide text-muted-foreground">
                        Beginner → Mastery
                      </p>
                    </div>
                  </div>
                  <Badge variant="outline" className="shrink-0">
                    {lessonCount} lessons
                  </Badge>
                </div>
              </CardHeader>
              <CardContent className="space-y-4">
                <p className="text-sm text-muted-foreground">{track.description}</p>
                <ul className="space-y-2" role="list">
                  {track.modules.map((module, index) => (
                    <li
                      key={module.id}
                      className="flex items-center gap-3 rounded-lg bg-muted/30 px-3 py-2 text-sm"
                    >
                      <span
                        className={cn(
                          'flex h-6 w-6 shrink-0 items-center justify-center rounded-full text-xs font-semibold',
                          getAccent(track.color).pill
                        )}
                      >
                        {index + 1}
                      </span>
                      <span className="min-w-0 flex-1 truncate font-medium">
                        {module.name}
                      </span>
                      <span className="shrink-0 text-xs text-muted-foreground">
                        {module.lessons.length} lessons
                      </span>
                    </li>
                  ))}
                </ul>
                <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-x-5 gap-y-1 text-xs text-muted-foreground">
                  <span className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5">
                    <Layers className="h-3.5 w-3.5" aria-hidden="true" />
                    {track.modules.length} levels
                  </span>
                  <span>~{hours} hours</span>
                  <span>{xp} XP</span>
                </div>
              </CardContent>
              <CardFooter className="border-t-0 pt-0">
                <Button variant="outline" className="w-full justify-between">
                  Explore the {track.name} path
                  <ArrowRight
                    className="h-4 w-4 transition-transform group-hover:translate-x-0.5"
                    aria-hidden="true"
                  />
                </Button>
              </CardFooter>
            </Card>
          </Link>
        )
      })}
    </div>
  )
}

===== FILE: src/components/lesson-client.tsx =====
'use client'

import { useState, useEffect, type ReactNode } from 'react'
import Link from 'next/link'
import { useProgress } from '@/hooks/use-progress'
import type { Lesson } from '@/lib/curriculum'
import { Button } from '@/components/ui/button'
import { Card, CardContent, CardHeader, CardTitle } from '@/components/ui/card'
import { Badge } from '@/components/ui/badge'
import { Separator } from '@/components/ui/separator'
import { Textarea } from '@/components/ui/textarea'
import { CommentThread } from '@/components/community/comment-thread'
import { Reveal } from '@/components/motion/reveal'
import {
  CheckCircle2, Circle, ExternalLink, Play, FileText, BookOpen, Code2,
  ChevronLeft, ChevronRight, Zap, Bookmark, BookmarkCheck, Lightbulb, StickyNote,
  Clock, type LucideIcon,
} from 'lucide-react'
import { cn, getAccent } from '@/lib/utils'

const resourceTypeMeta: Record<string, { icon: LucideIcon; label: string; color: string }> = {
  video: { icon: Play, label: 'Video', color: 'rose' },
  article: { icon: FileText, label: 'Article', color: 'blue' },
  docs: { icon: BookOpen, label: 'Docs', color: 'emerald' },
  interactive: { icon: Code2, label: 'Interactive', color: 'violet' },
  project: { icon: Code2, label: 'Project', color: 'amber' },
  cheatsheet: { icon: FileText, label: 'Cheatsheet', color: 'slate' },
}

interface LessonClientProps {
  lesson: Lesson
  track: string
  moduleKey: string
  prev: { id: string; title: string; moduleKey: string } | null
  next: { id: string; title: string; moduleKey: string } | null
  children?: ReactNode
}

export function LessonClient({ lesson, track, moduleKey, prev, next, children }: LessonClientProps) {
  const { isLoaded, isCompleted, markComplete, saveNotes, state, toggleBookmark, setLastVisited } = useProgress()
  const [notes, setNotes] = useState('')
  const [notesSaved, setNotesSaved] = useState(false)

  const completed = isLoaded && isCompleted(lesson.id)
  const bookmarked = state.bookmarks.includes(lesson.id)

  useEffect(() => {
    if (isLoaded) {
      // Hydrate local notes from the progress store, then record the visit.
      // eslint-disable-next-line react-hooks/set-state-in-effect
      setNotes(state.lessons[lesson.id]?.notes || '')
      setLastVisited(track, moduleKey, lesson.id)
    }
    // eslint-disable-next-line react-hooks/exhaustive-deps
  }, [isLoaded, lesson.id])

  const handleToggleComplete = () => {
    markComplete(lesson.id, lesson.xpReward, !completed)
  }

  const handleSaveNotes = () => {
    saveNotes(lesson.id, notes)
    setNotesSaved(true)
    setTimeout(() => setNotesSaved(false), 2000)
  }

  return (
    <div className="space-y-10">
      {/* Key Takeaways */}
      {lesson.keyTakeaways.length > 0 && (
        <Reveal>
          <Card className="border-amber-500/20 bg-amber-500/5">
          <CardHeader>
            <CardTitle className="flex items-center gap-2 text-lg">
              <Lightbulb className="h-5 w-5 text-amber-500" aria-hidden="true" />
              What you&apos;ll learn
            </CardTitle>
          </CardHeader>
          <CardContent>
            <ul className="grid grid-cols-1 gap-2 sm:grid-cols-2" role="list">
              {lesson.keyTakeaways.map((takeaway, i) => (
                <li key={i} className="flex items-start gap-2 text-sm">
                  <CheckCircle2 className="h-4 w-4 text-emerald-500 flex-shrink-0 mt-0.5" aria-hidden="true" />
                  {takeaway}
                </li>
              ))}
            </ul>
            </CardContent>
          </Card>
        </Reveal>
      )}

      {/* Written lesson */}
      {children && (
        <article className="prose prose-neutral max-w-none dark:prose-invert prose-headings:scroll-mt-24 prose-headings:font-bold prose-headings:tracking-tight prose-h2:mt-10 prose-h2:text-2xl prose-h3:mt-8 prose-h3:text-xl prose-p:leading-relaxed prose-li:my-1 prose-strong:text-foreground prose-code:rounded-md prose-code:bg-muted prose-code:px-1.5 prose-code:py-0.5 prose-code:font-medium prose-code:text-foreground prose-code:before:content-none prose-code:after:content-none">
          {children}
        </article>
      )}

      {/* Resources */}
      <Reveal>
        <section aria-labelledby="resources-heading">
        <div className="flex items-center justify-between mb-5">
          <h2 id="resources-heading" className="text-2xl font-bold tracking-tight">
            Go deeper
          </h2>
          <Badge variant="secondary" className="text-xs">{lesson.resources.length} resources</Badge>
        </div>
        <p className="text-sm text-muted-foreground mb-5">
          Optional supplementary material — videos, articles, and docs to reinforce what you just read.
        </p>
        <div className="grid grid-cols-1 gap-4 sm:grid-cols-2">
          {lesson.resources.map((resource, i) => {
            const meta = resourceTypeMeta[resource.type] || resourceTypeMeta.article
            const Icon = meta.icon
            return (
              <a
                key={i}
                href={resource.url}
                target="_blank"
                rel="noopener noreferrer"
                className="group flex flex-col rounded-xl border border-border/50 bg-card p-4 transition-all motion-safe:hover:-translate-y-0.5 hover:border-border hover:shadow-md"
              >
                <div className="flex items-center gap-2 mb-2">
                  <div className={cn('flex h-8 w-8 items-center justify-center rounded-lg flex-shrink-0', getAccent(meta.color).icon)}>
                    <Icon className="h-4 w-4" aria-hidden="true" />
                  </div>
                  <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5 flex-wrap">
                    <Badge variant="outline" className="text-xs uppercase tracking-wide sm:text-[10px]">
                      {meta.label}
                    </Badge>
                    {resource.isVerified && (
                      <Badge className="text-xs sm:text-[10px] bg-emerald-500/15 text-emerald-600 border-emerald-500/20 hover:bg-emerald-500/15">
                        ✓ Verified
                      </Badge>
                    )}
                  </div>
                  <ExternalLink className="h-3.5 w-3.5 ml-auto text-muted-foreground/50 group-hover:text-foreground transition-colors flex-shrink-0" aria-hidden="true" />
                </div>
                <h3 className="font-semibold text-sm group-hover:text-primary transition-colors">
                  {resource.title}
                </h3>
                {resource.description && (
                  <p className="mt-1 text-xs text-muted-foreground line-clamp-2">{resource.description}</p>
                )}
                <div className="mt-auto pt-2 flex items-center gap-3 text-xs text-muted-foreground">
                  {resource.provider && <span>{resource.provider}</span>}
                  {resource.duration && (
                    <span className="flex items-center gap-1">
                      <Clock className="h-3 w-3" aria-hidden="true" />
                      {resource.duration}
                    </span>
                  )}
                </div>
              </a>
            )
          })}
          </div>
        </section>
      </Reveal>

      {/* Playground CTA */}
      {lesson.playground && (
        <Reveal>
          <Card className="border-violet-500/20 bg-violet-500/5">
          <CardContent className="flex flex-col gap-4 pt-6 sm:flex-row sm:items-center sm:justify-between">
            <div className="flex items-start gap-3">
              <div className="flex h-10 w-10 items-center justify-center rounded-xl bg-violet-500/15 text-violet-500 flex-shrink-0">
                <Code2 className="h-5 w-5" aria-hidden="true" />
              </div>
              <div>
                <h3 className="font-semibold">{lesson.playground.title}</h3>
                <p className="text-sm text-muted-foreground">{lesson.playground.description}</p>
              </div>
            </div>
            <Button asChild variant="outline" className="border-violet-500/30 hover:bg-violet-500/10 flex-shrink-0">
              <Link href={`/playground?template=${lesson.playground.template}`}>
                Open Playground <ExternalLink className="ml-2 h-4 w-4" aria-hidden="true" />
              </Link>
            </Button>
            </CardContent>
          </Card>
        </Reveal>
      )}

      {/* Notes */}
      <Card data-print-hide>
        <CardHeader>
          <CardTitle className="flex items-center gap-2 text-lg">
            <StickyNote className="h-5 w-5 text-blue-500" aria-hidden="true" />
            Your Notes
          </CardTitle>
        </CardHeader>
        <CardContent className="space-y-3">
          <Textarea
            placeholder="Write your notes, key concepts, or things to revisit..."
            value={notes}
            onChange={(e) => setNotes(e.target.value)}
            rows={4}
            className="resize-y"
          />
          <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
            <Button size="sm" onClick={handleSaveNotes} variant={notesSaved ? 'secondary' : 'default'}>
              {notesSaved ? '✓ Saved' : 'Save Notes'}
            </Button>
            <span className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">
              Notes are stored locally in your browser.
            </span>
          </div>
        </CardContent>
      </Card>

      {/* Complete + Bookmark */}
      <Card
        data-print-hide
        className={cn(
          'transition-colors',
          completed ? 'border-emerald-500/40 bg-emerald-500/5' : 'border-border/50'
        )}
      >
        <CardContent className="flex flex-col gap-4 pt-6 sm:flex-row sm:items-center sm:justify-between">
          <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
            {completed ? (
              <CheckCircle2 className="h-8 w-8 text-emerald-500" aria-hidden="true" />
            ) : (
              <Circle className="h-8 w-8 text-muted-foreground/40" aria-hidden="true" />
            )}
            <div>
              <p className="font-semibold">
                {completed ? 'Lesson completed!' : 'Finished this lesson?'}
              </p>
              <p className="text-sm text-muted-foreground">
                {completed
                  ? `You earned ${lesson.xpReward} XP. Great work!`
                  : `Mark it complete to earn ${lesson.xpReward} XP.`}
              </p>
            </div>
          </div>
          <div className="flex gap-2 flex-shrink-0">
            <Button
              variant="outline"
              size="icon"
              onClick={() => toggleBookmark(lesson.id)}
              aria-label={bookmarked ? 'Remove bookmark' : 'Bookmark this lesson'}
              title={bookmarked ? 'Remove bookmark' : 'Bookmark this lesson'}
            >
              {bookmarked ? (
                <BookmarkCheck className="h-5 w-5 text-amber-500" aria-hidden="true" />
              ) : (
                <Bookmark className="h-5 w-5" aria-hidden="true" />
              )}
            </Button>
            <Button
              onClick={handleToggleComplete}
              disabled={!isLoaded}
              variant={completed ? 'outline' : 'default'}
              className="gap-2"
            >
              {completed ? (
                <>
                  <CheckCircle2 className="h-4 w-4" aria-hidden="true" />
                  Completed
                </>
              ) : (
                <>
                  <Zap className="h-4 w-4" aria-hidden="true" />
                  Mark Complete (+{lesson.xpReward} XP)
                </>
              )}
            </Button>
          </div>
        </CardContent>
      </Card>

      {/* Discussion */}
      <Separator />
      <CommentThread
        targetType="lesson"
        targetId={lesson.id}
        title="Lesson discussion"
        description="Stuck on a step, spotted an error, or found a better way? Leave it here for the next learner."
      />

      {/* Prev/Next Navigation */}
      <Separator />
      <nav className="flex flex-col gap-4 sm:flex-row sm:justify-between" aria-label="Lesson navigation">
        {prev ? (
          <Button variant="outline" asChild className="justify-start h-auto py-3 max-w-full sm:max-w-[45%]">
            <Link href={`/learn/${track}/${prev.moduleKey}/${prev.id}`}>
              <ChevronLeft className="h-4 w-4 mr-2 flex-shrink-0" aria-hidden="true" />
              <span className="flex flex-col items-start min-w-0">
                <span className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">Previous</span>
                <span className="truncate font-medium">{prev.title}</span>
              </span>
            </Link>
          </Button>
        ) : (
          <div />
        )}
        {next ? (
          <Button variant="outline" asChild className="justify-end h-auto py-3 max-w-full sm:max-w-[45%] sm:ml-auto">
            <Link href={`/learn/${track}/${next.moduleKey}/${next.id}`}>
              <span className="flex flex-col items-end min-w-0">
                <span className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">Next</span>
                <span className="truncate font-medium">{next.title}</span>
              </span>
              <ChevronRight className="h-4 w-4 ml-2 flex-shrink-0" aria-hidden="true" />
            </Link>
          </Button>
        ) : (
          <div />
        )}
      </nav>
    </div>
  )
}

===== FILE: src/components/mdx/callout.tsx =====
import type { ReactNode } from 'react'
import { Info, Lightbulb, AlertTriangle, CheckCircle2, ShieldAlert } from 'lucide-react'
import { cn } from '@/lib/utils'

type CalloutType = 'info' | 'tip' | 'warning' | 'danger' | 'success'

const styles: Record<
  CalloutType,
  { container: string; icon: string; title: string; Icon: typeof Info }
> = {
  info: {
    container: 'border-blue-500/30 bg-blue-500/5',
    icon: 'text-blue-500',
    title: 'text-blue-700 dark:text-blue-300',
    Icon: Info,
  },
  tip: {
    container: 'border-emerald-500/30 bg-emerald-500/5',
    icon: 'text-emerald-500',
    title: 'text-emerald-700 dark:text-emerald-300',
    Icon: Lightbulb,
  },
  warning: {
    container: 'border-amber-500/30 bg-amber-500/5',
    icon: 'text-amber-500',
    title: 'text-amber-700 dark:text-amber-300',
    Icon: AlertTriangle,
  },
  danger: {
    container: 'border-rose-500/30 bg-rose-500/5',
    icon: 'text-rose-500',
    title: 'text-rose-700 dark:text-rose-300',
    Icon: ShieldAlert,
  },
  success: {
    container: 'border-emerald-500/30 bg-emerald-500/5',
    icon: 'text-emerald-500',
    title: 'text-emerald-700 dark:text-emerald-300',
    Icon: CheckCircle2,
  },
}

export function Callout({
  type = 'info',
  title,
  children,
}: {
  type?: CalloutType
  title?: string
  children: ReactNode
}) {
  const meta = styles[type] ?? styles.info
  const Icon = meta.Icon

  return (
    <aside className={cn('not-prose my-6 rounded-xl border p-4 sm:p-5', meta.container)}>
      <div className="flex gap-3">
        <Icon className={cn('mt-0.5 h-5 w-5 shrink-0', meta.icon)} aria-hidden="true" />
        <div className="min-w-0 space-y-1.5">
          {title && <p className={cn('font-semibold text-sm', meta.title)}>{title}</p>}
          <div className="text-sm leading-relaxed text-foreground/90 [&>p]:m-0 [&>p+p]:mt-2 [&>ul]:my-1 [&>ul]:pl-5 [&>ul]:list-disc">
            {children}
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>
    </aside>
  )
}

export function KeyPoint({ children }: { children: ReactNode }) {
  return (
    <div className="not-prose my-6 rounded-xl border border-violet-500/30 bg-violet-500/5 p-4 text-sm leading-relaxed">
      <span className="mr-2 font-semibold text-violet-600 dark:text-violet-300">Key idea:</span>
      {children}
    </div>
  )
}


===== FILE: src/components/mdx/code-block.tsx =====
'use client'

import { useRef, useState, type ComponentPropsWithoutRef } from 'react'
import { Check, Copy } from 'lucide-react'
import { cn } from '@/lib/utils'

export function CodeBlock({ children, className, ...props }: ComponentPropsWithoutRef<'pre'>) {
  const ref = useRef<HTMLPreElement>(null)
  const [copied, setCopied] = useState(false)

  const copy = async () => {
    try {
      await navigator.clipboard.writeText(ref.current?.innerText ?? '')
      setCopied(true)
      setTimeout(() => setCopied(false), 1600)
    } catch {
      // Clipboard can be unavailable (non-secure context); ignore.
    }
  }

  return (
    <div className="not-prose group relative my-6">
      <button
        type="button"
        onClick={copy}
        className="absolute right-2.5 top-2.5 z-10 inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 rounded-md border border-white/10 bg-white/5 px-2 py-1 text-xs font-medium text-zinc-300 opacity-0 transition-opacity hover:bg-white/10 focus:opacity-100 group-hover:opacity-100"
        aria-label="Copy code"
      >
        {copied ? (
          <>
            <Check className="h-3.5 w-3.5 text-emerald-400" aria-hidden="true" /> Copied
          </>
        ) : (
          <>
            <Copy className="h-3.5 w-3.5" aria-hidden="true" /> Copy
          </>
        )}
      </button>
      <pre
        ref={ref}
        tabIndex={0}
        className={cn(
          'overflow-x-auto rounded-xl border border-border/60 bg-[#0d1117] p-4 text-sm leading-relaxed text-[#c9d1d9] shadow-sm',
          className
        )}
        {...props}
      >
        {children}
      </pre>
    </div>
  )
}

import type { ReactNode } from 'react'

        {children}

  )
}

        {title}

      <div className="[&>div]:my-2 [&>div:last-child]:mb-0">{children}</div>
    </section>
  )
}


===== FILE: src/components/mdx/quiz.tsx =====
'use client'

import { useState } from 'react'
import { HelpCircle, CheckCircle2, XCircle, RotateCcw } from 'lucide-react'
import { cn } from '@/lib/utils'

export function Quiz({
  question,
  options,
  answer,
  explanation,
}: {
  question: string
  options: string[]
  answer: number
  explanation?: string
}) {
  const [selected, setSelected] = useState<number | null>(null)
  const answered = selected !== null
  const correct = selected === answer

  return (
    <div className="not-prose my-8 rounded-xl border border-border/60 bg-card p-5">
      <div className="mb-3 flex items-center gap-2 text-xs font-semibold uppercase tracking-wide text-muted-foreground">
        <HelpCircle className="h-4 w-4" aria-hidden="true" />
        Quick check
      </div>
      <p className="mb-4 font-medium">{question}</p>

      <div className="grid grid-cols-1 gap-2" role="group" aria-label="Answer options">
        {options.map((option, index) => {
          const isSelected = selected === index
          const isAnswer = answer === index
          return (
            <button
              key={index}
              type="button"
              onClick={() => setSelected(index)}
              disabled={answered}
              className={cn(
                'flex w-full items-start gap-3 rounded-lg border px-3 py-2.5 text-left text-sm transition-colors',
                !answered && 'hover:border-primary/50 hover:bg-muted/50',
                answered && isAnswer && 'border-emerald-500/50 bg-emerald-500/10',
                answered && isSelected && !isAnswer && 'border-rose-500/50 bg-rose-500/10',
                !answered || (!isAnswer && !isSelected) ? 'border-border/60' : ''
              )}
              aria-pressed={isSelected}
            >
              <span
                className={cn(
                  'flex h-5 w-5 shrink-0 items-center justify-center rounded-full border text-xs font-semibold sm:text-[11px]',
                  answered && isAnswer
                    ? 'border-emerald-500 bg-emerald-500 text-white'
                    : answered && isSelected
                      ? 'border-rose-500 bg-rose-500 text-white'
                      : 'border-border text-muted-foreground'
                )}
              >
                {String.fromCharCode(65 + index)}
              </span>
              <span className="pt-0.5">{option}</span>
            </button>
          )
        })}
      </div>

      {answered && (
        <div
          className={cn(
            'mt-4 rounded-lg border p-3 text-sm',
            correct
              ? 'border-emerald-500/30 bg-emerald-500/5'
              : 'border-amber-500/30 bg-amber-500/5'
          )}
          role="status"
        >
          <p className="flex items-center gap-2 font-medium">
            {correct ? (
              <CheckCircle2 className="h-4 w-4 text-emerald-500" aria-hidden="true" />
            ) : (
              <XCircle className="h-4 w-4 text-amber-500" aria-hidden="true" />
            )}
            {correct ? 'Correct!' : 'Not quite — here is why:'}
          </p>
          {explanation && <p className="mt-1 text-muted-foreground">{explanation}</p>}
          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={() => setSelected(null)}
            className="mt-2 inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 text-xs font-medium text-muted-foreground underline underline-offset-4 hover:text-foreground"
          >
            <RotateCcw className="h-3 w-3" aria-hidden="true" />
            Try again
          </button>
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  )
}


===== FILE: src/components/module-lessons-client.tsx =====
'use client'

import Link from 'next/link'
import { useProgress } from '@/hooks/use-progress'
import type { Lesson } from '@/lib/curriculum'
import { Badge } from '@/components/ui/badge'
import { Reveal } from '@/components/motion/reveal'
import { CheckCircle2, Circle, Clock, Play, Zap, FileText, Code2, BookOpen, type LucideIcon } from 'lucide-react'
import { cn, getDifficultyColor, getAccent } from '@/lib/utils'

const typeIcons: Record<string, LucideIcon> = {
  video: Play,
  article: FileText,
  docs: BookOpen,
  interactive: Code2,
  project: Code2,
  cheatsheet: FileText,
}

export function ModuleLessonsClient({
  track,
  moduleKey,
  lessons,
}: {
  track: string
  moduleKey: string
  lessons: Lesson[]
}) {
  const { isLoaded, isCompleted } = useProgress()

  return (
    <div className="space-y-3">
      {lessons.map((lesson, index) => {
        const completed = isLoaded && isCompleted(lesson.id)
        const difficultyColor = getDifficultyColor(lesson.difficulty)
        const resourceTypes = Array.from(new Set(lesson.resources.map((r) => r.type)))

        return (
          <Reveal key={lesson.id} delay={Math.min(index * 0.035, 0.2)} distance={12}>
            <Link
              href={`/learn/${track}/${moduleKey}/${lesson.id}`}
              className="group block"
            >
            <div
              className={cn(
                'flex items-start gap-4 rounded-xl border p-5 transition-all motion-safe:hover:-translate-y-0.5 hover:shadow-md',
                completed
                  ? 'border-emerald-500/30 bg-emerald-500/5'
                  : 'border-border/50 bg-card hover:border-border'
              )}
            >
              {/* Completion indicator */}
              <div className="flex-shrink-0 pt-0.5">
                {isLoaded ? (
                  completed ? (
                    <CheckCircle2 className="h-6 w-6 text-emerald-500" aria-label="Completed" />
                  ) : (
                    <Circle className="h-6 w-6 text-muted-foreground/40" aria-label="Not completed" />
                  )
                ) : (
                  <div className="h-6 w-6 animate-pulse rounded-full bg-muted" aria-hidden="true" />
                )}
              </div>

              <div className="flex-1 min-w-0">
                <div className="flex items-center gap-2 flex-wrap">
                  <span className="text-xs font-medium text-muted-foreground">
                    Lesson {lesson.order}
                  </span>
                  <Badge
                    variant="outline"
                    className={cn('text-xs', getAccent(difficultyColor).borderSoft, getAccent(difficultyColor).text)}
                  >
                    {lesson.difficulty}
                  </Badge>
                  {completed && (
                    <Badge className="text-xs bg-emerald-500/15 text-emerald-600 border-emerald-500/20 hover:bg-emerald-500/15">
                      Completed
                    </Badge>
                  )}
                </div>
                <h3 className={cn(
                  'mt-1 font-semibold group-hover:text-primary transition-colors',
                  completed && 'line-through decoration-emerald-500/40 text-muted-foreground'
                )}>
                  {lesson.title}
                </h3>
                <p className="mt-1 text-sm text-muted-foreground line-clamp-2">
                  {lesson.description}
                </p>
                <div className="mt-3 flex items-center gap-4 text-xs text-muted-foreground">
                  <span className="flex items-center gap-1">
                    <Clock className="h-3.5 w-3.5" aria-hidden="true" />
                    {lesson.estimatedMinutes} min
                  </span>
                  <span className="flex items-center gap-1">
                    <Zap className="h-3.5 w-3.5" aria-hidden="true" />
                    {lesson.xpReward} XP
                  </span>
                  <span className="flex items-center gap-1.5" aria-label={`${lesson.resources.length} resources`}>
                    {resourceTypes.map((type) => {
                      const Icon = typeIcons[type] || FileText
                      return <Icon key={type} className="h-3.5 w-3.5" aria-hidden="true" />
                    })}
                    <span>{lesson.resources.length} resources</span>
                  </span>
                </div>
              </div>
            </div>
            </Link>
          </Reveal>
        )
      })}
    </div>
  )
}

===== FILE: src/components/motion/reveal.tsx =====
'use client'

import { motion, useReducedMotion, type HTMLMotionProps } from 'motion/react'
import { cn } from '@/lib/utils'

interface RevealProps extends Omit<HTMLMotionProps<'div'>, 'initial' | 'whileInView' | 'transition'> {
  delay?: number
  distance?: number
}

/**
 * Reveals a block once as it enters the viewport. Keep this for larger pieces
 * of content; repeating it on every element makes a page feel busy.
 */
export function Reveal({
  children,
  className,
  delay = 0,
  distance = 18,
  ...props
}: RevealProps) {
  const reduceMotion = useReducedMotion()

  return (
    <motion.div
      data-motion-reveal=""
      className={cn(className)}
      initial={reduceMotion ? false : { opacity: 0, y: distance }}
      whileInView={reduceMotion ? undefined : { opacity: 1, y: 0 }}
      viewport={{ once: true, amount: 0.12, margin: '0px 0px -60px 0px' }}
      transition={{ duration: 0.55, delay, ease: [0.22, 1, 0.36, 1] }}
      {...props}
    >
      {children}
    </motion.div>
  )
}

import Link from 'next/link'

===== FILE: src/components/newsletter-form.tsx =====
'use client'

import { useId, useState } from 'react'
import Link from 'next/link'
import { CheckCircle2, Loader2 } from 'lucide-react'
import { Button } from '@/components/ui/button'
import { Input } from '@/components/ui/input'
import { Label } from '@/components/ui/label'
import { cn } from '@/lib/utils'

type Mode = 'subscribe' | 'unsubscribe'

export function NewsletterForm({
  compact = false,
  mode = 'subscribe',
}: {
  compact?: boolean
  mode?: Mode
}) {
  const formId = useId()
  const emailId = `${formId}-email`
  const websiteId = `${formId}-website`

  const isSubscribe = mode === 'subscribe'
  const [email, setEmail] = useState('')
  const [website, setWebsite] = useState('')
  const [status, setStatus] = useState<'idle' | 'loading' | 'success' | 'error'>('idle')
  const [message, setMessage] = useState('')
  const [successMessage, setSuccessMessage] = useState('')

  async function handleSubmit(event: React.FormEvent<HTMLFormElement>) {
    event.preventDefault()
    if (status === 'loading') return
    setStatus('loading')
    setMessage('')

    try {
      const response = await fetch('/api/newsletter', {
        method: isSubscribe ? 'POST' : 'DELETE',
        headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
        body: JSON.stringify(
          isSubscribe
            ? { email, website, source: compact ? 'footer' : 'newsletter' }
            : { email }
        ),
      })
      const body = await response.json().catch(() => null)
      if (!response.ok) {
        throw new Error(body?.error?.message ?? 'Something went wrong. Try again.')
      }

      if (isSubscribe) {
        if (body?.data?.alreadyConfirmed) {
          setSuccessMessage("That address is already confirmed. You're on the list.")
        } else if (body?.data?.alreadySent) {
          setSuccessMessage('We already sent a confirmation email recently. Check your inbox.')
        } else {
          setSuccessMessage(
            compact
              ? 'Check your inbox and open the confirmation link to finish signing up.'
              : "Check your inbox and click the confirmation link. You're not on the list until you confirm."
          )
        }
      } else {
        setSuccessMessage('That address has been removed from the list.')
      }

      setStatus('success')
      setEmail('')
    } catch (error) {
      setStatus('error')
      setMessage(error instanceof Error ? error.message : 'Something went wrong. Try again.')
    }
  }

  if (status === 'success') {
    return (
      <p
        className={cn(
          'flex items-start gap-2 rounded-lg border border-emerald-500/30 bg-emerald-500/10 px-3 py-2 text-sm',
          compact && 'mt-4'
        )}
        role="status"
      >
        <CheckCircle2 className="mt-0.5 h-4 w-4 flex-shrink-0 text-emerald-500" aria-hidden="true" />
        <span>{successMessage}</span>
      </p>
    )
  }

  return (
    <form onSubmit={handleSubmit} className={cn(compact ? 'mt-4 space-y-2' : 'space-y-3')}>
      <div className="space-y-1.5">
        <Label htmlFor={emailId} className={compact ? 'sr-only' : undefined}>
          Email address
        </Label>
        <div className={cn(compact ? 'space-y-2' : 'flex gap-2')}>
          <Input
            id={emailId}
            type="email"
            required
            autoComplete="email"
            value={email}
            onChange={(event) => {
              setEmail(event.target.value)
              if (status === 'error') setStatus('idle')
            }}
            placeholder="you@example.com"
            maxLength={254}
            className={compact ? 'w-full' : 'h-9 flex-1'}
          />
          <Button
            type="submit"
            size={compact ? 'sm' : 'default'}
            disabled={status === 'loading'}
            className={compact ? 'w-full' : undefined}
          >
            {status === 'loading' && <Loader2 className="mr-2 h-4 w-4 animate-spin" aria-hidden="true" />}
            {isSubscribe ? 'Subscribe' : 'Unsubscribe'}
          </Button>
        </div>
      </div>

      {isSubscribe && (
        <div className="hidden" aria-hidden="true">
          <label htmlFor={websiteId}>Website</label>
          <input
            id={websiteId}
            name="website"
            type="text"
            tabIndex={-1}
            autoComplete="off"
            value={website}
            onChange={(event) => setWebsite(event.target.value)}
          />
        </div>
      )}

      {status === 'error' && (
        <p className="text-xs text-destructive" role="alert">
          {message}
        </p>
      )}

      {!compact && isSubscribe && (
        <p className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">
          Double opt-in: we send one confirmation email, and only confirmed addresses receive
          updates. By subscribing you agree to the{' '}
          <Link href="/privacy" className="underline underline-offset-2 hover:text-foreground">
            privacy policy
          </Link>
          . No spam, and you can unsubscribe at any time.
        </p>
      )}
    </form>
  )
}


===== FILE: src/components/newsletter-token-action.tsx =====
'use client'

import { useState } from 'react'
import Link from 'next/link'
import { CheckCircle2, Loader2, MailCheck, MailX, TriangleAlert } from 'lucide-react'
import { Button } from '@/components/ui/button'

type Mode = 'confirm' | 'unsubscribe'

export function NewsletterTokenAction({ mode, token }: { mode: Mode; token: string }) {
  const [status, setStatus] = useState<'idle' | 'loading' | 'success' | 'error'>('idle')
  const [message, setMessage] = useState('')

  async function handleAction() {
    if (status === 'loading') return
    setStatus('loading')
    setMessage('')

    try {
      const response = await fetch(
        mode === 'confirm' ? '/api/newsletter/confirm' : '/api/newsletter/unsubscribe',
        {
          method: 'POST',
          headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
          body: JSON.stringify({ token }),
        }
      )
      const body = await response.json().catch(() => null)
      if (!response.ok) {
        throw new Error(body?.error?.message ?? 'Something went wrong. Try again.')
      }
      setStatus('success')
    } catch (error) {
      setStatus('error')
      setMessage(error instanceof Error ? error.message : 'Something went wrong. Try again.')
    }
  }

  if (status === 'success') {
    return (
      <div className="space-y-4" role="status">
        <div className="flex items-start gap-2 rounded-xl border border-emerald-500/30 bg-emerald-500/10 px-4 py-3 text-sm">
          <CheckCircle2
            className="mt-0.5 h-4 w-4 flex-shrink-0 text-emerald-500"
            aria-hidden="true"
          />
          <div>
            <p className="font-medium text-foreground">
              {mode === 'confirm' ? 'Subscription confirmed' : 'You have been unsubscribed'}
            </p>
            <p className="mt-1 text-muted-foreground">
              {mode === 'confirm'
                ? 'Future curriculum updates will land in your inbox. We only write when there is something worth reading.'
                : 'That address has been removed from the list. You will not receive any more newsletter emails.'}
            </p>
          </div>
        </div>
        <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-3">
          <Button asChild>
            <Link href={mode === 'confirm' ? '/learn' : '/'}>
              {mode === 'confirm' ? 'Start learning' : 'Back to Nyxora Sync'}
            </Link>
          </Button>
          {mode === 'unsubscribe' && (
            <Button variant="outline" asChild>
              <Link href="/newsletter">Manage newsletter</Link>
            </Button>
          )}
        </div>
      </div>
    )
  }

  return (
    <div className="space-y-4">
      {status === 'error' && (
        <p
          className="flex items-start gap-2 rounded-xl border border-destructive/30 bg-destructive/5 px-4 py-3 text-sm text-destructive"
          role="alert"
        >
          <TriangleAlert className="mt-0.5 h-4 w-4 flex-shrink-0" aria-hidden="true" />
          <span>{message}</span>
        </p>
      )}

      <Button
        onClick={handleAction}
        disabled={status === 'loading'}
        variant={mode === 'unsubscribe' ? 'destructive' : 'default'}
        className="gap-2"
      >
        {status === 'loading' ? (
          <Loader2 className="h-4 w-4 animate-spin" aria-hidden="true" />
        ) : mode === 'confirm' ? (
          <MailCheck className="h-4 w-4" aria-hidden="true" />
        ) : (
          <MailX className="h-4 w-4" aria-hidden="true" />
        )}
        {mode === 'confirm' ? 'Confirm my subscription' : 'Unsubscribe this address'}
      </Button>

      <p className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">
        {mode === 'confirm'
          ? 'The confirmation link expires 48 hours after it was sent.'
          : 'This removes the address immediately and cannot be undone. You can sign up again later.'}
      </p>
    </div>
  )
}


===== FILE: src/components/notification-bell.tsx =====
'use client'

import { useCallback, useEffect, useState } from 'react'
import Link from 'next/link'
import { useSession } from 'next-auth/react'
import { Bell, CheckCheck, Loader2, UserPlus } from 'lucide-react'
import { Avatar, AvatarFallback, AvatarImage } from '@/components/ui/avatar'
import { Button } from '@/components/ui/button'
import {
  DropdownMenu,
  DropdownMenuContent,
  DropdownMenuTrigger,
} from '@/components/ui/dropdown-menu'
import { timeAgo } from '@/lib/time'

interface NotificationItem {
  id: string
  type: string
  message: string | null
  href: string | null
  readAt: string | null
  createdAt: string
  actor: { id: string; name: string | null; image: string | null } | null
}

export function NotificationBell() {
  const { status } = useSession()
  const [notifications, setNotifications] = useState<NotificationItem[]>([])
  const [unread, setUnread] = useState(0)
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(false)
  const [marking, setMarking] = useState(false)

  const load = useCallback(
    async (silent = false) => {
      if (status !== 'authenticated') return
      if (!silent) setLoading(true)
      try {
        const response = await fetch('/api/notifications', { cache: 'no-store' })
        if (!response.ok) return
        const payload = await response.json().catch(() => null)
        setNotifications(payload?.data?.notifications ?? [])
        setUnread(payload?.data?.unread ?? 0)
      } catch {
        // The bell is supplementary; a failed refresh should stay quiet.
      } finally {
        if (!silent) setLoading(false)
      }
    },
    [status]
  )

  useEffect(() => {
    if (status !== 'authenticated') return

    const initialLoad = window.setTimeout(() => load(), 0)

    // Polling works across serverless instances, unlike the old in-memory SSE
    // stream. Refresh when the tab becomes visible/focused and otherwise every
    // 45 seconds while it is open.
    const refreshIfVisible = () => {
      if (document.visibilityState === 'visible') void load(true)
    }
    const timer = window.setInterval(refreshIfVisible, 45_000)
    const onVisibilityChange = () => {
      if (document.visibilityState === 'visible') refreshIfVisible()
    }

    window.addEventListener('focus', refreshIfVisible)
    document.addEventListener('visibilitychange', onVisibilityChange)

    return () => {
      window.clearTimeout(initialLoad)
      window.clearInterval(timer)
      window.removeEventListener('focus', refreshIfVisible)
      document.removeEventListener('visibilitychange', onVisibilityChange)
    }
  }, [status, load])

  if (status !== 'authenticated') return null

  async function markRead(notification: NotificationItem) {
    if (!notification.readAt) {
      setNotifications((current) =>
        current.map((item) =>
          item.id === notification.id
            ? { ...item, readAt: new Date().toISOString() }
            : item
        )
      )
      setUnread((current) => Math.max(0, current - 1))
    }

    await fetch('/api/notifications/read', {
      method: 'POST',
      headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
      body: JSON.stringify({ id: notification.id }),
    }).catch(() => {})
  }

  async function markAllRead() {
    if (marking || unread === 0) return
    setMarking(true)
    try {
      const response = await fetch('/api/notifications/read', {
        method: 'POST',
        headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
        body: JSON.stringify({ all: true }),
      })
      if (response.ok) {
        const now = new Date().toISOString()
        setNotifications((current) =>
          current.map((item) => ({ ...item, readAt: item.readAt ?? now }))
        )
        setUnread(0)
      }
    } finally {
      setMarking(false)
    }
  }

  return (
    <DropdownMenu onOpenChange={(open) => open && load()}>
      <DropdownMenuTrigger asChild>
        <Button
          variant="ghost"
          size="icon"
          className="relative"
          aria-label={
            unread > 0 ? `Notifications, ${unread} unread` : 'Notifications'
          }
        >
          <Bell className="h-5 w-5" aria-hidden="true" />
          {unread > 0 && (
            <span className="absolute right-1 top-1 flex h-4 min-w-4 items-center justify-center rounded-full bg-emerald-500 px-1 text-xs font-bold leading-none text-white sm:text-[10px]">
              {unread > 9 ? '9+' : unread}
            </span>
          )}
        </Button>
      </DropdownMenuTrigger>
      <DropdownMenuContent align="end" className="w-80 p-0">
        <div className="flex items-center justify-between border-b px-3 py-2">
          <span className="text-sm font-semibold">Notifications</span>
          {unread > 0 && (
            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={markAllRead}
              disabled={marking}
              className="inline-flex items-center gap-1 text-xs font-medium text-muted-foreground transition-colors hover:text-foreground disabled:opacity-50"
            >
              {marking ? (
                <Loader2 className="h-3 w-3 animate-spin" aria-hidden="true" />
              ) : (
                <CheckCheck className="h-3 w-3" aria-hidden="true" />
              )}
              Mark all read
            </button>
          )}
        </div>

        <div className="max-h-96 overflow-y-auto">
          {loading && notifications.length === 0 ? (
            <div className="flex items-center justify-center gap-2 px-4 py-8 text-sm text-muted-foreground">
              <Loader2 className="h-4 w-4 animate-spin" aria-hidden="true" />
              Loading notifications...
            </div>
          ) : notifications.length === 0 ? (
            <p className="px-4 py-8 text-center text-sm text-muted-foreground">
              No notifications yet. Follow requests and their responses will
              appear here.
            </p>
          ) : (
            notifications.map((notification) => {
              const Icon =
                notification.type === 'follow_request' ? UserPlus : CheckCheck
              const href =
                notification.href ??
                (notification.actor
                  ? `/community/people/${notification.actor.id}`
                  : '/settings?tab=profile')

              return (
                <Link
                  key={notification.id}
                  href={href}
                  onClick={() => markRead(notification)}
                  className={`flex gap-3 border-b border-border/50 px-3 py-3 transition-colors last:border-b-0 hover:bg-accent ${
                    notification.readAt ? '' : 'bg-emerald-500/5'
                  }`}
                >
                  <Avatar className="mt-0.5 h-9 w-9">
                    <AvatarImage
                      src={notification.actor?.image ?? ''}
                      alt=""
                    />
                    <AvatarFallback>
                      {notification.actor?.name?.charAt(0) ?? (
                        <Icon className="h-4 w-4" aria-hidden="true" />
                      )}
                    </AvatarFallback>
                  </Avatar>
                  <div className="min-w-0 flex-1">
                    <p className="text-sm leading-snug">
                      {notification.message ?? 'You have a new notification.'}
                    </p>
                    <p className="mt-0.5 text-xs text-muted-foreground">
                      {timeAgo(notification.createdAt)}
                    </p>
                  </div>
                  {!notification.readAt && (
                    <span
                      className="mt-2 h-2 w-2 shrink-0 rounded-full bg-emerald-500"
                      aria-label="Unread"
                    />
                  )}
                </Link>
              )
            })
          )}
        </div>
      </DropdownMenuContent>
    </DropdownMenu>
  )
}
