function useDebouncedValue<T>(value: T, delay: number): T {
  const [debounced, setDebounced] = useState(value)
  useEffect(() => {
    const t = setTimeout(() => setDebounced(value), delay)
    return () => clearTimeout(t)
  }, [value, delay])
  return debounced
}

export function PlaygroundClient() {
  const [template, setTemplate] = useState('blank')
  const [html, setHtml] = useState(WEB_TEMPLATES.blank.html)
  const [css, setCss] = useState(WEB_TEMPLATES.blank.css)
  const [js, setJs] = useState(WEB_TEMPLATES.blank.js)
  const [activeTab, setActiveTab] = useState('html')
  const [copied, setCopied] = useState(false)

  // Persist to localStorage
  useEffect(() => {
    try {
      const saved = localStorage.getItem('mern-learn-playground')
      if (saved) {
        const parsed = JSON.parse(saved)
        // Hydrate editor from localStorage (external store) on mount.
        // eslint-disable-next-line react-hooks/set-state-in-effect
        setHtml(parsed.html ?? WEB_TEMPLATES.blank.html)
        setCss(parsed.css ?? WEB_TEMPLATES.blank.css)
        setJs(parsed.js ?? WEB_TEMPLATES.blank.js)
      }
    } catch {}
  }, [])

  useEffect(() => {
    try {
      localStorage.setItem('mern-learn-playground', JSON.stringify({ html, css, js }))
    } catch {}
  }, [html, css, js])

  const debouncedHtml = useDebouncedValue(html, 500)
  const debouncedCss = useDebouncedValue(css, 500)
  const debouncedJs = useDebouncedValue(js, 500)

  const srcDoc = useMemo(() => {
    return `<!DOCTYPE html>
<html>
<head>
<meta charset="UTF-8">
<style>${debouncedCss}</style>
</head>
<body>
${debouncedHtml}
<script>
try {
${debouncedJs}
} catch (err) {
  document.body.innerHTML += '<pre style="color:#f87171;background:#450a0a;padding:1rem;border-radius:0.5rem;font-size:0.85rem;">Error: ' + err.message + '</pre>';
  console.error(err);
}
</script>
</body>
</html>`
  }, [debouncedHtml, debouncedCss, debouncedJs])

  const [previewKey, setPreviewKey] = useState(0)

  const applyTemplate = (key: string) => {
    const t = WEB_TEMPLATES[key]
    if (!t) return
    setTemplate(key)
    setHtml(t.html)
    setCss(t.css)
    setJs(t.js)
    setPreviewKey((k) => k + 1)
  }

  const resetAll = () => {
    const t = WEB_TEMPLATES[template] || WEB_TEMPLATES.blank
    setHtml(t.html)
    setCss(t.css)
    setJs(t.js)
    setPreviewKey((k) => k + 1)
  }

  const handleCopy = async () => {
    const code = activeTab === 'html' ? html : activeTab === 'css' ? css : js
    await navigator.clipboard.writeText(code)
    setCopied(true)
    setTimeout(() => setCopied(false), 1500)
  }

  const currentCode = activeTab === 'html' ? html : activeTab === 'css' ? css : js
  const setCurrentCode = activeTab === 'html' ? setHtml : activeTab === 'css' ? setCss : setJs

  return (
    <div className="space-y-16">
      {/* Web Playground */}
      <div>
        <div className="flex flex-col gap-4 sm:flex-row sm:items-center sm:justify-between mb-6">
          <div>
            <h2 className="text-2xl font-bold tracking-tight flex items-center gap-2">
              <Zap className="h-6 w-6 text-amber-500" aria-hidden="true" />
              Web Playground
            </h2>
            <p className="mt-1 text-sm text-muted-foreground">
              HTML, CSS & JavaScript — runs locally, saved automatically.
            </p>
          </div>
          <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2">
            {Object.entries(WEB_TEMPLATES).map(([key, t]) => (
              <Button
                key={key}
                variant={template === key ? 'default' : 'outline'}
                size="sm"
                onClick={() => applyTemplate(key)}
              >
                {key === 'blank' && <Wand2 className="mr-1.5 h-3.5 w-3.5" aria-hidden="true" />}
                {t.name}
              </Button>
            ))}
          </div>
        </div>

        <div className="grid grid-cols-1 gap-4 lg:grid-cols-2">
          {/* Editor side */}
          <div className="rounded-xl border border-border/50 overflow-hidden bg-card">
            <div className="flex items-center justify-between border-b border-border/50 px-3 py-2">
              <Tabs value={activeTab} onValueChange={setActiveTab}>
                <TabsList className="h-8">
                  <TabsTrigger value="html" className="text-xs h-7 px-3">HTML</TabsTrigger>
                  <TabsTrigger value="css" className="text-xs h-7 px-3">CSS</TabsTrigger>
                  <TabsTrigger value="js" className="text-xs h-7 px-3">JS</TabsTrigger>
                </TabsList>
              </Tabs>
              <div className="flex items-center gap-1">
                <Button variant="ghost" size="icon" className="h-8 w-8" onClick={handleCopy} aria-label="Copy code">
                  {copied ? <Check className="h-4 w-4 text-emerald-500" /> : <Copy className="h-4 w-4" />}
                </Button>
                <Button variant="ghost" size="icon" className="h-8 w-8" onClick={resetAll} aria-label="Reset code">
                  <RotateCcw className="h-4 w-4" />
                </Button>
              </div>
            </div>
            <textarea
              value={currentCode}
              onChange={(e) => setCurrentCode(e.target.value)}
              spellCheck={false}
              className="w-full h-[400px] resize-none bg-[#0f172a] text-slate-200 p-4 font-mono text-sm leading-relaxed focus:outline-none"
              aria-label={`${activeTab} code editor`}
            />
          </div>

          {/* Preview side */}
          <div className="rounded-xl border border-border/50 overflow-hidden bg-card flex flex-col">
            <div className="flex items-center justify-between border-b border-border/50 px-4 py-2.5">
              <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                <div className="flex gap-1.5" aria-hidden="true">
                  <span className="h-2.5 w-2.5 rounded-full bg-rose-500/70" />
                  <span className="h-2.5 w-2.5 rounded-full bg-amber-500/70" />
                  <span className="h-2.5 w-2.5 rounded-full bg-emerald-500/70" />
                </div>
                <span className="text-xs text-muted-foreground ml-2">Preview</span>
              </div>
              <Button
                variant="ghost"
                size="sm"
                className="h-7 text-xs gap-1.5"
                onClick={() => setPreviewKey((k) => k + 1)}
              >
                <Play className="h-3 w-3" aria-hidden="true" />
                Re-run
              </Button>
            </div>
            <iframe
              key={previewKey}
              srcDoc={srcDoc}
              sandbox="allow-scripts"
              title="Code preview"
              className="w-full h-[400px] bg-white"
            />
          </div>
        </div>
        <p className="mt-3 text-xs text-muted-foreground">
          Tip: The preview re-runs automatically as you type (with a short delay).
          Use <kbd className="rounded border border-border px-1 text-xs sm:text-[10px]">Re-run</kbd> to force a refresh.
        </p>
      </div>

      {/* Full environments */}
      <div>
        <div className="mb-6">
          <h2 className="text-2xl font-bold tracking-tight flex items-center gap-2">
            <Code2 className="h-6 w-6 text-violet-500" aria-hidden="true" />
            Full Development Environments
          </h2>
          <p className="mt-1 text-sm text-muted-foreground">
            Node.js, Express, React and MongoDB need a real runtime. These launch instantly in
            StackBlitz — a free, browser-based dev environment. No installation, no account required.
          </p>
        </div>
        <div className="grid grid-cols-1 gap-4 sm:grid-cols-2">
          {LAUNCH_ENVIRONMENTS.map((env) => (
            <Card key={env.id} className="group hover:shadow-lg transition-shadow">
              <CardContent className="pt-6">
                <div className="flex items-start gap-4">
                  <div
                    className={cn('flex h-12 w-12 flex-shrink-0 items-center justify-center rounded-xl', getAccent(env.color).icon)}
                  >
                    <env.icon className="h-6 w-6" aria-hidden="true" />
                  </div>
                  <div className="flex-1 min-w-0">
                    <h3 className="font-semibold">{env.name}</h3>
                    <p className="mt-1 text-sm text-muted-foreground">{env.description}</p>
                    <div className="mt-2 flex flex-wrap gap-1.5">
                      {env.tags.map((tag) => (
                        <Badge key={tag} variant="secondary" className="text-xs sm:text-[10px]">{tag}</Badge>
                      ))}
                    </div>
                    <Button size="sm" className="mt-4 gap-2" asChild>
                      <a href={env.url} target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">
                        <Play className="h-3.5 w-3.5" aria-hidden="true" />
                        Launch {env.name}
                        <ExternalLink className="h-3 w-3 opacity-60" aria-hidden="true" />
                      </a>
                    </Button>
                  </div>
                </div>
              </CardContent>
            </Card>
          ))}
        </div>
        <div className="mt-4 rounded-lg border border-blue-500/20 bg-blue-500/5 p-4 text-sm text-muted-foreground">
          <p className="font-medium text-foreground mb-1">💡 How it works</p>
          StackBlitz runs a full Node.js environment in your browser using WebContainers technology.
          Everything is free and your code never leaves your machine.
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  )
}

===== FILE: src/components/progress-sync.tsx =====
'use client'

import { useEffect, useRef } from 'react'
import { useSession } from 'next-auth/react'
import {
  loadProgressState,
  mergeProgressStates,
  saveProgressState,
  switchProgressScope,
  type ProgressState,
} from '@/lib/progress-state'

const SYNC_DEBOUNCE_MS = 1200

/**
 * Keeps localStorage and the signed-in user's database record in sync.
 *
 * - On sign-in: merges guest + account-local + server progress, saves the
 *   union locally, then pushes it to the server (which recomputes XP).
 * - While signed in: every progress change is debounced and pushed.
 * - Signed out: does nothing; progress stays local-only.
 */
export function ProgressSync() {
  const { data: session, status } = useSession()
  const userId = session?.user?.id ?? null
  const syncedFor = useRef<string | null>(null)
  const suppressBroadcast = useRef(false)
  const timer = useRef<ReturnType<typeof setTimeout> | null>(null)
  const latest = useRef<ProgressState | null>(null)

  useEffect(() => {
    if (status === 'loading') return

    if (!userId) {
      if (syncedFor.current !== null) {
        syncedFor.current = null
        switchProgressScope(null)
      }
      return
    }

    if (syncedFor.current === userId) return
    syncedFor.current = userId
    let cancelled = false

    const initialSync = async () => {
      try {
        const response = await fetch('/api/progress', { cache: 'no-store' })
        if (!response.ok || cancelled) return

        const remote = ((await response.json()) as { state: ProgressState }).state
        const accountLocal = loadProgressState(userId)
        const guest = loadProgressState(null)

        // Account-scoped edits win; guest progress only fills gaps.
        const merged = mergeProgressStates(mergeProgressStates(accountLocal, remote), guest)

        suppressBroadcast.current = true
        switchProgressScope(userId)
        saveProgressState(merged, userId)
        suppressBroadcast.current = false
        latest.current = merged

        const saved = await fetch('/api/progress', {
          method: 'PUT',
          headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
          body: JSON.stringify({ state: merged }),
        })
        if (!saved.ok || cancelled) return

        const serverState = ((await saved.json()) as { state: ProgressState }).state
        suppressBroadcast.current = true
        saveProgressState(serverState, userId)
        suppressBroadcast.current = false
        latest.current = serverState
      } catch {
        // Offline or transient failure: keep local state, retry on next change.
      }
    }

    void initialSync()

    const handleUpdate = (event: Event) => {
      if (suppressBroadcast.current) return
      latest.current = (event as CustomEvent<ProgressState>).detail

      if (timer.current) clearTimeout(timer.current)
      timer.current = setTimeout(async () => {
        const state = latest.current
        if (!state) return
        try {
          await fetch('/api/progress', {
            method: 'PUT',
            headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
            body: JSON.stringify({ state }),
          })
        } catch {
          // Network hiccup; the next change will retry.
        }
      }, SYNC_DEBOUNCE_MS)
    }

    window.addEventListener('progress-updated', handleUpdate)
    return () => {
      cancelled = true
      window.removeEventListener('progress-updated', handleUpdate)
      if (timer.current) clearTimeout(timer.current)
    }
  }, [userId, status])

  return null
}

'use client'

import { SessionProvider } from 'next-auth/react'
import { ThemeProvider } from 'next-themes'
import { MotionConfig } from 'motion/react'
import { ReactNode } from 'react'
import { ProgressSync } from '@/components/progress-sync'
import { PwaRegister } from '@/components/pwa-register'

export function Providers({ children }: { children: ReactNode }) {
  return (
    <SessionProvider>
      <MotionConfig reducedMotion="user">
        <ThemeProvider attribute="class" defaultTheme="system" enableSystem>
          <ProgressSync />
          <PwaRegister />
          {children}
        </ThemeProvider>
      </MotionConfig>
    </SessionProvider>
  )
}

===== FILE: src/components/pwa-register.tsx =====
'use client'

import { useEffect } from 'react'

/** Register the app-shell service worker after paint, production only. */
export function PwaRegister() {
  useEffect(() => {
    if (process.env.NODE_ENV !== 'production') return
    if (!('serviceWorker' in navigator)) return

    const register = () => {
      navigator.serviceWorker.register('/sw.js').catch(() => {
        // Offline support is progressive; never block or alarm the learner.
      })
    }

    if (document.readyState === 'complete') {
      register()
    } else {
      window.addEventListener('load', register, { once: true })
      return () => window.removeEventListener('load', register)
    }
  }, [])

  return null
}

'use client'

import { memo, useEffect, useMemo, useState } from 'react'
import { Badge } from '@/components/ui/badge'
import { Button } from '@/components/ui/button'
import { Input } from '@/components/ui/input'
import {
  Play, FileText, BookOpen, Code2, GraduationCap, Book, Wrench, FolderGit2,
  ExternalLink, Search, Clock, X, SlidersHorizontal, CheckCircle2, type LucideIcon,
} from 'lucide-react'
import type { DirectoryResource } from '@/lib/resources-data'

import { cn, getDifficultyColor, getAccent } from '@/lib/utils'

const typeMeta: Record<string, { icon: LucideIcon; label: string; color: string }> = {
  video: { icon: Play, label: 'Video', color: 'rose' },
  article: { icon: FileText, label: 'Article', color: 'blue' },
  docs: { icon: BookOpen, label: 'Docs', color: 'emerald' },
  interactive: { icon: Code2, label: 'Interactive', color: 'violet' },
  course: { icon: GraduationCap, label: 'Course', color: 'amber' },
  book: { icon: Book, label: 'Book', color: 'slate' },
  project: { icon: FolderGit2, label: 'Project', color: 'indigo' },
  tool: { icon: Wrench, label: 'Tool', color: 'cyan' },
}

const PAGE_SIZE = 24

const ResourceCard = memo(function ResourceCard({ resource }: { resource: DirectoryResource }) {
  const meta = typeMeta[resource.type] || typeMeta.article
  const Icon = meta.icon
  const levelColor = getDifficultyColor(resource.level)

  return (
    <a
      href={resource.url}
      target="_blank"
      rel="noopener noreferrer"
      className="group flex flex-col rounded-xl border border-border/50 bg-card p-5 transition-all motion-safe:hover:-translate-y-0.5 hover:border-border hover:shadow-md"
    >
      <div className="flex items-center gap-2 mb-3">
        <div className={cn('flex h-9 w-9 items-center justify-center rounded-lg flex-shrink-0', getAccent(meta.color).icon)}>
          <Icon className="h-4.5 w-4.5" aria-hidden="true" />
        </div>
        <Badge variant="outline" className="text-xs uppercase tracking-wide sm:text-[10px]">
          {meta.label}
        </Badge>
        <Badge
          variant="outline"
          className={cn('text-xs sm:text-[10px]', getAccent(levelColor).borderSoft, getAccent(levelColor).text)}
        >
          {resource.level}
        </Badge>
        <ExternalLink className="h-3.5 w-3.5 ml-auto text-muted-foreground/50 group-hover:text-foreground transition-colors" aria-hidden="true" />
      </div>
      <h2 className="font-semibold group-hover:text-primary transition-colors leading-snug">
        {resource.title}
      </h2>
      <p className="mt-2 text-sm text-muted-foreground line-clamp-3 flex-1">
        {resource.description}
      </p>
      <div className="mt-4 pt-3 border-t border-border/50 flex items-center gap-3 text-xs text-muted-foreground flex-wrap">
        <span className="font-medium">{resource.provider}</span>
        {resource.duration && (
          <span className="flex items-center gap-1">
            <Clock className="h-3 w-3" aria-hidden="true" />
            {resource.duration}
          </span>
        )}
        {resource.isVerified && (
          <span className="flex items-center gap-1 text-emerald-600 dark:text-emerald-400" title="Verified quality">
            <CheckCircle2 className="h-3 w-3" aria-hidden="true" />
            Verified
          </span>
        )}
      </div>
    </a>
  )
})

export function ResourcesClient({ resources }: { resources: DirectoryResource[] }) {
  const [query, setQuery] = useState('')
  const [debouncedQuery, setDebouncedQuery] = useState('')
  const [topic, setTopic] = useState('all')
  const [type, setType] = useState('all')
  const [level, setLevel] = useState('all')
  const [showFilters, setShowFilters] = useState(false)
  const [visibleCount, setVisibleCount] = useState(PAGE_SIZE)

  useEffect(() => {
    const timer = window.setTimeout(() => setDebouncedQuery(query.trim()), 200)
    return () => window.clearTimeout(timer)
  }, [query])

  const filtered = useMemo(() => {
    const q = debouncedQuery.toLowerCase()

    return resources.filter((r) => {
      if (topic !== 'all' && r.topic !== topic) return false
      if (type !== 'all' && r.type !== type) return false
      if (level !== 'all' && r.level !== level) return false
      if (q) {
        const haystack = `${r.title} ${r.description} ${r.provider} ${r.tags.join(' ')}`.toLowerCase()
        if (!haystack.includes(q)) return false
      }
      return true
    })
  }, [resources, debouncedQuery, topic, type, level])

  const visibleResources = filtered.slice(0, visibleCount)
  const remaining = filtered.length - visibleResources.length
  const hasActiveFilters =
    topic !== 'all' || type !== 'all' || level !== 'all' || query.trim() !== ''

  const clearFilters = () => {
    setQuery('')
    setDebouncedQuery('')
    setTopic('all')
    setType('all')
    setLevel('all')
    setVisibleCount(PAGE_SIZE)
  }

  return (
    <div>
      {/* Search bar */}
      <div className="flex flex-col gap-4 mb-6 sm:flex-row">
        <div className="relative flex-1">
          <Search className="absolute left-3 top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 h-4 w-4 text-muted-foreground" aria-hidden="true" />
          <Input
            type="search"
            placeholder="Search resources by title, topic, or provider..."
            value={query}
            onChange={(e) => {
              setQuery(e.target.value)
              setVisibleCount(PAGE_SIZE)
            }}
            className="pl-9"
            aria-label="Search resources"
            aria-controls="resource-grid"
          />
        </div>
        <Button
          variant="outline"
          onClick={() => setShowFilters(!showFilters)}
          className="gap-2 sm:hidden"
        >
          <SlidersHorizontal className="h-4 w-4" aria-hidden="true" />
          Filters
        </Button>
      </div>

      {/* Filters */}
      <div className={cn('space-y-4 mb-8', !showFilters && 'hidden sm:block')}>
        <div>
          <p className="text-xs font-medium text-muted-foreground uppercase tracking-wide mb-2">Topic</p>
          <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-2">
            {resourceTopics.map((t) => (
              <button
                key={t.key}
                onClick={() => {
                  setTopic(t.key)
                  setVisibleCount(PAGE_SIZE)
                }}
                className={cn(
                  'rounded-full border px-3 py-1 text-xs font-medium transition-colors',
                  topic === t.key
                    ? 'border-primary bg-primary text-primary-foreground hover:bg-primary/90'
                    : 'border-border bg-background hover:bg-accent'
                )}
                aria-pressed={topic === t.key}
              >
                {t.label}
              </button>
            ))}
          </div>
        </div>
        <div className="flex flex-col gap-4 sm:flex-row sm:gap-8">
          <div>
            <p className="text-xs font-medium text-muted-foreground uppercase tracking-wide mb-2">Type</p>
            <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-2">
              {resourceTypes.map((t) => (
                <button
                  key={t.key}
                  onClick={() => {
                    setType(t.key)
                    setVisibleCount(PAGE_SIZE)
                  }}
                  className={cn(
                    'rounded-full border px-3 py-1 text-xs font-medium transition-colors',
                    type === t.key
                      ? 'border-primary bg-primary text-primary-foreground hover:bg-primary/90'
                      : 'border-border bg-background hover:bg-accent'
                  )}
                  aria-pressed={type === t.key}
                >
                  {t.label}
                </button>
              ))}
            </div>
          </div>
          <div>
            <p className="text-xs font-medium text-muted-foreground uppercase tracking-wide mb-2">Level</p>
            <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-2">
              {resourceLevels.map((l) => (
                <button
                  key={l.key}
                  onClick={() => {
                    setLevel(l.key)
                    setVisibleCount(PAGE_SIZE)
                  }}
                  className={cn(
                    'rounded-full border px-3 py-1 text-xs font-medium transition-colors',
                    level === l.key
                      ? 'border-primary bg-primary text-primary-foreground hover:bg-primary/90'
                      : 'border-border bg-background hover:bg-accent'
                  )}
                  aria-pressed={level === l.key}
                >
                  {l.label}
                </button>
              ))}
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* Results count */}
      <div className="flex items-center justify-between mb-6">
        <p className="text-sm text-muted-foreground" aria-live="polite">
          Showing <span className="font-medium text-foreground">{visibleResources.length}</span> of{' '}
          <span className="font-medium text-foreground">{filtered.length}</span>{' '}
          {filtered.length === 1 ? 'resource' : 'resources'}
          {hasActiveFilters && (
            <button onClick={clearFilters} className="ml-3 inline-flex items-center gap-1 text-xs underline hover:text-foreground">
              <X className="h-3 w-3" aria-hidden="true" />
              Clear filters
            </button>
          )}
        </p>
      </div>

      {/* Resource grid */}
      {filtered.length === 0 ? (
        <div className="text-center py-16">
          <p className="text-lg font-medium">No resources found</p>
          <p className="mt-2 text-muted-foreground">Try adjusting your search or filters.</p>
          <Button variant="outline" className="mt-6" onClick={clearFilters}>
            Clear all filters
          </Button>
        </div>
      ) : (
        <>
          <div id="resource-grid" className="grid grid-cols-1 gap-4 sm:grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-3">
            {visibleResources.map((resource) => (
              <ResourceCard key={resource.id} resource={resource} />
            ))}
          </div>
          {remaining > 0 && (
            <div className="mt-8 flex justify-center">
              <Button
                variant="outline"
                onClick={() => setVisibleCount((count) => count + PAGE_SIZE)}
                aria-controls="resource-grid"
              >
                Load {Math.min(PAGE_SIZE, remaining)} more{' '}
                {remaining === 1 ? 'resource' : 'resources'}
              </Button>
            </div>
          )}
        </>
      )}
    </div>
  )
}

===== FILE: src/components/settings/settings-client.tsx =====
'use client'

/* eslint-disable react-hooks/set-state-in-effect */

import { useEffect, useRef, useState } from 'react'
import Link from 'next/link'
import { useSession } from 'next-auth/react'
import {
  Award,
  Bell,
  BookOpen,
  Calendar,
  CalendarDays,
  Camera,
  Check,
  CheckCircle2,
  Clock,
  Eye,
  Flame,
  Inbox,
  Loader2,
  LogIn,
  Save,
  ShieldCheck,
  Trash2,
  Trophy,
  UserCog,
  UserPlus,
  Users,
  X,
  Zap,
} from 'lucide-react'
import { Avatar, AvatarFallback, AvatarImage } from '@/components/ui/avatar'
import { Badge } from '@/components/ui/badge'
import { Button } from '@/components/ui/button'
import { Card, CardContent, CardDescription, CardHeader, CardTitle } from '@/components/ui/card'
import { ConfirmDialog } from '@/components/ui/confirm-dialog'
import { Input } from '@/components/ui/input'
import { Label } from '@/components/ui/label'
import { Separator } from '@/components/ui/separator'
import { Tabs, TabsContent, TabsList, TabsTrigger } from '@/components/ui/tabs'
